## Design System Rules (standing instructions — apply to every future build, marketing site and product UI alike)

Established 2026-07-26 from direct feedback on the first landing page draft. These are not one-off fixes — apply them by default to every card, section header, and tagline built from here on, across the marketing site and the eventual product UI, unless a future instruction explicitly overrides them.

- [ ] **Cards (feature/capability/module/pricing cards, etc.):** icon and title are always **center-aligned**; the supporting body text underneath is always **justified** (`text-align: justify`), never left-aligned.
- [ ] **Section headers and taglines** (eyebrow label + heading + intro paragraph): same rule — the eyebrow/heading is **center-aligned**, the accompanying paragraph/tagline text is **justified**.
- [ ] **Grid layouts** should always resolve to complete rows/columns — pick item counts and column counts that divide evenly at each breakpoint (e.g. 9 items → 3×3, 12 items → 4×3 desktop / 2×6 tablet), rather than leaving a dangling partial last row. Add or trim content to make the grid land clean, don't just let `auto-fit` produce an orphan.
- [ ] Keep section vertical spacing tight and deliberate — avoid excess empty space between sections; this was flagged directly as a problem on the first draft.
- [ ] **Nothing hardcoded — ever** (user directive 2026-07-08). Any value a school or the Platform Super Admin could plausibly want to change (pricing, plan features, grading formulas, permissions, module lists, contact info, copy, term counts, thresholds) lives in config/database/admin settings, never as literals in code. This applies to every layer: backend, frontend, marketing site.
- [ ] **No dashes in on-screen text** (user directive 2026-07-08). Hyphens and em/en dashes must not appear as punctuation in any user-facing copy (marketing pages, UI labels, validation and error messages, notifications, report card text). Rephrase with commas, colons, or separate sentences. Existing marketing-site copy predates this rule and needs a sweep when next touched.

## Database & Storage Efficiency Rules (standing instructions, from the user's "Dbase recommendation.docx", read into this tracker 2026-07-11: "take note of my Dbase recommendation for all your previous, current and future builds as i need it")

The MVP launches on shared cPanel hosting with MySQL, not VPS or cloud infrastructure, so every build decision from here on should account for that constraint, while staying structured so a later move to VPS then cloud does not require a rewrite (Phase 1 cPanel and Database Driver queues and local storage and optional Redis, Phase 2 VPS with Redis/Supervisor/queue workers/object storage, Phase 3 cloud with load balancing/auto scaling/CDN — the codebase should not hardcode assumptions that block that path). Guiding question before adding any feature or module: how much storage will it consume, how many database queries will it generate, can it be archived/compressed/regenerated instead of stored, and will it still perform well on a modest shared hosting environment.

- [x] **One dedicated MySQL database per school, plus one shared platform database** — already the confirmed hybrid multi-tenancy architecture (see Confirmed technology stack in CLAUDE.md), matches this recommendation exactly, nothing to change here.
- [ ] **Never store files in the database.** Only ever store a path/reference column (e.g. `uploads/student/2026/passport.webp`), never the file bytes themselves. No feature built so far stores binary file content in a database column; keep it that way for every future upload feature (student/staff photos, documents, receipts).
- [ ] **Compress and convert every uploaded image to WebP** on upload (resize + compress), not stored as the original upload. Not built yet, there is no image upload feature in the product at all yet (no student/staff photo upload exists). Build this into whichever feature adds the first image upload, do not add a raw-upload path now and compress later.
- [ ] **Never store generated PDFs (report cards, certificates, receipts, invoices) forever.** Generate on demand and let the user download, or generate and delete after a configurable retention period, don't keep every generated PDF indefinitely. Report cards currently render live (`ResultService`/report card Blade views), not as stored PDFs, which already matches this; keep that pattern for any future PDF-generating feature (receipts, certificates) rather than switching to "generate once, store forever."
- [ ] **Per file type upload size limits** (e.g. passport photo max ~300KB, certificates max ~2MB, medical reports max ~3MB, video uploads not allowed at launch). Not built yet since no upload feature exists yet; bake these limits into `config('skulos.php')` (admin adjustable, not hardcoded) when the first upload feature is built, not added retroactively.
- [x] **Retention/cleanup policies for anything that grows unbounded** — BUILT 2026-07-12: `App\Console\Commands\RetentionCleanup` (`skulos:retention-cleanup`, weekly, Monday 02:00) purges read notifications and communication logs past a configurable window (`App\Services\Maintenance\RetentionService`, platform default + optional per-school override, same precedence pattern as idle timeout/mail settings, admin screens at Admin > Settings > Retention and School > Settings > Retention) and expired platform login sessions (platform-wide only, a login session is not scoped to one school). **Audit logs are deliberately excluded**: `App\Models\AuditLog` is immutable by design (throws on update/delete, "nothing deleted without a trace"), so audit trail retention needs an archive-not-delete strategy, not a purge job — flagged as a real open design decision, not silently skipped. Login history/SMS logs/temporary import/export files/orphaned uploads are not yet real (no login-attempt tracking, no SMS provider, no upload feature exists at all), nothing to clean up yet for those.
- [x] **Archive old academic sessions** — BUILT 2026-07-12: `academic_sessions.is_archived` (tenant migration), `AcademicSession::archive()`/`unarchive()` (refuses to archive the current session), `scopeActive()` excludes archived from the default Sessions & Terms list, an "Archived sessions" section on that page to restore one.
- [x] **Weekly scheduled MySQL maintenance** — BUILT 2026-07-12: `App\Console\Commands\OptimizeTenantDatabases` (`skulos:optimize-tables`, weekly, Monday 03:00, an hour after retention cleanup so they never overlap), runs `OPTIMIZE TABLE` per table on MySQL; on SQLite (local dev) runs `VACUUM` instead since `OPTIMIZE TABLE` is MySQL-only syntax, so local dev stays a harmless no-op rather than erroring every run.
- [x] **A per-school Maintenance Center** — BUILT 2026-07-12 as part of `school.maintenance.index` ("School Health & Storage"): "Optimize database now" and "Run retention cleanup now" (both real, sharing `MaintenanceActionsService` with the weekly scheduled commands), linked to Backups. Clear cache/delete temp files/compress images/remove duplicate files/rebuild search index are not built, none of those have anything to act on yet (no caching layer, no temp-file feature, no image upload, no search index wired up).
- [x] **A per-school Storage Dashboard** — BUILT 2026-07-12, same screen: real database size (file size on SQLite, `information_schema` on MySQL), row counts grouped by category, real backup count/size, and a usage bar against `plans.storage_limit_mb` (new, nullable, admin set per plan in Billing, never guessed).
- [ ] **Smart/deduplicated uploads**: if the same file is uploaded twice (by hash), reference the existing stored file instead of storing a second copy. Not built, applies once any upload feature exists.
- [ ] **Cache rarely-changing per-school reference data** (school info, classes, sections, subjects, houses, permissions) instead of re-querying every request. Partially true today only in the sense that these are small, cheap queries at current scale; no explicit caching layer has been added yet, revisit once a school's data volume makes it worth the added complexity of cache invalidation.
- [ ] **Modular installation, not modular hiding**: a module a school has not enabled (Hostel, Library, Payroll, Medical, Inventory, Transport, per the RBAC module-toggle system that already exists for Billing/`Plan.modules`) should mean no menus, no populated tables, no scheduled tasks, no background processing for that module, not just a hidden nav link. Worth auditing once any of those deferred modules (see section 5b) gets built, so an unused module stays genuinely weightless for a school that never turns it on.
- [x] **"SKULOS Health Engine"** — BUILT 2026-07-12, same screen: `HealthService` computes a live score and real findings (no current term, unassigned students, no staff logins, missing/stale backup), each with an action link. Computed live on page load, not a persisted nightly snapshot (revisit if trend history is wanted later); slow query/missing index/duplicate-file/orphaned-upload detection not built, no profiler or upload feature exists to scan.
- [ ] **Query discipline**: select only needed columns (avoid `SELECT *`/unscoped `::all()`/unscoped `::get()` equivalents in Eloquent), paginate every list screen (students, results, attendance, parents, staff, fees, logs) rather than loading full sets. Mixed compliance today: Students/Houses assignment lists already paginate (`Student::paginate(30)`), but this has not been swept across every list screen as a hard rule; apply it by default to every new list screen and audit existing ones opportunistically.

## Confirmed Business Decisions (post-launch-doc updates)

- [x] **Pricing tiers renamed (confirmed 2026-07-10)**, sourced from `SKULOS PRICING PLAN AND FEATURES.docx`: **Starter → Foundation**, **Growth → Advance**, Professional and Enterprise keep their names. Same 4 tiers, same order, same prices, only the bottom two names and the entire feature catalog per tier changed. See section 14 for the full catalog, build status audit, and launch scope. Old names (Starter/Growth) are retired; use Foundation/Advance everywhere from this point.
- [x] **Pricing (confirmed 2026-07-26, tier names updated 2026-07-10)**, per student per term:
  - Foundation (was Starter): ₦500 / child / term
  - Advance (was Growth): ₦800 / child / term
  - Professional: ₦1,200 / child / term
  - Enterprise: ₦1,500 / child / term
  - Figures and the feature list shown under each plan must be **admin-editable**, not hardcoded — see the Marketing Site / Admin CMS section below. The admin settings page must expose a checkbox per feature per plan so the Platform Super Admin controls what each tier displays without a code change.
- [x] **Launch scope (confirmed 2026-07-10):** SKULOS goes live with **Foundation and Advance only**. Every feature listed under those two tiers in section 14 must be genuinely built and working before launch, not just cataloged. Professional and Enterprise stay visible on the public pricing page with their full feature lists marked **Coming soon** (for credibility/upsell) but are not purchasable or provisioned until built.
- [x] **Unique identifiers (confirmed 2026-07-08):**
  - **Student** unique ID is the **Admission Number** (school-assigned, not auto-incrementing DB id) — this is the field used to look up/reference a student throughout the system, including bulk import matching (e.g. the "Row 15 — Admission Number already exists" validation example in section 4.3).
  - **Student email address is optional** — students must not be blocked from admission/record-creation for lacking one. Design student login/notifications so email is an enhancement (e.g. enables direct student portal login if present), not a requirement.
  - **Parent and staff** unique ID is **school configurable (revised 2026-07-11)**: email address or phone number, chosen per school per person type via tenant Settings `parent_identifier`/`staff_identifier` (default email, matching the original decision). See the Build Log entry "Configurable parent and staff identifier" below and CLAUDE.md's Confirmed business/data-model decisions for the full detail. `users.phone` now carries the same platform-wide unique constraint `users.email` already had either way.

## Working conventions reminder (read CLAUDE.md for the full list)

Standing directives that apply to **every current and future change** (backend, frontend, marketing site, docs):
- **Always commit AND push** after each completed item — never leave work only committed locally (user directive 2026-07-08).
- **Nothing hardcoded** — configurable values live in config/DB/admin settings.
- **No dashes in on-screen text** — hyphens and em/en dashes out of all user-facing copy.
- **Security in everything** — deny-by-default, tenant isolation, explicit `$fillable`, no leaking secrets to logs/serialization.
- **CSV** for all bulk templates + import files; every importer ships a downloadable template.
- Commit as the user (`emiiyaseyi`), no Claude co-author trailer.

## Architecture decision (2026-07-09): app UI is Laravel-rendered for now

To make the platform clickable from **one entry point** fast, the product UI is built as **Laravel server-rendered Blade pages** (reusing the design system), not the separately-planned Next.js SPA + REST API. Rationale: one deployable, cPanel-friendly, reuses the tenancy/RBAC/audit/services already built, and reaches a working unified platform far quicker than standing up a separate SPA + token auth + CORS + two deployments. **API-first is preserved** — the services (TenantManager, Rbac, ResultService, NotificationService, importers, etc.) are UI-agnostic, so a Next.js frontend consuming a REST/JSON API can be layered on later without rework. If/when that happens, the Blade UI can remain for admin or be retired. Recorded here so a future agent doesn't think the Next.js frontend was skipped by mistake.

## Fixes + additions 2026-07-09 (session/login/sidebar/lifecycle)
- [x] **419 Page Expired on school login FIXED (root cause).** `SESSION_DOMAIN=.localhost` was being rejected by browsers (leading-dot `.localhost` is not a valid cookie domain), so no session cookie was stored and every POST failed CSRF, even in incognito. Set `SESSION_DOMAIN=null` (host-only cookies) for local; documented that production with real subdomains can use `.yourdomain.tld`. Verified the full login POST cycle now returns 302 to the dashboard, not 419. Trade-off: sessions are per-subdomain locally (no cross-subdomain SSO), which is fine and safer; the EnsureSchoolMember guard still applies.
- [x] Login page now fills the **full screen** (added `min-height:100vh` to the split layout) and "Powered by SKULOS" is bigger using the marketing wordmark (SK**U**LOS with gold U).
- [x] **Collapsible sidebar**: a collapse button on the appbar toggles the sidebar to an icon-only rail (labels/groups hidden), persisted in localStorage. Desktop only; mobile keeps the slide-in drawer.
- [x] **School lifecycle actions** (super admin, on the schools table): **Suspend** (temporary block), **Archive** (long-term block), **Resume** (back to active), **Delete** (soft delete, data retained/recoverable). Access is blocked for suspended/archived (403) and soft-deleted schools 404 on their subdomain. 2 tests (full lifecycle + super-admin-only guard).

`### <Module/Item name>`
`- Status: Not started / In progress / Blocked / Done`

### Dev environment provisioning
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-08
- Notes: PHP 8.3.32 + Composer 2.10.x provisioned user-scope via winget/phar (details + exact paths in CLAUDE.md "Local environment reality"). SQLite stands in for MySQL locally; no Redis (use database/sync queue drivers locally).
- Issues/Bugs: (2026-07-08) winget package ships without php.ini — fresh install has no extensions loaded and extension_dir defaults to nonexistent `C:\php\ext`. Also, bare `php` on PATH resolves to a stale PHP 8.1 install.
- Resolution: hand-wrote php.ini inside the 8.3 package folder (extension_dir absolute-pathed, required extensions enabled); created `php.bat`/`composer.bat` shims in `C:\Users\HP\AppData\Local\composer-bin\`. Never invoke bare `php`.

### SSO: Google + Microsoft/Outlook (TD-005; user-requested 2026-07-08)
- Status: Done (plumbing + policy + tests; live OAuth round-trip needs real credentials, not testable in this env)
- Started: 2026-07-08
- Notes: Laravel Socialite + socialiteproviders/microsoft. Each provider is **enable-able and off by default** (`config/skulos.php` sso.*.enabled via env `SSO_GOOGLE_ENABLED`/`SSO_MICROSOFT_ENABLED`); a provider is only "available" when enabled AND credentialed (`config/services.php` from env). `SsoManager` resolves/links accounts by verified **email** (the platform identity key, consistent with parent-unique-ID = email), so Google and Outlook with the same email map to one account; new SSO users get no usable password and are never super admin; refuses when the provider returns no email. `SsoController` redirect/callback; disabled/unknown providers 404 (existence not advertised). Microsoft provider registered via `SocialiteWasCalled` in AppServiceProvider. Logins audited (`sso.user_provisioned`, `sso.login`). `.env.example` documents all keys.
- Nothing hardcoded: enable flags + credentials + button labels all in config/env; DB-backed admin toggle comes with the admin CMS.
- Issues/Bugs: live OAuth not exercisable here (no real Google/Microsoft app credentials).
- Resolution: 5 tests via mocked Socialite cover the enable gate, 404s, new-user provisioning + login, email account-linking, and audit. Full suite 53 tests / 151 assertions passing.

- Started: 2026-07-08

Status values come from `config/skulos.php` attendance_statuses (configurable, not a hardcoded enum). `AttendanceService`: `markClass` (idempotent whole-class register, correct-in-place), `markStaff`, `classSummary` (counts by status). Bulk class marking audited.
- Issues/Bugs: (2026-07-08) `date` cast serialized to "YYYY-MM-DD 00:00:00", so the idempotent `updateOrCreate` match on "YYYY-MM-DD" never matched and re-inserted, hitting the unique constraint. Also a summary assertion was order-sensitive.
- Resolution: store `date` as a plain date string (dropped the datetime cast; documented why in the model) so the match is exact; made the summary assertion canonicalizing. 5 attendance tests; full suite 58 tests / 161 assertions.

A test rebuilds a totally different CA format to prove nothing is baked in.

### Platform application shell (unified entry point) — user request 2026-07-09
- Status: In progress (auth + super-admin school creation + branded tenant login/dashboard + branding settings done; dashboard templates + more module screens next)
- Started: 2026-07-09
- Notes: one Laravel app, two route surfaces. **Platform (main domain, `config('app.domain')`)**: `/login` (throttled, session auth), `/admin` super-admin area (create school → provisions DB + seeds calendar + attaches creator; lists schools with their subdomain + Open link). Super-admin area gated by `EnsureSuperAdmin` (404 to non-admins). **School portal (`{slug}.<domain>` subdomain, tenant middleware)**: branded welcome page, branded login (verifies the user is a member of that school before authenticating), dashboard showing the user's notification feed (`Notification::feedFor`) + quick stats + upcoming calendar, and **school branding settings** (logo upload, brand colour, welcome headline/message) with a live preview, reflected on the welcome/login page. `platform:make-super-admin` console command bootstraps the first admin. Shared Blade layout `layouts/app` carries the design system (light/dark, gold accent, per-school accent override), no dashes in UI copy. Subdomain resolution added to `ResolveTenant` (route param first, so `{slug}.localhost` works in local dev without hosts edits).
- Security: super-admin area 404s for non-admins; school login rejects non-members before login; login throttled by email+IP; `is_super_admin`/`school_id` still non-mass-assignable; CSRF on all forms; logout regenerates session.

Login-then-logout for non-members left them authenticated → refactored to verify password+membership BEFORE `Auth::login`. Blade `@section('bare')` block flag caused output-buffer "risky" warnings → switched to inline `@section('bare', true)`. Default ExampleTest expected `/`=200 (now redirects) → updated.
- Resolution: 8 platform-flow feature tests (super-admin command, guest redirect, admin gate 404, create+provision school, branded welcome renders branding, member sign-in → dashboard, non-member rejected, branding update reflects). Full suite **84 tests / 250 assertions**. Login page verified rendering via headless screenshot.

### Multi-tenancy foundation (section 4.1)
- Status: Done (foundation scope)
- Started: 2026-07-08
- Notes: hand-rolled lean tenancy (no stancl/tenancy dependency — keeps cPanel path simple and the mechanism fully ours). Platform DB holds `schools`, `users` (+phone, +is_super_admin), `roles` (shell; RBAC fills in next), `school_user` pivot. Per-school DB: `TenantManager` provisions (SQLite file locally / `CREATE DATABASE` on MySQL), runs `database/migrations/tenant/*` against a runtime-repointed `tenant` connection. `ResolveTenant` middleware (alias `tenant`) resolves school by `X-School` header or subdomain, rejects unknown/unprovisioned (404) and suspended (403). `school:provision` artisan command works end-to-end (demo school provisioned in dev). First tenant table: `settings` key/value (backs TD-007 configurability).
- Issues/Bugs: none beyond the phpunit.xml one logged above.
- Resolution: 8 feature tests passing: provisioning creates dedicated DB w/ tenant schema, two schools fully isolated, idempotent provisioning, header resolution, unknown/unprovisioned/suspended rejection.

### RBAC engine + module toggles (section 4.2)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-08
- Notes: platform-DB tables `permissions` (catalog, seeded from `config/skulos.php` — 26 modules × standard actions + extras = 160 codes), `permission_role`, `user_permissions` (per-user allow/deny overrides), `modules`, `module_states` (Enable/Disable/Hidden/Read-only at school/role/user scope). 21 built-in role templates (school_id null) seeded; schools customize via `Rbac::cloneTemplateRole()` so templates stay shared and untouched. Resolution rules: permission = super admin > user deny > user allow > role grant > **deny by default**; module state = user > role > school > catalog default (core modules default enabled, optional + deferred default disabled). Deferred modules (admissions/finance/visitors/assets, section 5b) are cataloged so toggles stay stable, but nothing is built for them.
- Security posture baked in: deny-by-default everywhere; unknown permission codes and unknown modules resolve to "no"; suspended memberships denied; `is_super_admin` and `school_id` not mass-assignable (console/forceFill only); permission catalog not user-editable (schools grant/revoke, never invent codes).
- Issues/Bugs: (2026-07-08) `db:seed` on dev DB failed first run — new migration hadn't been applied to dev database (tests run on in-memory DB, so they didn't catch a stale dev DB).
- Resolution: ran `migrate` then reseeded — dev DB now has 26 modules / 160 permissions / 21 template roles. 10 RBAC feature tests + full suite (18 tests) passing.

### Audit trail engine (section 4.3)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-08
- Notes: identical `audit_logs` tables in the platform DB **and** every tenant DB — a school's audit history lives inside its own database, so tenant isolation holds for logs too. `Auditable` trait (created/updated/deleted/force_deleted/restored, old/new value diffs, changed-keys-only on update) + `Recorder` service (captures user_id, IP, user-agent, URL; marks console actions; also takes custom action names like "results.published" for non-model events). Wired into `School`, `User`, and the tenant `Setting` model; every future model just adds the trait. No cross-DB FK on user_id (impossible tenant→platform) — deliberate.
- Security: hidden attributes (password, remember_token, is_super_admin) never reach logs; per-model `auditExclude` for extra masking; logs are **immutable** — model throws on update and delete attempts.
- Issues/Bugs: (2026-07-08) first draft registered the `restored` listener per-instance (would have duplicated log rows); caught in review before commit.
- Resolution: moved to `bootAuditable` with a `SoftDeletes` trait check — fires once per class. 8 audit tests passing; full suite 26 passing.

### Bulk operations framework + import validation (section 4.3)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-08
- Notes: `app/Services/Bulk/` — `CsvReader` (dependency-free CSV parsing: BOM, blank lines, Excel trailing columns), abstract `Importer` (per-row Laravel validation, **strict** mode = all-or-nothing in one transaction vs **partial** mode = import valid rows and report the rest, in-file duplicate detection via `uniqueBy()` e.g. the same admission number twice in one upload, CSV template generation from `columns()`), `ImportResult` (row numbers reported Excel-style: header is row 1, first data row is 2 — matches the tracker's "Row 15: Admission Number already exists" requirement), `BulkActionRunner` (applies an action across many ids, collecting per-id success/failure instead of dying on the first error). Every import and bulk action writes an audit record into the tenant DB automatically. First real subclass will be the Students importer in the core-modules phase.
- Issues/Bugs: (2026-07-08) syntax typo in first Importer draft (`$index, => $row`); caught immediately.
- Resolution: fixed. 7 bulk framework tests passing; full suite 33 tests / 81 assertions passing.
- Confirmed 2026-07-08 (user): **CSV** is the chosen format for both templates and import files (not Excel/xlsx). Base `Importer::template()` already emits CSV. **Every** importer must expose a downloadable template so users can fill and re-upload — this is a hard requirement per importer, wire a download endpoint/button for each in the core-modules phase.

### Clickable platform UI (architecture pivot, 2026-07-08/09)
- Status: Done (foundation) / ongoing as more module screens are added
- Notes: pivoted from "separate Next.js SPA + REST API" to **Laravel server-rendered Blade** for the whole product UI (platform admin + branded school portals), so the platform is clickable end to end from one entry point without standing up a second app. Services stay UI-agnostic so a Next.js frontend can still be layered on later; nothing about this pivot removes API-first design from the services layer.
- Built: sidebar dashboard shell (collapsible desktop rail + mobile drawer) for both the platform admin area and school portals; school creation now collects a dedicated school-admin login (name/email/password) at creation time instead of only attaching the platform super admin; 4 selectable/brandable login page designs (`centered`/`split`/`cover`/`showcase`) plus 4 selectable dashboard look templates (`classic`/`vibrant`/`erp`/`modern`, re-skinned to match user-supplied reference images) with per-widget show/hide toggles, all admin-editable and stored per school (nothing hardcoded); school lifecycle actions (suspend/archive/resume/delete) with access blocked accordingly; `EnsureSchoolMember` guard so a signed-in member of School A cannot view School B by URL.
- Issues/Bugs: (2026-07-08) `SESSION_DOMAIN=.localhost` (leading dot) is rejected as an invalid cookie domain for the bare host `localhost`, so no session cookie was ever set and every POST 419'd, including in incognito. (2026-07-08) CSS grid bug: `.scrim` mobile overlay had no base `display:none`, so on desktop it occupied a grid cell and pushed `.main` out of view (blank page despite correct HTML). (2026-07-08) admin dashboard `@foreach($schools as $school)` leaked the last-looped `$school` into the shared layout's sidebar via Blade view scope.
- Resolution: `SESSION_DOMAIN=null` for local dev (host-only cookies); documented that a real leading-dot domain is valid in production with real subdomains. Added `.scrim{display:none}` as a base rule. Renamed the admin dashboard loop variable to `$s`.

Search matches admission number, first name, or last name. ### Role based dashboards, configurable subject offerings, campuses, report card templates (2026-07-09)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-09
- User request (verbatim intent): dashboards should differ by who is looking (owner/admin/principal want total classes, campuses, a performance overview; teachers want student counts and a gender chart; not all teachers are class teachers, so class teachers, subject teachers, and class assistants see different things; parents/students get their own view); SS1-3 style classes need subjects marked mandatory/optional and examinable/not; the school's own "List of subjects and Grading system.docx" (pushed to the repo root) is the source for default subjects per section and grading scales, loadable per school and editable after; several report card template designs (from `Templates/Report Card templates/`) should be selectable, with every field on the card (name, admission number, gender, age/DOB, class, house, class teacher, CA columns, total, grade, remarks, etc.) admin togglable, and a later term's report should bring in earlier terms' totals as extra columns plus a cumulative average.
- Built:
  - **Role based dashboard** — `App\Services\Dashboard\RoleDashboard` resolves the signed in user's role in the school and returns role specific figures + a panel: `admin` (students/staff/classes/campuses counts + a school wide performance overview), `class_teacher`/`class_assistant` (their own class only, found via `class_arms.class_teacher_staff_id`, with a boy/girl donut and class average; class assistant gets the identical read only view), `subject_teacher` (only the subjects/classes they actually teach, via `teaching_assignments`, not the whole school), `parent` (linked children via `student_guardians.guardian_user_id`), `student` (their own class/subjects/average/position via `students.user_id`), and a `general` fallback for other staff roles unchanged from before. Added a `class-assistant` role template to `config('skulos.roles')`. Wired into `SchoolPortalController::dashboard()` and rendered by `resources/views/school/widgets/role-panel.blade.php`, reusing the existing 4 dashboard-layout skins (the figures shown differ per role, the visual treatment still follows the school's chosen layout).
  - **Configurable subject offerings** — `class_subject` pivot gained `is_mandatory`/`is_examinable` booleans (default true, so existing/younger sections need no changes). New `AcademicsController` + `resources/views/school/academics/*` let an admin create sections/classes and, per class, add/remove subjects and toggle both flags. `App\Services\Academics\AcademicDefaults` suggests a default subject catalog per section by keyword matching the section's name (nursery/primary/jss/ss) and applies it with one click, sourced from `config('skulos.default_subjects')` (transcribed from the school's uploaded docx, both core Nigerian curriculum subjects and section specific mandatory/elective splits for JSS and SS). Nothing is seeded automatically on tenant provisioning (deliberate: several existing tests create their own "Mathematics" subject and would collide with an auto-seeded one) — loading a default list is always an explicit admin action, matching the "admin can decide to change them later" instruction.
  - **Default grading scales** — `config('skulos.default_grading_scales')` (nursery/primary, junior secondary 5 point A/B/C/P/F, senior secondary 9 point WAEC style A1 to F9), applied on demand via `AcademicDefaults::applyGradingScale()` (idempotent by scale name). Not auto-seeded for the same collision-avoidance reason as subjects.
  - **Campuses** — new tenant `campuses` table/model + `CampusController` (simple add/remove, one "main campus" at a time), feeding the admin dashboard's Campuses figure.
  - **Report cards** — `config('skulos.report_card_templates')` (`classic`/`modern`/`formal`/`compact`, modeled on the 9 sample images the user added to `Templates/Report Card templates/`) and `config('skulos.report_card_fields')` (every field named in the request, grouped identity/academic/remarks, each independently togglable) with `ReportCardPreferences` (tenant-setting backed, mirrors `DashboardPreferences`) and a settings screen with a template picker + field checkboxes. `ReportCardService::forStudent()` computes a real report from `scores`/`grading_scales` for one student/term: for every subject it walks every earlier term in the same academic session (by `sequence`, so this works for 2, 3, or any number of terms, not hardcoded to "third") and shows each as its own column plus a cumulative average across all terms shown — this is the generalised form of "third term brings in first and second term scores as a column, with an average." `ResultService` gained `subjectStats()` (per subject position/class average/highest/lowest within a class arm and term) to back the "modern" template's extra columns. New `student_term_remarks` table holds the class teacher/head of school remarks and the next term resumption date shown on the card. A `sample()` method on `ReportCardService` powers a live preview (`/report-cards/preview/{template}`) with realistic illustrative data so an admin can compare all 4 templates before any real scores exist; the real per student card renders at `/report-cards/{student}/{term}`.
- Issues/Bugs:
  - (2026-07-09) An em dash HTML entity (`&mdash;`) was caught in a first draft of the subjects screen heading before it was ever rendered, swept out immediately per the standing "no dashes on screen" rule; also fixed a raw hyphen fallback character in the report card cumulative column ("N/A" used instead).
  - (2026-07-09) **Route parameters silently swap under domain based tenancy.** Any controller action on a `Route::domain('{school}.'.$domain)` route that also has its own path parameters (e.g. `/campuses/{campusId}`) and declares those as typed method parameters (`function destroy(Request $request, string $campusId)`) gets the WRONG value: Laravel resolves untyped/scalar controller parameters **positionally** against the full list of route parameters including the domain one, not by name, so `$campusId` silently received the school's slug instead of the campus id. 404s resulted (`findOrFail()` on a non-numeric slug) with no visible exception (Laravel renders `ModelNotFoundException` as a clean 404 page). Confirmed by logging the actual bound value inside the controller. The one pre-existing route with this shape (`markNotificationRead(Request $request, string $school, int $id)`) happens to work only because it coincidentally declares `$school` first, matching the true positional order.
  - (2026-07-09) A route-bound `int $id` typed parameter throws a `TypeError` ("must be of type int, string given") under Laravel's reflection based controller dispatch even though the app has no `declare(strict_types=1)` anywhere — reflection invocation does not apply PHP's normal weak-type coercion.
  - (2026-07-09) `Student::fullName()`/`Staff::fullName()` produced a double space when `other_names` was blank (`trim("{$first} {$other} {$last}")` only trims the ends, not a doubled internal space) — surfaced by a report card test asserting an exact name string. Visible on every screen that renders a person's name whenever `other_names` is empty, which is the common case.
  - Resolution: swept the whole file for dash characters before commit. Standardised on reading tenant scoped route ids via `$request->route('paramName')` instead of typed method parameters in every new controller (`AcademicsController`, `CampusController`, `ReportCardController`), and documented the reason in each class docblock so it isn't reintroduced; left the one pre-existing occurrence alone since it isn't actually broken. Changed the `int`-typed route params to `string` where still present. Rewrote both `fullName()` methods to `implode(' ', array_filter([...]))`, which skips blank parts instead of trimming around them.
- Also fixed: `exists:sections,id` / `exists:subjects,id` validation rules were checking the platform's default database connection (which has no `sections`/`subjects` tables — those are tenant only) instead of the tenant connection; changed to `exists:tenant.sections,id` / `exists:tenant.subjects,id`.
- Pushed `Templates/` (previously gitignored, local only) to the repo, along with the user-uploaded "List of subjects and Grading system.docx", so this source material is available from any session/machine.
- QA: 17 new feature tests (`AcademicsScreenTest`, `CampusTest`, `ReportCardTest`, `RoleDashboardTest`) plus manual verification of every new screen against a real local school (rendered HTML fetched and inspected for correct data, 0 dashes, no errors) — full suite **123 tests / 389 assertions passing**.
- Not built in this pass (kept in scope for later, not fabricated): a dedicated Results/Scores entry screen (report cards currently compute from whatever `scores` rows exist; there is still no UI to enter them), affective/psychomotor skill ratings (not in the user's named field list, so not invented), a guided setup wizard tying academic structure + defaults + report card choice together at school creation time.

### Bug fixes, login/error page redesign, and a large batch of platform admin operational tooling (2026-07-09)
- Status: Done (each item individually tested; Billing, Results/Scores entry, and the guided setup wizard remain open — see tracker items below)
- Started: 2026-07-09
- **Root-caused two reported bugs** ("can't log into a new school", "logout shows Page Expired") to the same cause: a stale/expired CSRF token left in an already open tab, not an authentication bug — verified by reproducing the real login/logout flow via curl end to end, which worked correctly with a fresh token. Fixed the *experience* of hitting this regardless of cause: `bootstrap/app.php` now renders a graceful redirect-back-with-message for a 419 instead of Laravel's raw error page (matches on `HttpException` status code 419, since Laravel maps `TokenMismatchException` to a plain `HttpException` *before* custom render callbacks run — a `TokenMismatchException`-typed callback never fires).
- **Merged the school welcome page into login.** A separate low content "click Sign In" page in between was confusing; `/` now redirects a guest straight to `/login` and a signed in member straight to `/dashboard`.
- **Login page redesign** per direct feedback: logo and tagline centered and bigger (never left aligned) on every layout; the branded panel (split/showcase/cover) floats small feature icons (students, academics, attendance, results, calendar, communication) around the branding; admin can choose which side the sign in box appears on (`schools.login_box_side`, right by default) for the two panel layouts; the platform's real support email/phone (`config('skulos.support')`, sourced from the marketing site's own Privacy/Terms pages) now shows below "Powered by SKULOS" on every login layout.
- **Idle session security timeout**: any authenticated page warns after `config('skulos.session_timeout.idle_seconds')` (default 30) of no activity with a live countdown of `warning_seconds` (default 15) to automatic sign out, Resume/Log out choice, a `/session/keep-alive` endpoint backing Resume. Durations are config/env driven, never hardcoded in the JS.
- **Branded error pages**: 403/404/419/429/500/503 previously fell back to Laravel's raw default (a suspended school's 403 looked like a broken site); replaced with a small `resources/views/errors/*` template matching the product's look.
- **Vercel 404 recurrence**: switched `vercel.json` from a wildcard rewrite (fragile with `cleanUrls`) to `"outputDirectory": "site"` (Vercel's built-in "serve this subfolder as root" mechanism); documented the dashboard Root Directory setting as the guaranteed fallback in `DEPLOYMENT.md` since this had already recurred once.
- **Platform tools to manage a school's own administrators**: from a school's detail page, the platform owner can reset a school admin's password (now also emailed to the account owner via `PasswordResetByAdminMail`, not only shown to the platform admin), add another administrator, or revoke/restore one administrator's access independent of the school's own status.
- **Support access, deliberately not a backdoor** (explicit design question: "how to support a school with any issue, do we create a backdoor or not"). Built audited impersonation instead: the platform admin can "View as this admin" and is signed in AS that user with their own permissions (no privilege elevation), every start/end is written to the audit trail with who/when, and a persistent warning banner shows the whole time so it can never be silently forgotten. Chosen over sharing/resetting passwords because it is fully attributable, reversible at any time, and never exposes or changes the account owner's real credentials.
- **Archive page** for deleted (soft-deleted) schools with a Restore button — previously a deleted school just disappeared with no visible way back.
- **Clone a school**: copies sections/classes/arms/subjects (with mandatory/examinable flags)/grading scales/CA components/houses/look-and-feel settings onto a brand new school with its own new administrator. Student/staff/parent data is deliberately never copied — a structure starting point, not a data duplicate.
- **Split the platform Dashboard from Schools** (previously the same page under two nav labels). Dashboard (`PlatformDashboardController`) is now real analytics: schools by status donut, total administrators, recently created schools, recent audit trail activity, a pending-trial-requests alert banner. Schools (`SchoolAdminController::schools()`, renamed from `dashboard()`) stays the create/manage/lifecycle list; added a "Manage" link per row (previously "Open" only linked to the public portal, so the admin-management screen built earlier had no way to reach it).
- **Backup and restore per school**: the school admin or the platform admin can back up on demand, download any past backup, set a default schedule (daily/weekly/monthly + time of day, checked hourly via `Schedule::command('backups:run-scheduled')`), and restore from an earlier backup. `BackupService` branches by tenant driver (SQLite: file copy; MySQL: `mysqldump`/`mysql` via `Illuminate\Support\Facades\Process`); backup metadata lives in the platform database so history stays reachable even if a tenant database has a problem. A real backup-then-restore round trip is covered by a test that proves data created after the backup is actually gone afterward.
- **"Start a Trial" flow**: every "Start Free Trial"/"Start free term" button on the marketing site (25 occurrences across all 7 pages) now goes to a real form instead of a dead `#trial` anchor. `site/trial.html` is a tiny static redirect page with a single `BACKEND_URL` constant (the marketing site is static/Vercel hosted and cannot itself run the form) pointing at the backend's `/trial` page. Public submission has a honeypot field and per-IP rate limiting, no login required. **Approving a request now provisions the school and its administrator account automatically** (a follow up instruction: "the school account should be setup automatically") rather than only sending an email — the applicant becomes that school's owner and is emailed their sign in details plus the admin's note (setup status/demo/training). A pending-count badge on the nav item and an alert banner on the Dashboard make new requests hard to miss (an approved-but-unnoticed request was reported once — the record existed correctly, there was just no visible indicator anywhere).
- **Admin configurable SMTP + branded transactional email**: outgoing mail had nowhere real to go (`MAIL_MAILER=log` locally, no SMTP configured anywhere, so "no email was sent" reports were accurate). Added `Settings > Mail Settings` at both the platform level (default for everything) and per-school (optional override that wins for that school's own mail while its tenant context is active, applied in `ResolveTenant` after the tenant connection is set), each with a "send test email" button; SMTP passwords are encrypted at rest (`Crypt::encryptString`) and never round-tripped back into the settings form. Every transactional email (trial status, password reset, announcements, test emails) now renders through one shared branded HTML template (`resources/views/emails/layout.blade.php`, table-based inline-styled markup for real email client compatibility, not `<style>` blocks which most clients strip) with a plain text alternative, replacing ad hoc `nl2br()`'d strings.
- Issues/Bugs found and fixed along the way:
  - The **same domain-routing positional-parameter gotcha** documented in CLAUDE.md (an untyped scalar controller parameter on a `{school}.<domain>` route silently receives the school slug instead of its own value) recurred in the new `BackupController::download()`/`restore()` actions — caught by the same "read via `$request->route()`" fix before it shipped, and documented again in that controller's docblock so it is harder to reintroduce.
  - `SchoolAdminController::store()`/`cloneSchool()` read `$data['slug']` with the short ternary (`?:`), which throws an undefined-array-key error whenever the slug field is omitted from the request entirely (Laravel's `validate()` does not guarantee a `nullable` key exists in the returned array just because it was declared) — switched to null coalescing (`??`). Only surfaced because the clone feature's own test omitted the optional slug field.
  - `Student::fullName()`/`Staff::fullName()` (fixed in an earlier item, worth restating) produced a double space when `other_names` was blank — visible on every screen rendering a person's name.
  - An earlier, improperly isolated test (`TrialRequestTest`, before this pass added proper `tenancy.sqlite_path` isolation) had left a stray real tenant SQLite file (`storage/tenants/bright_kids.sqlite`) in the actual local dev environment from a prior run; found and removed during this pass.
- QA: every item above has its own feature tests (SchoolAdminManagementTest, BackupRestoreTest, TrialRequestTest, MailSettingsTest, and additions to PlatformFlowTest), each run individually and as part of the full suite before committing. Full suite **153 tests / 529 assertions passing** at the end of this pass. Manually verified via real HTTP requests against the local dev server (not just the test suite) for the login redesign, error pages, dashboard/schools split, and mail template rendering, since these are primarily visual/experiential changes.
- Not built in this pass (kept in scope for later, not fabricated): Billing (per-school plans/features, a free-term override, upgrade/downgrade, ad hoc extra features — see tracker section 7/section 13), a Results/Scores entry screen, a guided setup wizard at school creation, real production SMTP/domain deployment (repo is deployment-ready per `DEPLOYMENT.md`'s free-tier section, but no live deploy has actually happened).

### Billing rebuilt against the SKULOS Pricing Plan & Feature catalog, marketing site updated to match (2026-07-10)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-10
- Notes: this replaces the placeholder Billing feature from the previous pass (which had never actually been seeded, and used a technical module list rather than the real tier names/features). `config/skulos.php` gained `plan_feature_catalog` (the full ~140 item checklist from the pricing document, grouped into 18 categories) and `add_on_catalog` (22 items across School Operations/Finance/Communication/Learning/Platform, purchasable on any plan). `Plan` gained `features` (json, the marketing checklist) and `is_purchasable` (false for Professional/Enterprise, shown "Coming soon" and blocked at the controller level from being assigned to a school with a 422). `PlanSeeder` now seeds Foundation/Advance/Professional/Enterprise with the real cumulative feature lists ("everything in the tier before, plus") matching the source document exactly, and was actually run against the dev database (previously the direct cause of "plans are missing": the seeder existed but had never been executed). New `school_add_ons` table + `SchoolAddOn` model + `BillingController::grantAddOn()`/`revokeAddOn()` implement the add on marketplace as admin-recorded grants (mirroring the existing waiver pattern, since there is no live payment gateway yet); add ons that map to a real RBAC module (library, hostel, transport, medical, inventory, visitors, discipline) also flip that module on so the grant is functionally real, not just bookkeeping. Add ons with no corresponding module (custom domain, additional storage, white label, API access, etc.) are recorded honestly as a purchase/entitlement with no enforcement code yet, since that infrastructure does not exist, this is documented in the config comment rather than faked. Added a `discipline` module stub to `config('skulos.modules')` to close the gap flagged in tracker section 14. `site/pricing.html` and `site/index.html` (both the dedicated pricing page and the landing page's own pricing preview) were renamed Foundation/Advance/Professional/Enterprise, given real feature highlights from the catalog, Professional/Enterprise marked "Coming soon" with a "Join the waitlist" CTA instead of "Start free term", and the source document's recommended universal inclusions line added beneath the grid.
- Issues/Bugs: the previous pass had left an unexplained ~16 second delay specific to the `storePlan`/`updatePlan` HTTP actions (vs 0.1 to 0.2s for the same operations called directly). Re-ran the full rewritten `BillingTest` suite after this rebuild and the same actions completed in 2.11s with no code change targeting that path specifically, and the full 10 test file ran in a normal 13.13s, consistent with every other feature test in the suite. Also found and fixed an unrelated pre-existing bug while touching the pricing card CSS: `.tier li::before{ content:"¹3" }` on both marketing pages was a garbled bullet character (byte-verified as literal U+00B9 plus "3", not a real glyph), replaced with a proper unicode arrow via CSS escape (`content:"\2192"`).
- Resolution: the performance anomaly did not reproduce and no root cause was found in the code path; treating it as a one-off environment hiccup (Windows disk/AV contention or similar) rather than a defect, since the same HTTP actions are now consistently fast across a full suite run. If it recurs, revisit with a profiler rather than manual reasoning.
- QA: full backend suite run after this change, **163 tests / 573 assertions passing** (up from 153, the new `BillingTest` file now covers create/edit a plan with features, retire/reoffer, mark Coming soon/sellable and the resulting assignment block, assign-plan enabling/disabling modules including downgrade, ad hoc feature grant, add on grant/revoke with the module side effect verified, waiver create/remove, permission check, index display). Verified no stray tenant files leaked into the real `storage/tenants/` directory after the run. Byte-level checked both marketing pages for the section sign character and em/en dashes outside of exempt code comments, zero found in rendered content. Did not touch the deferred full Finance module, Admissions module, Timetable module, or any of the other section 14 "NOT BUILT" product features themselves, listing a feature in a plan's marketing checklist does not make the underlying product feature exist; those statuses in section 14 are unchanged by this item and remain the honest backlog.
- Not built in this pass: live payment processing for add on purchases (grants are admin recorded, matching how waivers already work, since Finance/payment gateway integration remains out of scope until Professional is actually built), storage/student-capacity metering or enforcement for the relevant add ons, and the full CMS wiring described in tracker section 13 (the marketing site content now matches the Billing data by hand, it does not yet read from the database live, `site/` is still static HTML per the existing stopgap note).

### Pricing page feature popups, Schools page redesign, backup schedule fix, cPanel subdomain plan (2026-07-10)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-10
- **Marketing pricing cards**: added a coloured tier badge emoji per plan and a "See all features" button that opens a modal listing every feature for that plan, grouped by category, on both `site/pricing.html` and `site/index.html`'s pricing preview. The modal's data is generated directly from the seeded `Plan` records (via a one-off tinker script grouping `plan.features` by `config('skulos.plan_feature_catalog')` category) rather than hand-typed, so it can't drift from what the admin Billing screen shows. The "Included with every plan" line was also pulled out of a single grey sentence into a bordered panel with a 3x3 checklist grid (9 items, divides evenly per the Design System Rule on grid counts) plus a shorter note line below it for the annual billing/add on mention. Found and fixed an unrelated pre-existing bug while in the same CSS: `.tier li::before{ content:"¹3" }` on both pages was a garbled bullet character (byte-verified as literal U+00B9 plus "3"), replaced with a proper arrow via a CSS unicode escape.
- **Admin Schools page redesign** (user request: "the top dashboard is ok, should be followed the create a section section and then the Every school section"): the "Create a school" and "Every school" cards were previously side by side in a 2 column grid; now they stack full width in that order, matching the requested reading order. The "Every school" table gained Plan and Next due columns, and a "View" button per row opens a read only overview modal (fetched via a new `admin.schools.overview` JSON endpoint) showing address, contact details, status, plan, next billing date, administrator count, and live student/staff/parent/class counts pulled from that school's own tenant database (skipped gracefully for a school that has not been provisioned yet). Added `schools.next_billing_date` (nullable date, migration `2026_07_10_000002`), admin editable from the school's Manage page; there is no automatic invoicing engine yet so this is a manually maintained field, not computed, and is labelled as such in the UI rather than implying automation that doesn't exist.
- **Auto backup schedule fix** (user reported "the auto backup setting is not there"): the platform admin's own school detail page (`admin/school.blade.php`) only ever had "Back up now" and the history table, the actual schedule form (frequency + time of day) only existed on the school's own portal backup page. Added the same schedule form to the admin side, backed by a new `BackupAdminController::updateSchedule()` and `admin.schools.backups.schedule` route. Also found and fixed a real functional bug while doing this: `routes/console.php` registered `backups:run-scheduled` as `->hourly()`, which only ever fires at the top of the hour, while `RunScheduledBackups::isDue()` matches on the exact `HH:MM` the admin chose, so any schedule not set to a "HH:00" time (the test suite's own example schedule was `03:30`) could never actually run, it would just silently never back up. Changed to `->everyMinute()->withoutOverlapping()` so any minute can be matched, and added a regression test that travels to a specific non zero minute and asserts the backup actually gets created.
- **cPanel subdomain provisioning, planning only** (user request: "start thinking about how we will link a create school to a subdomain in my cpanel when we go live"): documented in `DEPLOYMENT.md` under a new "Linking a created school to a subdomain in cPanel" section. Recommended approach is a one time wildcard subdomain (`*.skulos.app`) plus wildcard DNS plus wildcard SSL, after which `TenantManager::provision()` needs no changes at all and every future school's subdomain resolves for free, matching how the app already behaves locally against `*.localhost`. Documented the cPanel API per subdomain fallback (`SubDomain::addsubdomain`) for hosting plans that can't do wildcard AutoSSL. No code was written, this is a decision still blocked on the real production domain being confirmed and the actual cPanel plan's wildcard support being checked, both logged as open questions.
- QA: full backend suite passing, **165 tests / assertions** after these changes (up from 163), including the new backup schedule regression test and the platform admin schedule test. Manually smoke tested via a real HTTP session against a temporary dev server: logged in as a throwaway super admin (created and deleted within the same session, never left behind), fetched `/admin/billing` and confirmed all 4 plan names/prices/Coming soon badges render, fetched `/admin/schools` and confirmed the new page order and a real `/admin/schools/{id}/overview` JSON response with live tenant counts, then cleaned up the throwaway account and stopped the temporary server. Byte level checked every touched file for the section sign character and em/en dashes outside exempt code comments, all clear. Confirmed the redesigned marketing pages validate as well formed HTML (a small script based tag balance check, not a full W3C validation) and that the embedded per plan feature JSON parses.
- Not built in this pass: any actual cPanel/DNS changes (no hosting credentials, and this was explicitly a planning request), live payment processing (unchanged from the item above), a UI to edit `next_billing_date` from anywhere other than the Manage page (the overview modal is read only by design).

### Results/Scores entry screen (2026-07-10)

- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-10
- Notes: the previously flagged gap ("report cards compute from whatever `scores` rows already exist, there was no UI to enter them") is closed. New `App\Http\Controllers\School\ExaminationController` at `school.examinations.*`, view `resources/views/school/examinations/index.blade.php`, nav stub swapped from "Soon" to a real link. A teacher picks a term and a class-and-subject pair from one dropdown (built from their own `TeachingAssignment` rows), then enters every active student's score per `AssessmentComponent` in one table and saves in a single POST. Roles with a blanket `examinations.approve_results` grant (owner/admin/principal/vice-principal/examination-officer) see and can save for every class/subject the school offers, with no `TeachingAssignment` needed; roles without it (teacher/class-teacher/subject-teacher) are hard-scoped server side to only their assigned pairs, re-checked on save too, not just on the picker. Nothing new was needed in `Score`, `AssessmentComponent`, `ResultService`, or `ReportCardService`, this screen only ever writes `Score::updateOrCreate` rows keyed on `(student_id, subject_id, term_id, assessment_component_id)`, which is exactly what those already read.
- Issues/Bugs: a live smoke test (not the automated suite) caught a real bug the suite missed: `allowedPairs()` built pair `class_arm_id`/`subject_id` values without an explicit type cast, then compared them with strict `===` against `(int)`-cast query string values in `index()`. On a real HTTP request this silently failed to match (Eloquent's SQLite driver did not guarantee the same type the manual cast produced), so revisiting the entry screen with a class/subject already chosen fell back to "choose a class and subject" instead of showing the saved scores, even though saving itself worked. The automated tests had asserted the picker rendered and that saving worked, but never asserted revisiting a pre-filled URL actually showed the entry table, so this gap went unnoticed until manual testing.
- Resolution: cast both sides of every pair comparison to `(int)` at the point the pairs are built, not just at the point they're compared. Added a regression test (`test_revisiting_with_a_pair_in_the_query_string_prefills_saved_scores`) that saves scores then re-fetches the same URL a teacher would actually reuse and asserts the input values and student name are present, closing the coverage gap that let the bug through.
- QA: full backend suite passing, **173 tests / 598 assertions** (up from 165). New `ExaminationScreenTest` (8 tests) covers: a scoped teacher sees only their pair, saving scores end to end, resaving updates rather than duplicates (`Score`'s real unique key is `student_id, subject_id, term_id, assessment_component_id`, not including `class_arm_id`), a teacher without a matching `TeachingAssignment` is blocked with a 403 and nothing is written, a score above a component's `max_score` is rejected and the whole batch is discarded rather than partially saved, a school owner can save without any `TeachingAssignment` at all, the query-string-revisit regression above, and the empty state for a teacher with no assignments at all. Manually smoke tested via a real HTTP session on the actual subdomain host (not the bare domain, since school portal sessions are host scoped) with a throwaway teacher account and school, created and fully deleted afterward including its tenant SQLite file, confirmed via `find storage -iname "*<slug>*"` returning nothing.
- Not built in this pass: an explicit "clear this score" affordance (a blank cell on save is left untouched, not deleted, matching partial multi-session entry as the safer default); this is intentionally revisited if a school actually asks for it.

Added to `school.academics.index`/`AcademicsController`: typing a start year (e.g. 2025) creates an `AcademicSession` named "2025/2026 Session" with its three `Term` rows "First/Second/Third Term 2025/2026" in one action, matching the exact naming the user specified for Nigerian schools. ### Student import: Class, House, State of Origin, LGA, Address, and admin configurable required fields (2026-07-10)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-10
- Notes: `StudentImporter` gained 5 columns (`class`, `house`, `state_of_origin`, `lga`, `address`); `lga` did not exist on `Student` at all before (new tenant migration, nullable, alongside the existing `state_of_origin`). "class" and "house" are matched by name (case-insensitive, trimmed) against the school's real `ClassArm` labels and `House` names, not stored as free text, an unrecognized name is rejected with the exact value quoted back in the error rather than silently dropped. Which of the optional fields (this new set, plus the pre-existing gender/date of birth/other names/parent mobile) a school requires is now admin configurable at `school.students.settings.*`, backed by `config('skulos.student_optional_fields')` as the catalog and tenant `Setting` (`student_required_fields`) as the per-school choice, new permission `students.manage_settings`. `admission_number`/`first_name`/`last_name` stay unconditionally required and `email` is unconditionally excluded from the configurable list, per the standing "student email must never be required" business decision, confirmed with a test that stuffing "email" into the stored setting directly (bypassing the validated admin form) still does not make it required.
- Issues/Bugs: found and fixed a second pre-existing em-dash in on-screen text while touching the import page, `resources/views/school/students/import.blade.php`'s columns list rendered "admission_number — Admission Number" with a literal em-dash (byte-verified U+2014), same defect as the pricing card bullets fixed earlier this session, replaced with a comma. Also confirmed operationally (via a live smoke test, not the automated suite, since it depends on server/database state the test harness resets every run) that adding a new permission code to `config('skulos.extra_permissions')` requires re-running `RbacSeeder` against the platform database before it takes effect anywhere, and that even after reseeding, a school role that was `cloneTemplateRole()`'d before the reseed does not retroactively gain the new permission, only the shared template role and any newly cloned role do. This is an existing characteristic of how role cloning works (a snapshot copy, not a live reference to the template), not something introduced by this change, but it had not been written down anywhere before and is worth knowing before adding the next new permission code.
- QA: full backend suite **184 tests / 631 assertions** (up from 178). Updated the two pre-existing tests that asserted the exact old CSV header string, added new coverage for class/house name resolution (valid and unrecognized), admin configured required fields being enforced on import, and the email exclusion. Manually smoke tested via a real HTTP session: viewed and saved the new settings screen, confirmed the import page reflects "Required" badges, and uploaded a real CSV with all 13 columns filled in and confirmed the student's class, house, state of origin, LGA, and address all landed correctly. Cleaned up the throwaway school/user/tenant file afterward.
- Not built in this pass: the single student create/edit form these settings will also govern once it exists (tracker section 15, still queued); the dedicated house-only bulk assignment template (admission number, class, house columns, pre-filled with every current student) is a related but distinct item, still queued below, this pass only added `house` as one column among many on the general student importer.

### Assessment structure admin section (2026-07-10)
- Status: Done

New `AssessmentController` at `school.examinations.structure`, linked from the Results page. Added a `type` column ('ca'/'exam', migration `2026_07_10_000002`) purely to group the page into "Test / Continuous Assessment" and "Examinations" as the user asked, this does not change grading math at all, `ResultService::subjectResult()` still sums every component regardless of type, a school can still run any formula summing to any total (re-verified by the existing `test_different_school_can_use_a_totally_different_format` in `ResultsTest`, unaffected and still passing). Written correctly from the start once noticed, `destroy()` checks for existing `Score` rows first and refuses with a clear explanation instead of deleting, covered by `test_a_component_with_scores_cannot_be_deleted`.

New `AssessmentStructureScreenTest` (8 tests) covers adding a CA and an Exam component and the running total, the page actually grouping them correctly, a duplicate name being rejected, editing, deleting a component with no scores, refusing to delete one that has scores (with real `Subject`/`ClassArm`/`Term`/`Score` fixtures rather than arbitrary foreign key numbers, since SQLite's FK enforcement setting could otherwise mask a broken test), a teacher without `approve_results` being blocked from writing, and the same teacher still being able to view. Manually smoke tested via a real HTTP session: added a CA and an Exam component, confirmed the page grouped them under the right headings and the "100 marks in total" running sum was correct, cleaned up the throwaway school and user afterward.

### Default class structure loader, plus a real ClassArm bug found while building it (2026-07-10)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-10
- Notes: `config('skulos.default_class_structure')` uses the same 4 keys as `default_subjects` (nursery/primary/jss/ss) so the two catalogs stay in lockstep; "jss" and "ss" deliberately share one "Secondary" section rather than creating two, matching how most schools actually run it. `AcademicDefaults::applyClassStructure()` is idempotent by name at every level (section, class), same pattern as the existing `applySubjectCatalog()`, safe to tick the same tier twice. New action on `school.academics.index` above the sections list, ticking any combination and submitting creates everything in one request.
- Issues/Bugs: found a genuine blocking bug while building this, not a hypothetical one. `AcademicsController::storeClass()` (the existing, already shipped "Add a class" action) only ever created a `SchoolClass`, never a `ClassArm` — and `Student.current_class_arm_id` points at a `ClassArm`, not a `SchoolClass`. Every class created through the UI up to this point could never actually hold a student. Confirmed by grepping the codebase for any `ClassArm::create` outside of `SchoolCloneService` and test fixtures, there genuinely was none. Also had no way to add a second stream/arm to a class at all (e.g. "JSS 1 B" alongside "JSS 1 A").
- Resolution: `storeClass()` now also creates one default (unnamed, single stream) `ClassArm` so a class is immediately usable the moment it is created, and a new `storeArm()` action lets a school add named streams later (found or creates the `Arm` by name, refuses a duplicate stream on the same class). The class structure loader reuses the same "create with a default ClassArm" logic. The rough edge noted at the time (adding a named stream to a class that still only has the unnamed default one used to leave both sitting side by side) was fixed 2026-07-11, see "Stream splitting fix" below.
- QA: full backend suite **199 tests / 671 assertions** (up from 192). New tests cover: loading a tier creates the section, classes in sequence, a usable `ClassArm` per class, and default subjects; loading the same tier twice does not duplicate; JSS and SS share one section; a role without `academics.create` is blocked; the `storeClass`/`storeArm` regression tests (a created class has a `ClassArm`, a named stream can be added, the same stream cannot be added twice). Manually smoke tested via a real HTTP session: loaded JSS and SS together, confirmed one "Secondary" section with all 6 classes each showing "Single stream" for their default arm, cleaned up the throwaway school and user afterward.
- Not built in this pass: a rename/merge flow for the auto-created default stream (see the known rough edge above), the "Pre-Nursery" class name keeps its hyphen deliberately (user confirmed 2026-07-10 this is a standard spelled term, not stylistic dash punctuation, exemption now also recorded in CLAUDE.md's no-dashes rule).

### Houses: defaults, manual assignment, and CSV round trip (2026-07-10)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-10
- Notes: `House` existed as a model with genuinely no admin screen at all, found the same way as the ClassArm and AssessmentComponent gaps this session, by grepping for any controller referencing it and finding none. New `HouseController`/`school.houses.*`: `config('skulos.default_houses')` (4 neutral colour based names, not any real school's actual house names, those would be fabricated) loadable in one action; add/remove a house by hand (removing nulls out `house_id` on its members via the existing `nullOnDelete`, does not delete students); a manual list at `school.houses.assign-form` with a picker per student, auto saved on change; and a CSV round trip that is deliberately not a normal "create new rows" importer, `bulk-template` downloads every active student pre-filled with admission number, current class (reference only), and current house name, `bulk-assign` re-reads the same file and only ever updates the house column, a blank house cell for a row leaves that student's assignment untouched rather than clearing it, so a partially filled in sheet can never wipe assignments no one meant to touch.
- Issues/Bugs: two real bugs caught by running the tests, not found in review this time. (1) `House` had no `students()` relation at all, `HouseController::index()`'s `withCount('students')` threw "Call to undefined method" the first time the test suite actually exercised the page, added the missing `hasMany(Student::class)`. (2) `bulkTemplate()` was gated behind `students.manage_settings` (the same permission as the actual mutating actions), which meant a plain teacher who should be able to view and export could not, downloading a read only CSV is a `students.view` level action, not a management one, corrected. Also found and fixed a fourth em-dash placeholder this session while working in this area, `resources/views/school/students/index.blade.php`'s gender column rendered a literal "—" for an unset gender, replaced with "Not set"; swept the rest of `resources/views/` and every `.php` file for the same character and confirmed everything else is inside exempt code comments.
- QA: full backend suite **208 tests / 692 assertions** (up from 199). New `HouseScreenTest` (9 tests) covers loading defaults, loading them twice not duplicating, adding a house and assigning a student manually, removing a house unassigning rather than deleting its members, the bulk template being correctly pre-filled (including confirming standard CSV quoting behavior for a value containing a space is not a bug), bulk assign only touching rows with a house filled in, bulk assign reporting an unknown admission number or house name, a teacher without `students.manage_settings` being blocked from every mutating action, and the same teacher still being able to view the page and download the template. Manually smoke tested via a real HTTP session: loaded the 4 defaults, confirmed all 4 rendered, confirmed the assign screen listed the one seeded student, cleaned up the throwaway school, user, and tenant file afterward.
- Not built in this pass: nothing deferred, this item is complete as specified.

### Houses: rename, retire/reactivate, a view page, and a bulk save fix (2026-07-10/2026-07-11)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-10
- Notes: three direct follow ups on the Houses feature above, all from live testing feedback rather than the original request. (1) A house detail page (`school.houses.show`) with a rename/recolour form, a retire/reactivate toggle, its member list, and a members CSV download; retiring adds `houses.is_active` (migration `2026_07_10_000003`) rather than deleting, so history and current members are kept, it just drops out of every assignment picker (`House::active()` scope). (2) The manual assign screen was redesigned per direct feedback: it originally auto-saved on every single row change (a page reload per student), replaced with one form covering the whole page and a single "Save this page" button, matching the same array-of-inputs pattern the Results entry screen already established; also added a "show only students without a house" filter so an admin can page through exactly the gap that needs filling. (3) Staff membership in a house was asked for too ("download all the students and staff in that house") but the Staff module itself has no admin screens or a house_id column at all yet (nav still says "Soon"), so there is nothing real to pick from or list, staff are deliberately left out of both the page and the download rather than building a picker with nothing behind it, flagged as blocked on the Staff module existing first.
- Issues/Bugs: two real bugs, both caught by live smoke testing, not the automated suite (mirrors the exact pattern from the Results entry screen bug earlier this session). (1) A test assertion for "retired houses disappear from the picker" false failed because the retire action's own flash message legitimately names the house on the very next page load, a blanket `assertDontSee($name)` cannot tell that apart from an actual `<option>`, fixed by asserting the specific `<option>` tag via regex instead. (2) A genuine production bug: the bulk save's empty "No house" selection threw a 500 (`FOREIGN KEY constraint failed`). Laravel's `ConvertEmptyStringsToNull` middleware turns an empty form value into `null` before the controller ever sees it, but the code only checked for `''`; `(int) null` casts to `0`, and `house_id = 0` matches no house row. Fixed to check for both `''` and `null`, with a regression test that submits `null` directly (replicating what the middleware actually delivers, not what the code assumed it would receive).
- QA: full backend suite **213 tests / 711 assertions** (up from 208). Added 5 new tests to `HouseScreenTest`: bulk assigning several students in one save, the empty-selection regression above, the unassigned only filter, renaming, and retire/reactivate (including the fixed "disappears from the picker" check). Manually smoke tested via a real HTTP session end to end: fetched the assign page, submitted a bulk save for two students in one request (confirming no per-row reload), hit and fixed the 500 live, then confirmed the house detail page correctly showed the resulting member state.
- Not built in this pass: staff membership in a house (blocked on the Staff module itself, see Notes above), a rename/merge UI for the auto created default class stream flagged in the previous item (unrelated, still open).

- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-11
- Notes: two direct corrections from user feedback. (1) `config('skulos.default_class_structure')`'s nursery tier was wrong: had "Creche, Pre-Nursery, Nursery 1, Nursery 2" (4 classes), corrected to the real Nigerian order "Creche/Toddler, Reception 1, Reception 2, Nursery 1, Nursery 2" (5 classes); also added "reception" and "toddler" to `default_subjects.nursery`'s section-name match keywords so a "Reception" section still suggests the right subject catalog. (2) Fixed the "known rough edge" flagged in the class structure loader item above: adding the first named stream to a class that still only has the auto-created default (unnamed) stream now converts that same `ClassArm` row (renames it) instead of creating a second one alongside it, keeping any already-enrolled student correctly assigned since the row's id never changes, only its `arm_id`. Once a class has any named stream, further additions create new rows as before.
