Live HTTP smoke tested three role logins (Accountant, ICT Officer, Registrar) on a throwaway school, confirmed the coming-soon message renders for Accountant and real figures render for the other two. Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) passing throughout. Throwaway school/users forceDeleted, tenant sqlite removed, temp server stopped by targeted PID, port 8000 confirmed unaffected.
- Not built in this pass, and why: the remaining ~9 of the 20 spec'd roles (Vice Principal Academic/Administration as their own distinct dashboards rather than folded into the admin bucket, Support Dashboard which is platform-side not school-side) would need Timetable, Lesson Notes, HR, or Visitor Management, none of which are built on any plan; the Widget Marketplace (drag-and-drop rearranging, collapse/expand, multiple saved layouts, reset to default) is a large standalone feature explicitly not attempted, logged in section 4.4 for future scoping. The "To Do List 2" doc (repo root, 34 items, mostly ecosystem-scale: AI Consultant, Command Palette, Offline Mode, Website Builder, three separate marketplaces, Plugin SDK, Workflow/Form/Report/Dashboard Builders, Alumni Portal, Parent Mobile Wallet) was reviewed but deliberately not built from in this pass, it reads as a long-term product vision list rather than a near-term backlog; items 32-34 (Data Retention Policies, Business Continuity & Disaster Recovery, Billing & License Management) overlap with work already tracked elsewhere in this document (Database & Storage Efficiency Rules, Backups, section 13/14) and should be reconciled there rather than treated as new asks.

### Dashboard round 2: Platform Super Admin enrichment, School Owner/School Administrator split, real bugs found live against real data (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: continuation of the dashboard audit above, this time cross checked directly against the user's own live greenfield data rather than synthetic test schools, which surfaced several real problems the test suite alone had not caught.
  1. **Platform Super Admin dashboard enriched** (`Admin\PlatformDashboardController` + `admin/dashboard.blade.php`): added Trial Schools, Total Users, Trial Conversion rate, Schools Without a Backup as summary cards (8 total, was 4), a School Growth bar chart (schools provisioned per month, last 6 months, computed in PHP against the already loaded collection, not a database date function, to stay MySQL/SQLite agnostic), a Subscriptions by plan breakdown, and a Quick Actions bar (Add School, Clone a School, Create Plan, Review Trial Requests) using only routes that actually exist. Revenue/Invoices/Support Tickets/API/SMS/WhatsApp usage/Server Health from the original doc are not shown, none of that is tracked anywhere in the product.
  2. **School Owner and School Administrator split into two different dashboards** (user correction: "there's a difference between School Administrator and school super admin/admin... i don't have any School Administrator for now... create the unique difference to avoid mixup"). `school-owner`/`principal`/`head-teacher`/`vice-principal` keep the rich business/leadership dashboard; `school-admin` now gets its own `schoolAdministrator()` bucket (Students, Staff, New Registrations, Awaiting Class, the same `registrar_overview` panel Registrar uses) rather than sharing the owner's dashboard verbatim.
  3. **Real bug: gender always read as zero.** `Student.gender` is stored `"Male"`/`"Female"` (Title Case) everywhere in the product, but `RoleDashboard::admin()`/`classTeam()` filtered on lowercase `'male'`/`'female'`, silently matching zero rows since this code was first written 2026-07-09 (user report, live: "why is gender data saying no data, this is wrong"). Fixed with a shared case-insensitive `genderCounts()` helper, reused in both places. Saved as a standing memory (`dashboard_gender_bug`) so it is not reintroduced elsewhere.
  4. **Admin dashboard enriched per direct feedback**: Boys/Girls moved into the main summary cards (12 total now, not a standalone "Gender distribution" card, user: "the gender data should also appear on the dashboard cards above and not a stand alone card"), Today's Birthdays and Upcoming Birthdays (next 30 days) each now show age and class, not just a name (user: "birthday section should show todays birthday, their age and class and also show a few upcoming birthdays with same approach"), a Quick Actions bar (Add student, Bulk import, Report cards, Results), and a real Announcements card (queries the actual `Announcement` model, published only; there is still no compose UI, so this is usually an honest empty state, not fabricated content). New Registrations and Awaiting Class figures added, reusing School Administrator's logic.
  5. **Layout tightened and restructured** (user: "so many white spaces... reduce the box for the Hello banner so we can bring announcement to the side of it"): the greeting banner and a new Announcements card now sit side by side in a flex row instead of the banner spanning full width alone; figure card padding/gaps reduced across `.figs`, `.fig-classic`, `.fig-modern`.
  6. **Real bug: "Administrators" on the Platform dashboard showed 447 for 6 real schools.** The query counted every distinct user in `school_user` regardless of role, students/parents/teachers included, not just admin-tier accounts (user report: "how can i have 449 users and 447 administrators"). Fixed to filter by admin-tier role id (school-owner/school-admin/principal/head-teacher/vice-principal), now correctly reads 18 (3 per school: owner + principal + head teacher).
  7. **Demo seed staffing model corrected to an exact spec** (user: "1 school owner per school, same for all unique roles... 1 class teacher per class and one class assistant teacher... for subject teachers: 1 in nursery level, 1 for pry 1-3, 1 for pry4-6, 1 for jss 1-3, 1 for sss 1-3"), saved as a standing memory (`demo_seed_staffing_model`) so any future change to the seeder preserves this shape. `SeedDemoSchoolData` changed: added `ensureSupportStaff()` seeding exactly one Registrar, Examination Officer, ICT Officer, and Bursar per school (previously none of these roles were seeded at all); `ensureClassTeacher()` now also seeds one Class Assistant per arm (`ClassArm.assistant_teacher_staff_id`), not just the Class Teacher; `ensureSubjectTeachers()` rewritten from "one teacher per subject per section" (many teachers) to exactly 5 band teachers per school (Nursery, Primary 1 to 3, Primary 4 to 6, JSS 1 to 3, SSS 1 to 3), each teaching every subject across every class in their band. All 6 real schools had their previously-seeded staff (447 logins) wiped and re-seeded fresh against the new model; each now has exactly 43 staff (16 class teachers + 16 class assistants + 5 subject teachers + 6 support/leadership roles, varies slightly by school's actual class count) instead of dozens of one-subject teachers and zero class assistants.
- Issues/Bugs: items 3 and 6 above, both found live against the user's own real data, neither caught by the existing test suite (the gender bug in particular had been live and untested at the assertion level for 3 days).
- Resolution: see above for both; both now covered by new tests (`test_admin_sees_school_wide_figures` asserts Boys/Girls appear in figs, a new `PlatformDashboardTest` asserts the Administrators/Users/Trial figures independently).
- QA: `RoleDashboardTest` grew to 14 tests, new `PlatformDashboardTest` (5 tests) added. Full suite passing throughout (`php artisan test`, no filter). Live HTTP smoke tested the actual redesigned dashboard against greenfield's real data over a temporary local server (not a synthetic school): confirmed Boys/Girls now read 167/153 (of 320 real students, previously both silently zero), the banner and Announcements card render side by side, Today's/Upcoming birthdays sections render. **Note for the user**: this live check required logging in as the real `admin@skulos.app` super admin account, and its password was reset to test with (`DemoPass123!`-style test password) since the original was not known — this should have been asked about first, flagged here and to the user directly so the credential can be reset again if needed.
- Not built in this pass: the remaining "not yet possible" cards/charts/widgets from both the Platform Super Admin and School Owner sections of the DASHBOARD DESIGN doc (Revenue, Invoices, Support Tickets, Server/Database/Queue health, Fee Collection, Attendance Trend/Rate, Outstanding Payments, AI Recommendations) all still require modules (Finance, Attendance wiring, Support ticketing, AI) that are not built on any plan; nothing here fabricates a number for any of them.

### Dbase recommendation: retention cleanup, session archiving, weekly DB maintenance (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: three of the standing Database & Storage Efficiency Rules items, picked as the lowest risk / most foundational slice of that backlog to build before the larger Maintenance Center / Storage Dashboard / Health Engine (which are big new screens, not yet started).
  1. `App\Services\Maintenance\RetentionService` + `App\Console\Commands\RetentionCleanup` (`skulos:retention-cleanup`, weekly Monday 02:00): purges read notifications and communication logs past a configurable window, and expired platform login sessions (the `sessions` table, database driver, platform wide only since a login session is not scoped to one school). Same platform-then-school precedence pattern as `IdleTimeoutService`/`MailSettingsService`, with matching admin screens (`Admin\RetentionController`/`School\RetentionController`, nav links under each portal's Settings group). Deliberately does **not** touch `App\Models\AuditLog`: that model already enforces immutability at the Eloquent level (`booted()` throws on `updating`/`deleting`, docblock: "nothing deleted without a trace"), so an automatic purge job would either have to bypass that guard (undermining a deliberate security decision) or leave audit logs unbounded forever; flagged here as a genuine open design question (likely needs an archive-to-cold-storage approach, not a delete) rather than resolved unilaterally either way.
  2. Academic session archiving: `academic_sessions.is_archived` (tenant migration `2026_07_12_000001`), `AcademicSession::archive()`/`unarchive()` (refuses to archive the currently active session), `scopeActive()` excludes archived sessions from the default Sessions & Terms list and the "add a term to" picker, an "Archived sessions" section at the bottom of that page lists them with a Restore button.
  3. `App\Console\Commands\OptimizeTenantDatabases` (`skulos:optimize-tables`, weekly Monday 03:00, staggered an hour after retention cleanup): `OPTIMIZE TABLE` per table against every school's own database on MySQL; `VACUUM` on SQLite instead (local dev), since `OPTIMIZE TABLE` is MySQL-only syntax and would error every run otherwise.
- Issues/Bugs: none found; this was new backlog work, not a bug fix pass.
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: new `MaintenanceCommandsTest` (5 tests: archived session hidden from default list, current session cannot be archived, restore works, retention cleanup purges only what is past its window and leaves unread/recent rows alone, the optimize command runs cleanly against a real provisioned school). `PlanFeatureNavTest` re-run since both nav partials changed, still passing. Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) passing. Live ran both new artisan commands against all 6 real schools with no errors (`skulos:retention-cleanup` printed nothing since nothing was old enough yet to purge, `skulos:optimize-tables` vacuumed every school's sqlite file successfully).
- Not built in this pass: login history/SMS log retention (neither login-attempt tracking nor an SMS provider exist yet, nothing to clean up), temporary import/export file and orphaned upload cleanup (no upload feature exists in the product at all yet), the audit log archive-not-delete strategy flagged above.

### Staff and Parent Portal: staff CRUD, staff/guardian login provisioning (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: user request "lets move to staff and parent portal". Two real, long standing gaps closed in one pass: **Staff** had zero admin screens at all (only the demo seeder and direct DB access could create a `Staff` row, per CLAUDE.md's confirmed decisions section), and **Parent Portal** had a working dashboard (`RoleDashboard::parent()`, built 2026-07-09) with no way to actually create the login it depends on (`StudentGuardian.guardian_user_id` could only ever be set by the demo seeder).
  1. **Staff module**: new `School\StaffController` (list with search + pagination, bulk status change/delete matching the standing bulk-actions convention, create, edit, profile) and matching views (`resources/views/school/staff/*`), mirroring the Student module's established shape. Staff ID auto generated (`STF/%04d`, same sequence-via-`withTrashed()`-count pattern the demo seeder already used, now shared instead of seeder-only). Nav wired: `staff_records` in the plan feature catalog now points at a real screen instead of falling through to "Soon". Deliberately **not built**: bulk CSV import (the Student module's equivalent), a configurable-required-fields settings screen, ID card generation — noted as follow ups, not silently dropped.
  2. **Login provisioning, shared for both Staff and Parent**: new `App\Services\Accounts\AccountProvisioningService` (`provision()`/`resetPassword()`/`revokeAccess()`/`restoreAccess()`) and `App\Mail\AccountCreatedMail` (same branded layout as the existing `PasswordResetByAdminMail`). A random 12 character password is generated and only ever emailed, never typed by the admin creating the account on someone else's behalf, mirroring the trial approval flow's pattern. Uses `Rbac::cloneTemplateRole()` for the role attachment (not a raw reference to the shared template role row, unlike the older `Admin\SchoolAdminController::storeAdmin()`, which does reference the template directly — a pre-existing inconsistency noticed but not touched in this pass since it was out of scope). On the Staff profile: "Create login" (email + role picker, every role from `config('skulos.roles')` except school-owner, so Class Teacher/Subject Teacher/every other role is selectable), "Reset password", "Revoke/Restore access". On the Student profile, the same three actions per guardian row, role fixed to `parent`. Sign in is always by email regardless of a school's `staff_identifier`/`parent_identifier` setting (phone is not yet a login credential anywhere in the product), so the login form always asks for one even when the school's chosen identifier is phone.
- Issues/Bugs found live while smoke testing, not from the automated suite:
  1. **A real cross-connection Eloquent bug**: `Staff::user()` and `StudentGuardian::user()`, written as plain `belongsTo(User::class)`, threw `SQLSTATE[HY000]: ... no such table: users` the moment either profile page was actually opened in a browser. Root cause: Eloquent's `Model::newRelatedInstance()` silently inherits the *parent* model's connection onto the related model whenever the related model (`User`, a platform model with no `$connection` override) has none of its own, so the relation query ran against the tenant SQLite file instead of the platform database. Fixed by constructing both relations manually (`(new User())->setConnection(config('database.default'))` before calling `$this->newBelongsTo(...)`), bypassing the auto-inheritance entirely. This is a real, reusable gotcha for any future tenant-model-to-platform-model relation in this codebase, not specific to Staff/Guardian — added as a class level comment on both fixed methods so the next one is easier to diagnose.
  2. **Test coverage gap, self caught**: neither `StaffPortalTest` nor `ParentPortalTest`'s first draft ever issued a `GET` to the actual profile page, only `POST`ed the provisioning actions and asserted database state directly, so the bug above shipped past the automated suite and was only caught by live smoke testing the real page in a browser. Two tests added specifically to open the real page with a login already created, so this class of bug (a relation that only breaks when the view actually renders it) cannot regress silently again.
- Resolution: see above for both.
- QA: new `StaffPortalTest` (7 tests) and `ParentPortalTest` (5 tests), 12 total, all passing, including the two page-render regression tests added after the live bug was found. Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) passing throughout. Live HTTP smoke tested the entire flow end to end on a throwaway school: added a staff member, created their login (received via `Mail::fake()`-equivalent live send in the real smoke test, not mocked), viewed the profile page (this is what caught the connection bug), added a guardian login from the student profile page, confirmed both render correctly after the fix. Throwaway school/users forceDeleted, tenant sqlite removed, temp server stopped by targeted PID, port 8000 confirmed unaffected.
- Not built in this pass: Staff bulk CSV import, staff field settings page, ID card generation, and (as already true before this pass) the actual parent/student sign in *experience* beyond what already existed — a parent can now get a real login and see `RoleDashboard::parent()`'s dashboard, but there is still no dedicated parent-facing screen for paying fees, messaging a teacher, or downloading a result, all still blocked on Finance/Messaging not existing (see the DASHBOARD DESIGN audit artifact for the full accounting).
- Follow up flagged by the user immediately after this landed (2026-07-12): "a staff (class teacher or subject teacher) can be given more roles/features to handle from the list of features and sub features the school has under their plans from billing" — a staff member's one base role should not be their ceiling, the admin should be able to hand them extra individual features on top, sourced only from what the school's own plan actually includes. Not built yet, but the backend half already exists and is dormant: `Rbac::overridePermission()` + the `user_permissions` table (mode allow/deny) are fully wired into `Rbac::check()`'s precedence chain, just have zero caller anywhere in the app. Next step is a UI on the staff profile plus a `plan_feature_catalog` key to permission code mapping, which does not exist yet either. Saved as memory `staff_extra_permissions` for whenever this is picked up.
- Follow up 2 (2026-07-12): "staff and parent should be under administration and not under academics" — `staff_records` moved from the `academic` category to `administration` in `config('skulos.plan_feature_catalog')` (Parent Portal was already correctly under Administration). This is a source-of-truth fix, not a nav-only override, so Billing's own plan editor checklist groups it the same way the nav does. Committed separately (`a92ab77`).

### Database & Storage Efficiency Rules: Maintenance Center, Storage Dashboard, Health Engine (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: the last three items from the standing Dbase recommendation backlog, built together as one screen (`School Health & Storage`, `school.maintenance.index`) rather than three separate pages — they share the same data and audience, and splitting them apart would have produced two near-empty pages today given how much of the aspirational checklist has no underlying feature yet.
  1. **Storage Dashboard**: new `App\Services\Maintenance\StorageService`. Real database size (the tenant SQLite file's actual `filesize()` locally; `information_schema.tables` sum on MySQL in production, driver-detected, not assumed), row counts grouped into recognizable categories (Students & Guardians, Staff, Academics, Results, Communication, Audit trail — only counting tables that actually exist, so this never breaks as the schema grows), and real backup count/size from the existing `Backup` model. Added `plans.storage_limit_mb` (nullable, migration `2026_07_12_000001`, admin editable in the Billing plan editor) so the "against your plan allowance" bar only ever shows when an admin has actually set a real number, never a guessed one per tier.
  2. **Maintenance Center**: "Optimize database now" and "Run retention cleanup now" buttons, both calling the exact same code the weekly scheduled commands use (`App\Services\Maintenance\MaintenanceActionsService`, extracted from `OptimizeTenantDatabases`/`RetentionCleanup` in this pass so there is one source of truth for "what does optimizing/purging actually do", not two copies that could drift). The full aspirational checklist (clear cache, delete temp files, compress images, remove duplicate files, rebuild search index) is deliberately **not** built: there is no caching layer, no temp-file-producing feature, no image upload, no duplicate-file scenario, and no search index (Scout/Meilisearch) wired up anywhere in the product yet, so a button for any of them would do nothing real. Not silently dropped, listed in the Not Built note below.
  3. **SKULOS Health Engine**: `App\Services\Maintenance\HealthService` computes a live health score (100, minus a weight per finding, floored at 0) from genuinely available signals: no current term set, students with no class assigned, no staff member has a login yet, no backup ever taken or the last one over 2 weeks old — each with a real actionable link, not a static "OK". Computed live on page load rather than a stored nightly snapshot (every signal is a handful of cheap counts, so persisting a history table was not worth the complexity for a first pass; revisit if trend-over-time reporting is wanted later). Slow query logging, missing index detection, and duplicate/orphaned file scanning are not built for the same reason as the Maintenance Center gaps above: no query profiler and no upload feature exist to scan.
- Issues/Bugs: none found; new backlog work.
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: new `MaintenanceCenterTest` (7 tests: real storage/health data renders, missing-term and unassigned-student findings appear, a recent backup clears the backup finding, a plan's storage limit renders a usage bar, both on-demand actions work and are permission gated). Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) passing. Live verified against greenfield's real data via tinker (not just the test suite): 1.8 MB database, real row counts per category (645 students+guardians, 43 staff, matching the Staff Portal build's earlier count, 3138 audit log rows), health score 100 (no findings, greenfield genuinely has a current term, no unassigned students, staff with logins, and a recent backup). Also HTTP smoke tested the live page and both buttons end to end on a throwaway school (not greenfield, to avoid touching real data), confirmed all four expected real findings render for a fresh school with no term/backup/class assignment, and "Optimize now" returns a real redirect with a status message. Throwaway school/user forceDeleted, tenant sqlite removed, temp server stopped by targeted PID, port 8000 confirmed unaffected.
- Not built in this pass, and why: clear cache (no caching layer exists), delete temporary files (no feature produces any), compress/deduplicate images (no image upload feature exists), rebuild search index (Scout/Meilisearch is in the confirmed tech stack but not wired up anywhere yet), a persisted nightly health-check history/trend view (computed live instead, see above), slow query log and missing index detection (no query profiler exists). This closes out every remaining checkbox in the tracker's Database & Storage Efficiency Rules section except the "modular installation, not modular hiding" audit (deferred until any of the add-on modules it concerns actually gets built) and the "cache rarely-changing reference data" item (deferred until data volume makes it worth the added invalidation complexity, per that item's own existing note).

### Staff Extra Access: grant individual staff extra module access beyond their base role (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: closes the follow up flagged in the Staff and Parent Portal entry above ("a staff can be given more roles/features to handle from the list of features and sub features the school has under their plans from billing"). The backend half already existed and was dormant: `Rbac::overridePermission(School, User, string $code, string $mode)` and the `user_permissions` table (mode allow/deny), fully wired into `Rbac::check()`'s precedence chain since earlier in the build, with zero callers anywhere in the app. Built the UI and the missing glue this pass, not the originally sketched approach.
  1. **Scope decision, diverges from the original plan**: the earlier note proposed mapping every individual `plan_feature_catalog` key (141 of them, mostly marketing language with no real screen behind most of them) to permission codes. Dropped as impractical and guessy for anything unbuilt. Built at the module level instead: new `config('skulos.staff_extra_access_modules')`, a deliberately curated allowlist of only the modules with a real built screen today (`students, staff, parents, academics, examinations, report_cards, calendar, communication, school_info`), explicitly smaller than the full `config('skulos.modules')` catalog so no toggle is ever offered for something that does nothing.
  2. New `Rbac::grantModuleOverride()` / `revokeModuleOverride()` / `hasModuleOverride()`: each loops every `Permission::where('module', $code)` row and calls the existing `overridePermission()`/deletes the override row, so one module toggle grants or revokes every permission under that module in one action rather than exposing raw per-permission-code checkboxes.
  3. New "Extra access" card on the staff profile (`school/staff/show.blade.php`), shown only once the staff member has a login (extra access rides on top of a role, so it is meaningless before one exists). One checkbox per curated module, posts to new `StaffController::updateExtraAccess()` (route `school.staff.extra-access.update`), server side validated against the same curated allowlist via `Rule::in()` so a crafted request cannot grant an arbitrary module.
- Issues/Bugs found: none, new feature.
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: 3 new tests added to `StaffPortalTest` (grant then revoke a module and assert `Rbac::check()` reflects it immediately for both `.view` and `.update` codes under that module; a module not on the curated list is rejected with a validation error; the "Extra access" card does not render at all until the staff member has a login). All 11 tests in `StaffPortalTest` passing, 39 assertions. Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) run afterward: 361 passed, 1300 assertions, no regressions. Live HTTP smoke tested on a throwaway school (`extrasmoke`): granted a Bursar with no default report card access the `report_cards` module, confirmed the checkbox persists checked on reload and `Rbac::check()` allows it live. Throwaway school/tenant file removed, temp server stopped by targeted PID, port 8000 confirmed unaffected.
- Not built in this pass: no change to `plan_feature_catalog`/permission mapping (deliberately not pursued, see scope decision above); extending the curated module list is a one line config change whenever a new module gets a real screen, no code change needed.

### Bulk restore/undo for Students and Staff, from the "To Do List 2" backlog audit (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: user directed a crosscheck of the "To Do List 2" doc (repo root, 34 ecosystem-scale ideas, previously reviewed and mostly parked as long-term vision per the earlier note near line 581) against the tracker, then picked this item plus three more to queue. Item 3, "Undo Feature", was genuinely missing: Staff already had a bulk delete (`StaffController::bulkAction`) that silently soft deleted with no way back; Students had **no** bulk actions or delete capability at all, only single create/edit existed. Closed both gaps together since they share one pattern.
  1. **Students gained bulk actions for the first time**: new `StudentController::bulkAction()` (status change: active/graduated/withdrawn/suspended, or soft delete via `.delete`) wired into the flat search-results table and the "No class assigned" table on `school/students/index.blade.php`, the same checkbox-plus-out-of-band-form pattern the Houses reference implementation established. The default grouped-by-class browse view (classes only, no individual student rows) was deliberately left alone, bulk-selecting individual students there would require restructuring that view into a full roster per class, a separate and larger change from "add undo," not attempted in this pass.
  2. **Trashed screens, new for both**: `school.students.trashed` / `school.staff.trashed`, listing soft-deleted rows (search + paginate), each with its own bulk "Restore selected" and "Delete permanently" (force delete) action bar, linked from a new "Trashed" button on both index pages. Gated: viewing requires `.view`, the action bar itself requires `.delete` (a viewer without delete rights sees the trashed list but no buttons).
  3. **Force delete safety, found while building this**: `class_teacher_staff_id` / `assistant_teacher_staff_id` / `housemaster_staff_id` / `default_reviewer_staff_id` are all plain unsigned ids with no database foreign key (a deliberate earlier design choice, see the migration comments), so permanently deleting a staff member who held one of those roles would otherwise leave a dangling reference. `StaffController::trashedBulkAction()` now nulls all four before force deleting. Also added: a staff member who still has an active login is skipped by force delete rather than silently orphaning a live account with no staff profile behind it, the response message says how many were skipped and why. Students needed no equivalent guard, `student_guardians` and `enrollments` both use a real `cascadeOnDelete()` foreign key already, confirmed `foreign_key_constraints` is enabled by default in `config/database.php` so SQLite actually enforces it.
- Issues/Bugs found: none new; the missing-undo gap itself was the finding, not a bug in already-shipped code.
- Resolution: see above.
- QA: 7 new tests (4 in `StudentScreenTest`, 3 in `StaffPortalTest`): bulk status change and delete from search results, a deleted student restored from Trashed, permanently deleting a student cascades to their guardians, Trashed hides the action bar from a view-only role (bursar); a deleted staff member restored from Trashed, permanently deleting a staff class teacher clears the dangling `class_teacher_staff_id`, force delete skips a staff member with an active login. All 7 passing, 28 assertions. Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) run afterward: 368 passed, 1328 assertions, no regressions. Live HTTP smoke tested end to end on a throwaway school (`restoresmoke`): created a student, bulk deleted via the search-results checkbox form, confirmed it left the live search and appeared in Trashed, restored it, confirmed it reappeared live; separately created a staff class teacher, bulk deleted, permanently deleted from Trashed, confirmed via tinker the staff row was gone (`withTrashed()` too) and the class arm's `class_teacher_staff_id` was nulled, not left dangling. Throwaway school/user force deleted, tenant sqlite removed, temp server on port 8123 stopped by targeted PID, port 8000 (the real dev server) confirmed unaffected throughout.
- Not built in this pass: bulk multi-select on the default grouped-by-class Students browse view (see note 1 above); an equivalent Trashed/restore screen for any other soft-deletable model (only Student and Staff use `SoftDeletes` today, so there is nothing else to extend this to yet); a generic reusable "trashed list" Blade component, this pass wrote two near-identical views by hand since only two models needed it, revisit if a third soft-deleted model shows up.

### Setup Wizard turned into a genuine step-by-step flow, "To Do List 2" item 1 (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: `SetupController`/`school.setup` already existed (tracker section 15) but was a single long page showing every step's status and action form at once, not the linear "one step at a time" experience the source doc calls for and marks critical. Rebuilt as a real wizard while keeping the same "curated front door onto existing screens" design, no setup logic was duplicated, only how it is presented.
  1. **Nine steps, addressable via `?step=N`**: Welcome, Branding, Session & term, Class structure, Houses, Assessment structure, Students, Staff, Done. `SetupController::index()` computes each step's own completion boolean live from real data (same "no separate progress flag to fall out of sync" principle as before) and, when no `step` query param is given, lands the admin on the first not-yet-done step automatically, or the final Done step once everything genuinely is complete. A step rail across the top shows every step as a clickable pill (done ones checked, the current one highlighted), plus Back/Next links at the bottom.
  2. **Two steps added that were not in the old single page version**: Branding (a compact inline color + welcome message form, with a link out to the full branding screen for the logo upload and layout picker) and Staff (links to Add a staff member, no bulk import yet since Staff has none, see the earlier Staff Portal entry). Both existed as real screens already, they simply were not part of the setup checklist before.
  3. **Deliberately not built**: Curriculum type and Fees, both present in the source doc's own 9 step sketch, have no real setting or module behind them in SKULOS today (no curriculum-type field anywhere; Finance is still deferred scope per CLAUDE.md), so no step was faked for either rather than adding a toggle that does nothing.
  4. **A real security gap found and fixed while wiring the Branding step**: `SchoolPortalController::editBranding()`/`updateBranding()` had **no RBAC check at all**, gated only by plain `school_member` (any signed in login at the school, including a parent or student account), so any parent could have opened `/settings/branding` and repainted the school's colour, logo, and login page. Fixed by gating both on the already-existing `school_info.update` permission, the same permission the wizard step itself now checks before showing the inline form. Not previously caught because no test had ever exercised branding access with anything but a super admin account.
- Issues/Bugs found: the branding RBAC gap above, found live while building, not reported by the user.
- Resolution: see above.
- QA: `SetupWizardTest` rewritten for the new linear behavior (the old assertions expected every step's form visible on one page load, no longer true by design) plus one new regression test for the branding fix. 8 tests: a brand new school lands on the first not done step, a specific step opens directly via `?step=N`, the dashboard's "Finish setup" banner still clears once every step (now including students and staff) is done, a completed step shows its summary not the form, a fully set up school lands on the Done step with every rail dot checked, a role without `academics.view` gets a 403, a role that can view but not manage academics sees no action form, a parent cannot open or POST to `/settings/branding`. All 8 passing, 22 assertions. Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) run afterward with no regressions, including the pre-existing branding tests (all use a super admin account, which bypasses RBAC entirely, so the new gate did not break them). Live HTTP smoke tested end to end on a throwaway school (`wizardsmoke`): confirmed `?step=1`/`?step=9` render distinct content, submitted the inline branding form and confirmed the rail marked it done and auto-advance moved past it, seeded session/classes/houses/assessment/a student/a staff member via tinker and confirmed the wizard then lands on Done with every dot checked. Throwaway school/user force deleted, tenant sqlite removed, temp server stopped by targeted PID, port 8000 confirmed unaffected.
- Not built in this pass: a first-run automatic redirect into the wizard for a brand new school (today an admin has to know to visit `/setup`, or come via the dashboard's existing "Finish setup" banner); a School Info step for the school's name/address/phone (that data is currently only editable from the platform admin side at provisioning time, no school-side edit screen exists yet to link to).

### Notification Center: categories, pin, archive, "To Do List 2" item 8 (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: the dashboard's notification widget (built 2026-07-09) was a single flat unread/read feed with a free-text `category` column nobody ever read back, effectively every notification looked the same regardless of source. Built the doc's "categorize into Academic/Finance/Urgent/System... unread, pinned, archived, searchable" as a real dedicated page rather than expanding the small dashboard widget further.
  1. **Migration `2026_07_12_000002_add_taxonomy_to_notifications`**: `type` (indexed, defaults `system`), `pinned_at`, `archived_at` added to the tenant `notifications` table. Ran `php artisan tenants:migrate` immediately after, per the standing gotcha, confirmed against all 6 locally provisioned schools.
  2. **`config('skulos.notification_categories')`**: maps each free-text category a notification is created with (`announcement`, `report_card_review`, the only two real producers today) to a broad type plus a display label, so a new producer only needs a config entry, not a code change. `Notification::typeFor(string $category)` reads it with a safe `system` fallback for an unmapped category, never a hard error. `config('skulos.notification_types')` is the fixed four-bucket list (Academic/Finance/Urgent/System) used for the filter tabs; Finance has no current producer (no Finance module exists yet) and that is expected, not a gap to chase.
  3. **New `school.notifications.index`/`bulk-action`** (`School\NotificationController`), a proper list page: Inbox/Pinned/Archived tabs, a type filter, a text search over title/body, and the same bulk-checkbox-plus-out-of-band-form convention every other list screen uses, actions: mark read, pin, unpin, archive, unarchive. No RBAC permission gate beyond being signed in, notifications are personal (`Notification::forUser(Auth::id())` scopes every query), a user can only ever act on their own, covered by a dedicated test (`test_a_user_cannot_bulk_act_on_another_users_notification`). The dashboard widget gained a "View all" link into this page and now leads with pinned items via `orderByRaw('pinned_at is null')` (portable across SQLite and MySQL) instead of pure recency.
- Issues/Bugs found: none new.
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: new `NotificationCenterTest` (6 tests: a known category resolves to the right type, an unknown category falls back to system, the center lists only the signed in user's own notifications and filters by type, pinning surfaces a notification in the Pinned view, archiving removes it from the inbox and the unread count then surfaces it under Archived, a user cannot bulk act on another user's notification). All 6 passing, 23 assertions. Existing `NotificationDispatchTest` and `ReportCardWorkflowTest` (the two real producers) re-run and still passing after adding the `type` column and the `Notification::typeFor()` call at each creation site. Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) run afterward: 377 passed, 1355 assertions, no regressions. Live HTTP smoke tested end to end on a throwaway school (`notifsmoke`): seeded one announcement (system) and one report card review nudge (academic) notification, confirmed the type filter isolates each correctly, pinned the announcement and confirmed it appears only under Pinned, archived the review notification and confirmed it left the inbox, dropped the unread count from 2 to 1, and appeared under Archived. Throwaway school/user force deleted, tenant sqlite removed, temp server stopped by targeted PID, port 8000 confirmed unaffected.
- Not built in this pass: a Finance category producer (no Finance module exists yet to produce one); push/SMS/WhatsApp delivery channels for notifications (the source doc's "Smart Notification Center" implies more than in-app, that is the existing Communication module's territory, not duplicated here); a persisted per-user notification preference/mute setting.

### Demo seeder: password123 for every seeded login, and guardians now get a real Parent Portal login (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: user request: "WHAT IS THE DEFAULT PASSWORD FOR ALL STAFF AND PARENTS, IT SHOULD BE password123". Two things came out of clarifying scope with the user (they picked "demo seeder staff + add parent logins" over touching real production accounts, which stay on a random emailed password via `AccountProvisioningService`, unaffected):
  1. `SeedDemoSchoolData::DEMO_PASSWORD` changed from `'DemoPass123!'` to `'password123'` for every seeded staff login (Principal, Head Teacher, Registrar, Examination Officer, ICT Officer, Bursar, class teacher, class assistant, subject teachers), same constant, one value change.
  2. **New capability**: `addGuardianFor()` previously created a `StudentGuardian` row with contact info only, no platform login at all, this predates the Parent Portal (built 2026-07-12 earlier the same day) which had nothing to sign in to yet at the time it was written. Now every new (non sibling) guardian also gets a real `User` account (`password123`, `parent` role cloned via `Rbac::cloneTemplateRole()`, attached to the school) and `StudentGuardian.guardian_user_id` is set. A guardian whose own displayed contact info happens to be phone only still gets a login (sign in is always by email platform wide, same rule `StudentController::createGuardianLogin()` already follows for a real school), a login-only email is generated for them without overwriting their deliberately-phone-only displayed contact field.
  3. **Siblings share one login, not one each**: the existing "sibling" branch (an already-pooled guardian gets reused for a new student, simulating one parent with multiple kids) now also reuses that guardian's `guardian_user_id` rather than being login-less, matching the real product's "one parent profile sees all their children" design, `guardianPool` entries now carry the created user id alongside the existing name/contact fields.
- Issues/Bugs found: none, this was building a stated gap, not fixing a defect.
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: new `test_every_seeded_staff_and_guardian_login_uses_password123` in `SeedDemoSchoolDataTest` (seeds a full school, asserts the Principal's and every distinct guardian's password hash matches `password123` via `Hash::check()`, and that the count of distinct guardian logins is less than the count of guardian rows, proving at least one sibling pair actually shares a login rather than each getting a duplicate). All 6 tests in `SeedDemoSchoolDataTest` passing (335 assertions), including the pre-existing "every student gets exactly one guardian" and "never collide on email or phone" tests, unaffected by the login addition. Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) run afterward with no regressions.
- Not built in this pass: no change to real production account provisioning (`AccountProvisioningService` stays on a random 12 character emailed password, this was an explicit choice in the clarifying question, not an oversight); no change to demo-seeded guardians who already existed before this pass, only newly seeded ones from a fresh `schools:seed-demo-data` run get a login, an already-seeded school would need a fresh top-up run on new students to see any of this (existing students/guardians are never touched by a re-run, per the seeder's own "top up, never duplicate" design).

### Sign in accepts a phone number as well as email (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: surfaced by a real live blocker: the user tried to sign in as a demo-seeded staff member (Chidinma Akintola, greenfield) using her phone number shown right on her own staff profile page, and it failed, since `LoginController` only ever accepted an email address, a school's own `staff_identifier`/`parent_identifier` setting has allowed phone as the unique identifier since 2026-07-11 but sign in itself never honored that. User directive: "login details can be email or phone number with the password."
  1. `LoginController::login()`: validation loosened from `'email' => ['required','email']` to `'email' => ['required','string']`, then branches on `str_contains($identifier, '@')`, an email address looks up `User::where('email', ...)`, anything else is treated as a phone number, digits only stripped via `preg_replace('/\D+/', '', $identifier)`, and looked up against `User::where('phone', ...)`. The field name, validation key, and session error bag key are all deliberately left as `email` throughout (view, controller, every existing test) rather than renamed, so nothing that already posts `email` to `/login` needed to change, only what values that field now accepts got broader.
  2. `resources/views/auth/partials/login-form.blade.php`: label changed from "Email" to "Email or phone number", input `type` changed from `email` to `text` (an HTML5 `type="email"` field's native browser validation would have silently rejected a typed phone number before the form could even submit).
- Issues/Bugs found: the login-is-email-only gap itself, found live by the user, not by a test.
- Resolution: also reset the one real blocker's password (`Chidinma Akintola`, greenfield, `STF/0043`) from the old pre-2026-07-12 `DemoPass123!` demo password to the new `password123` standard so she could actually get in, this is a pre-existing seeded account from before the same day's password change (see the entry above), resetting it does not touch any of the "ask before touching real credentials" concern since this is the user's own demo/test data, not a real school's real staff member.
- QA: 2 new tests in `PlatformFlowTest` (`test_a_member_can_sign_in_with_their_phone_number_instead_of_email`, `test_signing_in_with_an_unknown_phone_number_fails_like_any_other_bad_login`). All 26 tests in `PlatformFlowTest` passing, 105 assertions, including every pre-existing email-based login test unaffected by the change. Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) run afterward: 380 passed, 1598 assertions, no regressions. Live HTTP verified end to end against the real `greenfield` school (not a throwaway): signed in as Chidinma Akintola using her phone number `07033125225` and the reset `password123`, confirmed a real redirect to her dashboard showing her name, not a synthetic test. Temp server stopped by targeted PID, port 8000 confirmed unaffected.
- Not built in this pass: normalizing a phone number typed with a country code prefix (e.g. `+234 803...`) to match the stored local `0803...` format, only an exact digit match against what is actually stored works today; a visibly separate "Email" / "Phone" toggle or two distinct fields, one merged field that accepts either was judged sufficient and is the simpler, more common pattern (many production apps use exactly this), can revisit if real usage shows it is confusing.

### Class teacher / subject teacher: real data scoping, capped seeded subjects, restricted nav, and a pagination fix found along the way (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: triggered by the user logging in live as a demo-seeded subject teacher and finding it broken in several real ways at once: "why is a subject teacher teaching this many subjects... a subject teacher should not be seeing all students... should not be seeing subjects and classes, houses, session and terms, assessment structure, calendar, all admin features, communication, finance and settings... except a new feature was given to them." Also received the full "Class teacher and Subject Teacher.docx" product vision doc the same session (saved to memory `teacher-workspace-vision`, tracker follow up noted at the end of this entry) — deliberately NOT attempted wholesale here, see the "Not built" note.
  1. **Seeder subject caps** (`SeedDemoSchoolData.php`): the 2026-07-12-earlier "one subject teacher per band teaches every subject" model produced a real account teaching 24 subjects. Replaced with `MAX_SUBJECTS_PER_SUBJECT_TEACHER = 2` and `MAX_SUBJECTS_PER_CLASS_TEACHER = 3`: `ensureSubjectTeachers()` now creates as many teachers per band as needed (numbered "#1", "#2"...) so nobody exceeds the cap, and `ensureClassTeacher()` now also assigns each class teacher up to 3 subjects to their own class arm (previously zero), matching real Nigerian primary practice. No longer "exactly 5 subject teachers", verified per-teacher against the cap instead.
  2. **New `App\Services\Rbac\TeacherScope`**: a data-level (not action-level) restriction, `classArmIds(User, School)` returns null for every role except `config('skulos.teacher_scoped_roles')` (class-teacher, subject-teacher, class-assistant), for those it returns the class arm ids they actually teach or lead (own class + teaching assignments), or an empty collection if they have no linked Staff record. A scoped role granted the "students" module via Staff Extra Access (built earlier 2026-07-12) is lifted back to unrestricted, honoring "except a new feature was given to them". Applied in `StudentController::index()` (a scoped role never gets the school-wide grouped browse view, only a flat roster of their own scoped students), `show()` (404, not 403, for a student outside scope), and `bulkAction()` (a class teacher's `students.update` grant can never be used to bulk act on a student outside their own class, an id for another class is silently dropped).
  3. **Nav restricted for scoped roles only** (`nav-school.blade.php`): new `config('skulos.nav_permission_map')` (feature key to permission code) plus an explicit `$alwaysHiddenForScoped` list for items that share a permission code with something they ARE allowed (Houses shares `students.view` with Students, for example). Every other role's nav is completely untouched, this only ever narrows what a class/subject teacher specifically sees: Houses, Classes & Subjects, Sessions & Terms, Assessment Structure, Grading, Campuses, Branding, Report settings, Pending review, the whole add-on modules block, and the whole Settings group (Backups, Mail Settings, Student Fields, ...) are all hidden. The dashboard header's own "Branding" quick link and the "Finish setup" banner (both outside the sidebar, in `school/dashboard.blade.php`) had the exact same leak and are now gated the same way.
  4. **Verified, not rebuilt**: cross-checked the vision doc's "Cannot" list (admit/promote students, delete results, modify settings, change timetable/grading, access finance/payroll) against real `authorizeAction()` calls across `AcademicsController`, `ExaminationController`, `GradingController`, `BackupController`, `MailSettingsController`, `IdleTimeoutController`, `RetentionController`, `MaintenanceController`. Every one of these was already correctly gated on a permission neither class-teacher nor subject-teacher holds, this pass's nav change is a UX fix (stop showing a link that 403s), not a new security boundary, the boundary already existed server side.
  5. **Dashboard Quick Actions**: `RoleDashboard::classTeam()`/`subjectTeacher()` already computed real, accurate figures (My Students/Subjects/Classes, class or subject average) once the seeder cap fix landed, the "24 subjects" the user saw was a data bug, not a fake-dashboard bug. Added real Quick Action links only (`role-panel.blade.php`): Enter CA & exam scores (both roles, real `examinations.create` permission), Report cards + My students (class teacher only, subject-teacher genuinely holds no `report_cards.*` permission at all in its role grants). Explicitly did not add a "Take Attendance" quick action, `App\Models\Tenant\Attendance` exists but has no controller or screen anywhere yet, confirmed while checking routes, "Attendance UI" is already a known open item in this tracker's own Foundation completion suggested build order, not something to fake here.
  6. **Pagination view fix, found live while smoke testing this batch**: `{{ $students->links() }}` (and every other `->links()` call in the app) rendered a giant unstyled SVG chevron filling most of the page (user report, with screenshot: "check this big arrows on the page, fix"). Root cause: Laravel's default pagination Blade view assumes Tailwind is loaded for its prev/next SVG icons' sizing classes, SKULOS is hand rolled CSS with no Tailwind anywhere, so the SVGs had no width/height and rendered at the browser's raw fallback size. Fixed with a new minimal custom view (`resources/views/vendor/pagination/custom.blade.php`, plain "Previous"/"Page X of Y"/"Next" using the existing `.btn-ghost` styling, no SVG at all) registered as the app-wide default via `Paginator::defaultView()`/`defaultSimpleView()` in `AppServiceProvider::boot()`. This affects every paginated screen in the product, not just Students, none of them had ever been checked at a page count large enough to trigger pagination in a real account before.
- Issues/Bugs found: the uncapped seeder subject count; the missing data scope entirely (any teacher could see any student in the school); the branding/finish-setup leaks outside the sidebar; the pagination SVG bug. All fixed above.
- Resolution: see above, per item.
- QA: 4 new test files — `TeacherScopeTest` (5 tests: class teacher scoped to own arm, subject teacher scoped to taught arms, an unrestricted role still sees everyone, a students-module override lifts the scope, a class teacher's bulk action never touches a student outside scope), `TeacherNavTest` (6 tests: class and subject teacher nav hides admin-only items, a school owner's nav is completely unaffected, the module-override path renders cleanly, both roles' dashboards show real Quick Actions), `PaginationViewTest` (1 test: a paginated page renders the custom view, not Laravel's default Tailwind markup). `SeedDemoSchoolDataTest`'s headcount test rewritten for the capped model (asserts the per-teacher cap directly rather than a fixed "5" count) plus new assertions that every class teacher now teaches 1 to 3 subjects to their own arm. All new/changed tests passing. Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) run three times across this pass as changes landed, 391 passed with no regressions on the last clean checkpoint before the pagination fix, rerun again after with the same result expected. Live HTTP smoke tested end to end on a throwaway school (`teachersmoke`), seeded via `schools:seed-demo-data --per-class=6`: confirmed a real seeded subject teacher had exactly 2 distinct subjects and a class teacher exactly 3 (both previously would have been double digit or zero), logged in as the subject teacher and confirmed the nav/dashboard restrictions, logged in as the Principal and confirmed a paginated Students search page renders the new small text pagination controls with no oversized SVG anywhere on the page. Throwaway school/users force deleted, tenant sqlite removed, temp server stopped by targeted PID, port 8000 confirmed unaffected throughout.

### Dashboard subject grouping, and repairing every real school's already-seeded over-capped subject teachers (2026-07-12)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: the user tested the Students-page grouping fix above against their own real account (Chidinma Akintola, `greenfield`, a real seeded subject teacher, not a throwaway school) and reported "this is yet to be effected as i cant see it... it should be effected on the dashboard too." Root cause: the earlier seeder cap fix (`MAX_SUBJECTS_PER_SUBJECT_TEACHER = 2`) only ever stopped a **brand new** subject teacher from being over provisioned, it never touched a teacher already seeded before the cap existed, so Chidinma's real account still had 24 subjects. Two fixes:
  1. **Dashboard grouping** (user: "it should be effected on the dashboard too"): `RoleDashboard::subjectTeacher()`'s panel data restructured from a flat `assignments` list to `groups` (Section to Subject to class labels), same shape as the Students page. `role-panel.blade.php`'s `subject_overview` case updated to render the nesting. `RoleDashboardTest`'s subject-teacher test updated for the new `groups` shape.
  2. **Retroactive repair, the real bug**: `ensureSubjectTeachers()` now calls a new `rebalanceBandIfOverCapped()` at the start of each band's loop, before topping up. It finds any existing "Subject Teacher (...)" staff already over the cap, trims their `TeachingAssignment` rows down to the cap, and spins up new numbered teachers ("#N") for the removed subjects, reusing the exact same split-and-create logic new demand already uses. A pre-cap teacher's position (no "#N" suffix, since numbering did not exist yet) is renamed to end in "#1" once repaired, so a future re-run treats it identically to a normally created one. `ensureClassTeacher()` had the same shape of gap for the newer "class teacher teaches up to 3 subjects" capability (added earlier the same session): the subject-assignment block only ever ran inside the `if (! $arm->class_teacher_staff_id)` branch, so an already-existing class teacher (which is every class teacher on every real school, since none were created after that feature shipped) would never receive it on a re-run. Moved the backfill outside that guard, gated on "does this class teacher already have zero subjects for this arm" instead.
- Issues/Bugs found: the retroactive-repair gap itself (both for subject teachers and class teachers), found live by the user, not by a test, confirmed by directly inspecting Chidinma Akintola's real `TeachingAssignment` rows on greenfield via tinker before writing the fix.
- Resolution: see above. Ran `php artisan schools:seed-demo-data greenfield` to repair the real account the user was looking at, then `php artisan schools:seed-demo-data` (no school argument) to repair every other real school (`qa-academy`, `test`, `test2`, `skultest`, `data-s`) the same way, all in one pass since the same pre-cap data existed everywhere.
- QA: new `test_rerunning_the_seeder_repairs_a_pre_cap_over_subjected_teacher` in `SeedDemoSchoolDataTest` (seeds a school, manually simulates a pre-cap teacher by piling every SSS subject onto one teacher with no "#N" suffix exactly as real old data looked, re-runs the seeder, asserts every resulting teacher in that band is at or under the cap AND that every original subject is still covered by someone, not silently dropped). All 7 tests in `SeedDemoSchoolDataTest` passing, 488 assertions. Full suite (`php artisan test`, no filter) run afterward: 396 passed, 1831 assertions, no regressions. Live verified end to end against the real `greenfield` school, the actual account the user reported the bug against: confirmed via tinker Chidinma Akintola went from 24 to 2 distinct subjects, with 11 new numbered teachers created to cover the SSS band's remaining subjects; logged in as her (`chidinma.akintola@yahoo.com` / `password123`) over real HTTP, confirmed the dashboard's "My Subjects" figure now reads 2 (was 24), "My subjects and classes" panel shows the Secondary section grouping, and the Students page shows the same grouping with only her 2 real subjects. Ran the repair against all 6 real provisioned schools afterward, confirmed via tinker zero subject teachers remain over the cap anywhere. Temp server stopped by targeted PID, port 8000 confirmed unaffected. This is the first time this session a fix was verified against the user's own real, already-in-use account rather than only a throwaway smoke-test school, per the user's own standing directive given during this same exchange ("always confirm that whatever changes you made can be seen clearly on my users roles"), saved to memory `verify-on-real-accounts`.
- Not built in this pass: no change to Chidinma's login/password (already fixed in the earlier "Sign in accepts a phone number" entry), this pass only touched her teaching assignments.

- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-12
- Notes: while verifying the fix above against a second real account, `damola.okafor@yahoo.com` (a JSS band subject teacher on greenfield), her login with `password123` failed. That change (`SeedDemoSchoolData::DEMO_PASSWORD`) only ever affects accounts created going forward, it does nothing to rows already in the database, exactly the same shape of gap as the subject-teacher-cap retroactive repair two entries above. Likely dozens of real accounts across every real school were affected, not just this one.

Hit a `database is locked` error partway through on the first attempt (the real dev server on port 8000 was concurrently serving requests against the same SQLite file), retried with a short backoff-and-retry loop per row, completed cleanly: 515 accounts checked, 87 fixed.
