### Foundation Finance slice: Bank Transfer, Evidence Upload, Verification, Basic Fee Records (2026-07-17)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-17
- Notes: user picked "Foundation Finance slice" from an explicit menu of what to build next once the "ALL THERE" backlog closed out. Confirmed scope against the tracker's own catalog before writing any code: this is "a deliberately minimal slice, not the full deferred Finance module from section 5b" (fees, discounts, scholarships, installment plans, automatic invoicing, expenses, income, payroll, budgets, ledger, payment gateway integration, refunds, financial reports, all still out of scope), exactly the 4 named catalog features (`bank_transfer_payment_option`, `payment_evidence_upload`, `payment_verification`, `basic_fee_records`). Completely greenfield: no Fee/Payment/Invoice/Transaction/Receipt model, migration, controller, or route existed anywhere in the codebase before this.
- Resolution: two new tenant tables (`fee_items`: a school's own basic fee catalog per term, name + amount + term; `payments`: one bank transfer submission against one student, evidence_path only ever a storage path per the standing "never file bytes in the database" rule, status pending/verified/rejected, submitted_by_user_id/verified_by_user_id are plain `unsignedBigInteger` columns referencing a platform user with no cross database FK, the same pattern `notifications.user_id`/`chat_messages.sender_user_id` already use, not a real foreign key). New `App\Models\Tenant\FeeItem`/`Payment`, both `Auditable` (automatic create/update/delete audit trail, no manual `Recorder::record()` calls needed in the controllers). New `FeeItemController` (basic fee catalog CRUD, retire instead of delete once a fee item has real payments against it) and `PaymentController` (submit, view, verify, reject), both gated on the standard `finance.*` permission set RbacSeeder already auto-generates for every module key in `config('skulos.modules')`. A guardian/student can only ever submit or view their own family's payments (`FamilyScope::studentIds()`, the exact same scoping pattern Students/Assignments already use), a crafted student_id outside that set 404s rather than silently redirecting. Evidence upload: an image gets the existing `ImageProcessingService::toWebp()` treatment (resize + compress, matching the standing rule), a PDF (many Nigerian bank apps generate a PDF receipt, not a screenshot) is stored as-is since it cannot be meaningfully "converted to WebP"; a new `config('skulos.upload_limits.payment_evidence_kb')` entry (3072, the first entry in a brand new config section, this is the first real upload feature in the product, so the very first thing the standing "bake size limits into config, admin adjustable, not hardcoded" rule has ever applied to) caps it. Verifying or rejecting notifies the submitter via a real in-app `Notification` row (new `payment_submitted`/`payment_verified`/`payment_rejected` categories, `finance` notification type, already existed as a type key with nothing producing it until now); submitting one notifies the school's Bursar/Accountant/leadership roles the same way. Bursar/Accountant moved off the `peopleOnly()` "nothing built yet" dashboard placeholder onto a real `financeOverview()` (Pending Payments count, Verified This Term real collected total, a quick link straight to the pending queue when there is one). All 4 catalog features wired into `nav-school.blade.php`: the 3 "same real capability" keys collapse into one "Payments" nav line (the same merge pattern already used for Results/Report Cards and Staff/Teacher Portal), `basic_fee_records` gets its own "Fee Records" line.
- Issues/Bugs found, a real, pre-existing production gap, not new code: `Rbac::check()` denies a `{module}.*` permission whenever `Rbac::moduleState()` reports that module disabled, and `moduleState()`'s only source of "enabled" was an explicit `module_states` database row, which is only ever written when a platform admin visits Billing and manually clicks "assign plan" for a school (`BillingController::assignPlan()`). A school that never went through that one specific admin action, which is every school provisioned through the self-service trial approval flow and every factory-created school in this whole test suite, has zero `module_states` rows, so every non-core module (Finance among them) silently read as disabled regardless of what its Plan's `modules` array actually promised, a real conflict where the nav correctly shows "Payments" (`School::hasFeature()` already has its own, separate, correct plan fallback) but clicking it would 403. This has been latent and harmless since the day the module-toggle system was built, because no non-core module ever had a real permission-gated screen behind it before, Library/Hostel/Transport/Medical are all still "Soon" everywhere; Finance is the first one to actually exercise this path. Fixed at the source: `Rbac::moduleState()` now falls back to the school's own Plan (or the platform default plan, same null-coalesce `School::hasFeature()` already uses) before falling to the module's bare `is_core` default, so a school actually gets what its plan promises without needing a platform admin to have separately, manually flipped a switch for it. Confirmed the fix does not regress the existing, deliberately-manual module toggle behavior `RbacTest` already covers (a school with no plan at all still correctly defaults every non-core module to disabled until explicitly toggled, since `moduleState()`'s new plan-lookup only ever adds an "enabled" answer, never removes the existing manual-override precedence chain above it, user > role > school > plan > catalog default).
- Also fixed as part of the above: `PlanSeeder` uses `Plan::firstOrCreate()`, so adding `finance` to Foundation/Advance's `modules` array in the seeder file never reached the already-seeded Foundation/Advance `Plan` rows in the local dev platform database on its own (the exact same shape of gotcha as the two already documented in CLAUDE.md for tenant migrations and `extra_permissions`, just for `Plan.modules` specifically); patched the two existing rows directly, and additively resynced `finance.*`/`finance.view`/`finance.create` onto every already-cloned Bursar/Accountant/Parent/Student/leadership role across every already-provisioned local school (`syncWithoutDetaching`, never a destructive full `sync()`), plus ran `tenants:migrate` for the two new tables.
- QA: 12 tests in new `FinanceTest.php` (fee catalog CRUD by a Bursar; a role without `finance.*` gets 403; **a school on a real Plan that includes `finance`, with no explicit module toggle ever called, can actually reach the screen** — the test that directly proves the Rbac fix, plus its inverse, a plan without `finance` staying blocked; a parent submits with evidence, image converted to WebP; a PDF stored as-is, not converted; a parent cannot submit for another guardian's child, 404; a Bursar verifies a pending payment and the submitting parent is notified; a Bursar rejects with a note; an already-decided payment cannot be decided again; a parent only ever sees their own family's payments, not another's; the Bursar dashboard shows real pending count and real verified total, not fabricated figures). 1 new nav test confirms all 4 catalog features render as real links, not "Soon", and the pages behind them actually load. Full backend suite run as the final checkpoint before commit, `RbacTest`'s existing module-toggle assertions specifically re-verified unaffected by the `moduleState()` change.
- Not built in this pass: everything explicitly still in the full deferred Finance module (section 5b) or the separate Advance/Professional-tier Finance catalog entries (Installment Payment Plans, Online Payment Gateway Integration, School Fees Management, Discounts & Scholarships, Expense/Income Management, Payroll, Financial Reports/Dashboard, Automated Receipts/Invoicing, Outstanding Fee Tracking); no per-student running balance/ledger view (this slice is submission + verification, not a full accounts-receivable system); no admin settings screen for the new `payment_evidence_kb` upload limit (lives in config only for this first pass, matching the standing rule's own "bake into config" first step, same as it was written); Fee Records has no per-section/per-class scoping, a fee item applies school wide for the term it belongs to.
- Found during the full-suite checkpoint: two pre-existing `RoleManagementTest` tests used `finance.view` specifically as their example of "a permission with no real screen behind it, not on the curated create-role/extra-access checkbox list" (`config('skulos.staff_extra_access_modules')`, a separate, smaller curated catalog than the RBAC `modules` list, reused by both the role creation checkboxes and the Staff extra-access screen). Now that Finance has a real screen, both premises were simultaneously wrong: one crashed outright (`UNIQUE constraint failed` attaching `finance.view` to a Bursar role that now already holds it via its own template grant), the other's assertion direction was no longer correct either. Added `'finance'` to `staff_extra_access_modules` (matching the exact precedent set for `'assignments'` on 2026-07-15, "extend this list as more modules get real screens"), and swapped both tests' "not on the curated list" example to `library.view` (still genuinely unbuilt), rather than leaving Finance permission checkboxes missing from role creation/extra-access, which would have been a real, separate gap for a Bursar/Accountant custom role.
- Also found during the same checkpoint: `RoleDashboardTest::test_roles_with_partial_real_data_see_student_counts_not_a_blank_state` looped over `['bursar', 'accountant', 'librarian', 'nurse', 'hostel-master', 'transport-officer']` asserting every one of them shows the `people_only` placeholder with a real "Students" figure, an assertion that was deliberately no longer true for Bursar/Accountant now that they get a real `financeOverview()` instead. Removed Bursar/Accountant from that loop (the other 4 roles unaffected, their modules genuinely still have no screen), their own dashboard is covered by `FinanceTest::test_the_bursar_dashboard_shows_real_pending_and_verified_figures` instead.

### Platform subscription billing: countdown, reminders, extensions, demand payment (2026-07-17)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-17
- Notes: user request, verbatim: "add a countdown subscription... for school owners, head teacher, asst headteacher, principal and vice principal and school admins to see when next the subscription will be due and the amount due (number of current learner determines this multiply by the cost of the plan) - the platform owner also sees all these details too and can trigger reminder to schools for payment, add extension and demand payment. the pop for the payment renewal pops up on the final 21 days to the due date daily", followed by "add similar payment options for the schools to pay as we have for parents" (the Foundation Finance slice's bank transfer + evidence + verification flow, mirrored here for a school paying SKULOS itself instead of a parent paying a school) and "add billing plan to all the schools we have now". Three clarifying questions asked before building (real bank details vs. placeholder; whether the popup interrupts all 5 roles daily or just the Owner; what "demand payment" should concretely restrict): user answered real bank details directly (account 0036905082, GTBank, Michael Oluwaseyi Adeniran, not fabricated), daily popup for all 5 roles, and "full lockout until resolved" for demand payment.
- Resolution: `schools.next_billing_date` already existed (a platform admin's manually-typed date, added 2026-07-10, never wired to anything beyond the admin's own edit screen); this is everything that was missing around it. New `SubscriptionService` (`amountDue()` = real active learner count, read from the school's own tenant database via `TenantManager::connectTo()`, × the school's plan price, falling back to the default plan the same way `School::hasFeature()` already does; `daysUntilDue()`; `isWithinReminderWindow()` = the final 21 days through any day overdue). New platform-scoped `SubscriptionPayment` (real foreign keys to `schools`/`users`, unlike the tenant-side `Payment` model, since both already live in the platform database) and `SubscriptionEvent` (one row per reminder/extension/demand/reinstated action, a real audit trail behind the countdown UI). School side: `school.subscription.index`, visible only to `config('skulos.subscription_visible_role_slugs')` (`school-owner`, `principal`, `head-teacher`, `vice-principal`, `school-admin`, a direct role-slug check, not an RBAC permission, since every school on every plan has a subscription, this is not a toggleable feature; no separate "Assistant Head Teacher" role exists, Vice Principal already is that role under its secondary-school naming, the same pairing `RoleDashboard`'s own leadership bucket already treats as parallel titles, not additional ones), shows plan/learners/amount due/due date, the real bank details (`SubscriptionBankService`, config seeded with the real account then admin-editable, same `PlatformSetting`-override-wins pattern as `SupportContactService`), a submission form (image evidence compressed to WebP, PDF stored as-is, same pattern as the Foundation Finance slice), and payment history. Daily popup: computed once in `layouts/app.blade.php` (role + window check, real data only, never shown at all if outside the window or the wrong role), rendered via new `subscription-popup.blade.php` (mirrors the existing `broadcast-popup.blade.php` extraction pattern), shown once per day per school per browser via a `localStorage` key dated `YYYY-MM-DD`, so it reappears the next calendar day without needing a server round trip to track dismissal. Platform side: new `admin.subscriptions.index` (every provisioned school, real learners/amount due/days left/status at a glance) and `admin.subscriptions.show` (verify/reject payments, send reminder, grant an extension in days, demand payment, reinstate, a real activity log). Demand payment reuses the pre-existing `School::STATUS_SUSPENDED` + `ResolveTenant` 403 block (already fully built and tested, "full lockout" already existed as a mechanism for the unrelated manual "Suspend" action) rather than inventing a second, parallel lock system; a new `suspension_reason` column (`School::SUSPENSION_REASON_BILLING`) distinguishes the two so the 403 message can actually explain why and what to do ("subscription is overdue, contact your school administrator") instead of the pre-existing generic "contact your administrator" message, which now only shows for the original manual-suspend path.
- Issues/Bugs found: none new in the shipped code; this feature reused the exact `Rbac::moduleState()` plan-fallback fix from the Finance slice above (already in place) rather than hitting the same gap twice, since `finance.*` grants are not involved here at all (the subscription screen gates by role slug, not RBAC permission).
- Also done per the user's explicit instruction: "add billing plan to all the schools we have now" — every real provisioned local school that had `plan_id = null` (7 of 8) assigned to Foundation, mirroring `BillingController::assignPlan()`'s own module-state sync, not just a raw column update.
- QA: 15 tests in new `SubscriptionTest.php` (a school owner sees the real amount due, active learners × plan price; a teacher (not one of the 5 named roles) gets 403; a school owner submits a payment with evidence, image converted to WebP; a platform admin sees every school's real amount due on the index; verify/reject a payment, the school notified either way; send a reminder, a real event logged and the school notified; grant an extension, the due date actually moves and the event records the note; demand payment locks the school out with the billing specific 403 message; reinstate restores access; a school manually suspended for an unrelated reason still shows the old generic message, not the billing one, proving the two paths stay genuinely distinct; reinstate refuses a school suspended for a different reason, 404, so it can never accidentally un-suspend something demand payment did not itself lock; the bank details save and then actually appear on the school's own page; the daily popup renders within the 21 day window and stays absent outside it). Full related suites (`TenancyTest`, `PlanFeatureNavTest`, `RbacTest`, `ManageModulesTest`, 47 tests) and the full backend suite both re-run clean.
- Not built in this pass: no email delivery alongside the in-app notifications for reminders/verification/lockout (the Foundation Finance slice's parent-facing flow does not send email either, in-app only for both, consistent); no self-serve "download my subscription invoice/receipt" (matches the standing "never store generated PDFs forever" rule, nothing to build differently here yet, no receipt generation exists in the product at all); the bank details screen has no logo image upload, "use their logo" was answered with real account details but no actual GTBank logo asset file was supplied, and improvising or sourcing a trademarked bank logo was judged out of scope for this pass, the bank name renders as clear text instead.

### Birthday & Anniversary Notifications (2026-07-16)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-16
- Notes: part of the "ALL THERE" Reports & Analytics backlog above. The dashboard already had a passive "Birthdays" widget (students only, nothing ever pushed to anyone); this is a genuinely different thing, real `Notification` rows created the moment a student's or staff member's birthday, or a staff member's work anniversary, actually falls on today.
- Resolution: new `skulos:notify-birthdays-anniversaries {school?}` command, scheduled `dailyAt('06:00')` in `routes/console.php`. Loops every provisioned school; for a student birthday, notifies the school's leadership (`config('skulos.leadership_role_slugs')`) plus that student's own class teacher; for a staff birthday or work anniversary, leadership only. Idempotent via `notifyOnce()` (checked per user+category+title+day before creating), safe to re-run or re-schedule on the same day without duplicating. Added `birthday`/`anniversary` entries to `config('skulos.notification_categories')`.
- Issues/Bugs found: two real bugs, both in Carbon 3.x (confirmed installed, `nesbot/carbon: ^3.8.4`), not in this command's own logic. (1) `diffInYears()`'s default `$absolute` behaviour changed from Carbon 2's always-positive result to signed: `$today->diffInYears($pastDate)` now returns a *negative* number for a birthday/anniversary that already happened this year, silently breaking the `if ($years < 1) continue` anniversary guard (a hire 3 years ago was computed as -3, which is less than 1, so the anniversary was silently never created) and producing text like "turns -10 today" for a birthday. Fixed by passing `true` explicitly as the second argument on all 3 call sites. (2) less obvious, found only once the first fix was in and a test still failed: Carbon 3's `diffInYears()` also stopped returning a whole number by default, `3 years ago` now diffs to `3.0016285148063` (a real fractional value from the sub second gap between the record's stored date and the moment `now()` is called), so the anniversary body read "marks 3.0016285148063 year(s) with..." instead of "marks 3 year(s)". Fixed by wrapping all 3 calls in `(int)`. Both fixes needed together; the sign fix alone was not sufficient. This is a new, previously undiscovered class of bug for this codebase given Carbon 3 is now confirmed installed, worth checking any other `diffIn*` call that assumes Carbon 2 semantics.
- Also found: a weak test assertion that let bug (1) ship past the birthday half undetected: `assertStringContainsString('10', $note->body)` would incorrectly pass even for "turns -10 today" (still contains the substring '10'), and separately would have passed for the fractional "turns 10.0016285148063" too. Tightened to `assertStringContainsString('turns 10', $note->body)`.
- Also found and cleaned up, unrelated to this feature: while tinkering to isolate the Carbon bug, several throwaway debug schools (`bdaydebug`, `bdaydebug3`, `figdebug`) were created directly via tinker/artisan calls whose SQLite files never persisted; the orphaned `School` rows (still marked `provisioned_at`) broke the real `/admin/broadcast` platform admin page, which loops every provisioned school and hit `Database file... does not exist` on the first missing one. Force deleted the 3 orphaned rows (no real school data existed for them, only broken references) and confirmed every remaining provisioned school's tenant file exists.
- QA: 5 tests in new `BirthdayAnniversaryNotificationsTest.php` (a student birthday notifies leadership and their own class teacher, with the exact age asserted; a staff birthday and work anniversary both notify leadership with the exact age/years asserted; hired today this year is correctly not yet an anniversary; running the command twice in one day does not duplicate; nobody with a birthday/anniversary today gets nothing). Full backend suite (672 tests, 0 failures) confirmed green with this command included.
- Not built in this pass: no in-app UI to configure which roles count as "leadership" for this feature specifically, it reuses the existing platform wide `leadership_role_slugs` config catalog already used elsewhere.

### Nav search box (2026-07-16)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-16
- Notes: user directive, repeated three times across the same feedback batch: "LETS ALSO ADD A SEARCH BOX ABOVE THE MENU BAR WHERE ONE CAN SEARCH FOR MENUS AND FEATURES TO EASILY LOCATE THE FEATURE/MENU ONE IS LOOKING FOR".
- Resolution: a plain text `<input type="search" id="navSearchInput">` added to `layouts/app.blade.php`, sitting between the school name/badge block and `<nav class="side-nav">` (above the menu, as asked), with a small search glyph (new `search` case added to the shared `layouts/partials/icon.blade.php`). Filtering is a client side vanilla JS listener on `input`, no server round trip, no framework: it precomputes each `.side-link`'s visible label text once on load, then on every keystroke shows/hides links whose label contains the typed text (case insensitive substring). Written once to work identically for both portals despite their different markup shapes: the school portal's collapsible grouped nav (`.side-section`/`.side-submenu`, from `nav-school.blade.php`) auto opens any group with a match and hides a group with none; the platform admin portal's flatter list (`.side-group` plain divider labels, from `nav-admin.blade.php`, no collapse/expand at all) walks each divider's following siblings up to the next divider to decide whether to hide it, since there is no wrapping element per group there to toggle instead. A "No menus or features match your search" message shows when a query matches nothing, so an empty sidebar never reads as broken. Clearing the box restores every link/group to its normal shown state (whatever accordion state a group happens to be in already, untouched, so a group the search forced open stays open until the user manually collapses it again, consistent with how the existing accordion toggle already behaves).
- Issues/Bugs found: the same "raw response body" trap flagged earlier this same session with a CSS comment now hit a *test*, not shipped copy. `test_settings_group_always_renders_last` scanned the raw HTML for any further occurrence of the bare substring `side-section` after the Settings group's position, to prove no other nav group renders after it. The new script's own functional code legitimately calls `querySelectorAll('.side-section')` (a real CSS selector, needed for the group-aware filtering above) inside a `<script>` tag that renders near the end of the page, after Settings, which the bare substring scan flagged as a phantom violation. Fixed by tightening the test's own assertion to look for the actual rendered element pattern `class="side-section"` instead of the bare word, since that is what the test's own comment already said its intent was ("must be the very last side-section", i.e. no more `<div class="side-section">` elements, not "the text side-section may never appear again anywhere on the page").
- QA: 2 new tests in `PlanFeatureNavTest.php` (the search input renders, and renders strictly above `<nav class="side-nav">`'s opening tag, not inside or below it; the same input also renders on the platform admin portal, logged in as a real super admin visiting `/admin`, confirming the shared `app.blade.php` placement works for both `$portal` values, not just `school`). Full `PlanFeatureNavTest`/`TeacherNavTest` suite (25 tests) and the full backend suite (672 tests) both pass.
- Not built in this pass: no server side "jump to page" beyond simply clicking the now-visible filtered link (i.e. no keyboard up/down + Enter navigation), and no fuzzy/typo tolerant matching, a plain substring match was judged sufficient for a short, mostly-single-word nav label set; revisit only if a future report says exact substring matching is too strict.

### Admin dashboard figure cards: real fixes from a live school's screenshots (2026-07-16)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-16
- Notes: a real school's actual dashboard, screenshotted directly, surfaced a cluster of genuine issues in one pass: "ALL THE TEXT LOOKS FADED, FIX THEM SO THAT THEY APPEAR SHARP IN COLOUR BLACK"; "INSTEAD OF FIRST TWO ROWS, MAKE IT ONE ROW AND THE SHOW MORE BUTTON"; "FIX THE Attendance Rate AND Staff Attendance SHOWING n/A - ADD THE DATA IF NEEDED"; "WHAT IS THE MEANING OF AWAITING CLASS, CHANGE THE DATA THERE"; "WHY DO WE HAVE STAFF AND TEACHERS DATA... MERGE INTO ONE AS STAFF"; "MAKE EACH CARD CLICKABLE THAT IT TAKES ONE TO THE ACTUAL PAGE"; then a second screenshot of the "modern" dashboard layout specifically: "THE MODERN DASHBOARD LOOKS IS POORLY DESIGN WITH MANY ERRORS - FIX" plus "THERE SHOULD BE MORE ITEMS UNDER THE PANEL CHECKLIST"; then a third, of the calendar badges again after the earlier dark mode fix: "THIS DESIGN ISSUES WAS NOT FIXED".
- Resolution, one per report: (1) `.fig b` had no explicit `color`, `.fig span` used `--mut-dim` (the dimmer of the two muted tones); both now set explicitly, `b` to `var(--ink)`, the label to the less-dim `var(--mut)`. (2) the "show past the first N, reveal the rest" threshold was `$i >= 8` (2 complete rows of 4); changed to `$i >= 4` (1 row), matching the button's own `count($figs) > 4` trigger. (3) `RoleDashboard::attendanceRate()` used to require a mark on literally today or return null; now looks back up to 7 days for the most recent day that actually has any marks, so a real but slightly stale rate shows instead of a bare "N/A" the moment nobody has opened the register yet today. Checked the real school's own data directly: it had 20 real student marks (most recent 3 days old, now correctly surfaces) and zero staff marks ever (genuinely no data to surface, "N/A" there is honest, not a bug, fabricating staff attendance marks for a real school would violate the "no dummy data" rule, flagged to the user rather than invented). (4) "Awaiting Class" (students not yet placed in a class arm, a real but niche admin metric that read as unclear) replaced with "Active Assignments" (`Assignment` rows with no due date or a future one), immediately self explanatory and ties into the Assignments feature moved under Academic earlier this session. (5) "Teachers" (a subset count) dropped entirely, "Staff" is the one remaining card, same merge idea already applied to the nav/portal links earlier this session now applied to this card set too; `RoleDashboard::teacherCount()` deleted outright once its only caller was gone, no other real usage. (6) every admin figure now carries a real `href` (computed once via `route()`, reusing the school slug already in scope) to the actual screen it summarises; the template overlays a transparent, full cover `<a class="fig-link">` inside each card rather than changing the card's own tag, so none of the 4 layout templates' existing markup needed restructuring. (7) the "modern" layout's ring was a `conic-gradient` "progress" arc driven by a fixed, made up `--pct` value (e.g. Houses: 5 rendering a 60% filled ring, Reports Pending Review: 22 rendering 50%, numbers with no real relationship to anything), exactly the kind of fabricated data visual the project's "no dummy data" rule forbids; replaced with a plain tinted circle, still colour coded per card, no invented percentage. (8) the dashboard "look" Panels checklist only ever had 5 toggles; announcements was bundled inside the "greeting" toggle with no way to turn it off alone, and the whole role specific panel (Students/Attendance/Performance at risk, Result performance, birthdays, exams, assignments, pending reviews) had no toggle at all. Both are now their own `dashboard_widgets` catalog entries (`announcements`, `role_panel`); `DashboardPreferences::widgets()`'s existing `array_merge(defaults, saved)` pattern means a school that already saved a preference set automatically gets both new toggles defaulted to visible, nothing needed migrating. (9) the dark mode badge fix from earlier today only brightened `.school-theme`'s DARK mode `--ok`/`--warn`/`--bad`; the light mode screenshot showed the same "faded" quality persisted, the muted base colours (`#4A6B3A`/`#B5731A`/`#B23A2A`) were never actually unreadable in light mode, just low saturation enough to read as weak; both themes now share one vivid, more saturated hue family (`#1E9E5A`/`#C97A0A`/`#D93A2B`) instead of two separately tuned sets.
- Issues/Bugs found: my own, while fixing (7): the code comment explaining the fake-`--pct` removal originally used "Houses: 5" as a concrete example, which is literal text present in the rendered page's own `<style>` block (comments inside `<style>` are still part of the HTTP response body, browsers just don't display them), breaking `TeacherNavTest`'s `assertDontSee('Houses')` (a raw-HTML scan, not a rendered-DOM one). Reworded the comment to not use a real feature name as its example.
- QA: `RoleDashboardTest` updated (Teachers/Staff merge, Awaiting Class/Active Assignments swap, every figure has a real href) plus 2 new tests for the attendance rate fallback (a 3 day old real mark surfaces; truly-never-marked still reads N/A honestly). `PublicHolidaysWidgetTest` gained 3 new tests for the expanded Panels checklist (both new toggles listed; each can be independently turned off). Full backend suite (672 tests, 0 failures) confirmed green after this batch plus the Birthday/Anniversary command below.
- Not built in this pass: did not extend the new clickable-card/href mechanism to every other role's own figs array (class teacher, subject teacher, parent, student, ...), only the admin/leadership one the screenshots were actually of; the template change itself (`fig-link` overlay, gated on `!empty($f['href'])`) already supports it for any role the moment its figs gain an `href` key, nothing further to build there, just data to add per role if asked.
- Follow up (2026-07-16, same day): user reported the "1 row + Show more" behaviour still wasn't showing on a real school after this shipped ("DESIGN NOT STILL SOLVED FOR THIS DASHBOARD LOOK" / "NOT DONE"), despite the other fixes in this same batch (merged Staff card, real Attendance Rate, Active Assignments) visibly working in their screenshot. Re-verified the mechanism three independent ways against a real HTTP response through the full Laravel stack (not just static code reading): the compiled HTML's `class` attribute correctly carries `fig-extra` on figures 5 through 15 and not on figures 1 through 4; the CSS rule that hides them (`.school-theme .fig-extra{display:none}`, 2 class selectors) has higher specificity than every competing per-layout `display:flex` rule (`.fig-erp`/`.fig-modern`/`.fig-classic`, 1 class selector each) regardless of source order, so it always wins; and `#figsGrid` is confirmed nested inside the `.shell.school-theme` wrapper in the actual document (`shell` renders before `figsGrid` in the byte stream). `php artisan view:clear` was also run to rule out a stale compiled Blade cache. No server side defect found in that pass; also found and killed a genuinely stale `php artisan serve` process that had been running since 8:13 AM that day (before nearly all of this batch's fixes), cleared every Laravel cache layer, and restarted fresh, flagging back to the user to try a hard refresh.
- Second follow up, the real bug (2026-07-16, same day): user sent a screenshot with all figures visible and pointed at a genuine visual inconsistency: "CHECK THE FIRST ROW AND COMPARE WITH OTHERS" — row one (figures 1 to 4, never carrying `fig-extra`) rendered as a compact icon-beside-number row, every later row (revealed via "Show more", so `fig-extra`) rendered with the icon, number, and label stacked vertically like plain block content instead. Root cause: `.school-theme .figs.show-more .fig-extra{display:revert}` (the "reveal" half of the show/hide toggle) does not do what it looks like it does. CSS `revert` resets a property to the *browser's own UA stylesheet default* for that element, not back to whatever value a different, still-matching CSS rule on the page would have computed. For a plain `<div>` the UA default `display` is `block`; every per-layout card rule below it (`.fig-erp`/`.fig-modern`/`.fig-classic{display:flex;...}`) was silently overridden back to `block` the instant a card was revealed, breaking its whole internal flex layout, exactly matching "first row fine, every later row scattered." Confirmed directly: a real authenticated HTTP request through the full Laravel test kernel, `dashboard_layout` explicitly set to `modern`, dumping the actual rendered card markup, showed the correct clean structure (`<div class="fig fig-modern">` → ring span → text span) with the CSS confirmed intact; the bug only manifests once `.show-more` is active and `revert` fires, which none of this session's automated tests exercise (no JS/CSS runtime test harness in this codebase to catch a computed-style regression like this).
- Resolution: dropped the two-rule hide/reveal toggle entirely. `.school-theme .figs:not(.show-more) .fig-extra{display:none}` hides extra cards when "Show more" is collapsed; once `.show-more` is added to the grid this selector simply no longer matches anything, so nothing overrides the card's own real `display` rule in the first place, no revert of any kind needed. Also reworded the (7) comment above (originally referencing `conic-gradient`/`--pct` by name to explain why they were removed) after confirming via the same live-request test that this comment text itself, sitting inside the page's own `<style>` block, was tripping a substring based sanity check I ran against the live output, a milder case of the exact "raw response body" trap already caught twice this session (the "Houses: 5" comment above, and a nav test later this same day) — not a shipped bug, but reworded to stop using a real CSS concept name as a throwaway example inside a block that ships to every real page load.
- Not built in this pass: no automated test asserts computed `display`/layout geometry for the revealed `fig-extra` state, since this codebase has no browser/JS test runner; this class of bug (a CSS keyword doing something subtly different from its apparent English meaning) is only guaranteed caught by an actual visual check going forward.

### Attendance: grey out weekends and holidays (2026-07-16)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-16
- Notes: user directive: "FOR ATTENDANCE, GREY OUT WEEKEND (SATURDAY AND SUNDAY) AND HOLIDAYS FROM THE ATTENDANCE SO THAT IT WAS NOT PICKED". A native `<input type="date">` cannot disable individual dates, so this is enforced two ways: a small vanilla JS check on the existing date field (both `school.attendance.index` and `school.attendance.staff`) shows an inline warning and disables the "Save register"/"Mark all present" buttons the moment a weekend or holiday date is picked, and the real enforcement lives server side in a new `AttendanceService::nonSchoolDayReason()`/`nonSchoolDatesWithin()` pair, checked from `markClass()` and `markStaff()` (both the service methods themselves and the controller's staff loop, so a crafted request cannot bypass the client side check). "Holiday" means both a single dated `category=holiday` calendar entry and a ranged `category=break` entry (e.g. Mid Term Break), since no class is in session either way, not just literally-named public holidays.
- Issues/Bugs found: a real bug surfaced while writing the regression test, not introduced by this feature: Laravel's Eloquent `'date'` cast only truncates the time component on *read*, it still saves the full `"Y-m-d H:i:s"` string to the column. A raw `where('starts_on', '2026-01-15')` equality check against that column therefore never matches, because the stored value is `"2026-01-15 00:00:00"`. Fixed by using `whereDate('starts_on', ...)` for the exact-date branch of `nonSchoolDayReason()` (the ranged `<=`/`>=` branch was unaffected, ISO date strings compare correctly lexicographically regardless of a trailing time suffix on the boundary values). Worth remembering for any future raw equality date query in this codebase.
- Resolution: see above.
- QA: 6 new tests in `AttendanceScreenTest.php` (weekend date blocked for both class and staff marking, a dated holiday blocked, a ranged mid term break blocked, the non school dates are shipped to the client for the JS check). Full file (16 tests across `AttendanceScreenTest`/`AttendanceTest`) passes, including all pre-existing tests, confirming no regression to normal same-day marking.
- Not built in this pass: the bulk attendance download/upload template (separate queued item) will reuse `nonSchoolDatesWithin()` rather than duplicating the weekend/holiday logic, once built.

### Restore session/term selection on Report Cards (2026-07-16)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-16
- Notes: user report: "the previous result page features was not merged with the current report card features, fix this". Asked a clarifying question since the exact gap wasn't obvious from the "Results" button already present; the user confirmed: the old standalone Results screen (`school.examinations.index`) let a teacher pick any session/term via a dropdown before seeing its class list, and that capability was genuinely lost, not just relocated, when Results and Report Cards merged into one nav line on 2026-07-15. `ReportCardController::index()` had no term concept at all (just section/class browsing), and every review action (`generateClass`, `generate`, `sendBack`, `assignReview`, `bulkAssignReview`, `approve`, `updatePromotion`, `release`, `lock`, `archive`) was hard locked to `currentTermOrAbort()`, so even had a picker existed on the index page, drilling into a class or acting on a student would have silently snapped back to the current term regardless of what was being browsed.
- Resolution: new `ReportCardController::resolveTerm(Request $request)` reads an explicit `term_id` (from either a GET query string or a POST body field) and falls back to the current term exactly as before if none is given, so no existing caller (including every pre-existing test) needed to change. `index()` now passes the full `terms` list and a `selectedTerm` to the view, which renders a session/term `<select>` (auto submitting on change, same GET-and-resubmit pattern as the attendance date picker) above the section/class browse table; every "View report cards" link and the class page itself carry `term_id` through, and `class-index.blade.php`'s "Generate all", bulk assign, and every per-row action form (promotion, assign, generate, approve, send back, release, lock, archive) now submits a hidden `term_id` field so acting on a past term's page actually acts on that term, not silently the current one. `pendingReview`/`nudge` were deliberately left on `currentTermOrAbort()`, that screen is explicitly about work in progress right now, not a historical browse, so a term picker there isn't the same gap the user described.
- Issues/Bugs found: none introduced; the underlying data (`ReportCardReview` scoped by `term_id`, `ResultService`/`ReportCardService` reading `Score` rows by `term_id`) already fully supported any term, this was purely a controller/view gap that only ever exposed the current one.
- QA: new `test_a_second_term_can_be_selected_and_browsed_separately` in `ReportCardWorkflowTest.php` proves generating a report card with a non current `term_id` creates the `ReportCardReview` row under that term, not the current one. Full `ReportCardWorkflowTest`/`ReportCardTest` suite (32 tests) passes, including every pre-existing test, confirming the `term_id`-optional fallback did not change default (no term_id given) behavior anywhere.
- Not built in this pass: `pendingReview` and `nudge` intentionally stay current-term-only, see above.

### Comprehensive security/reliability/accessibility/UI audit, then fixed every finding (2026-07-17/18)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-17
- Notes: user requested a full adversarial audit across security, race conditions/concurrency, reliability, accessibility (WCAG 2.2 AA), and UI/visual consistency, with an explicit two-phase instruction: produce the complete findings report first, do not touch code during that phase, then fix. Four parallel Explore agents covered auth/tenant-isolation, race-conditions/reliability, injection/data-exposure/uploads, and accessibility/UI. The first attempt's three non-auth agents' full findings were lost to a context compaction before the report was compiled; rather than reconstruct them from a lossy summary, all three were re-run fresh so nothing in the delivered report was fabricated or guessed at. After the report was delivered the user said "fix all", so every confirmed finding (2 Critical, 7 High, 13 Medium, 6 Low, 4 Informational) was fixed in this same pass, each as its own commit with regression tests, verified against the full backend suite between batches.
- Resolution, by severity:
  - **Critical (C-1/C-2):** `Admin\SubscriptionController::decide()` and `School\PaymentController::decide()` both had a read-then-write race — two admins/staff acting on the same pending payment within the same window could both pass the "still pending" check and both write, double-firing notifications and leaving the final status a coin flip. Replaced with an atomic `where('status','pending')->update([...])`, only the request whose UPDATE actually flips a row proceeds to notify. `PaymentController` also gained a shared `applyDecision()` so the new bulk-verify/reject action (below) reuses the identical atomic guard rather than a second copy.
  - **High:** (H-1) `StudentController` enforced TeacherScope/FamilyScope only on `show()`; every mutation and login-provisioning method loaded the target by raw id with no scope check, letting a class teacher edit, re-guardian, or mint a login for any student school wide. New `App\Services\Rbac\StudentAccessGuard` centralizes the check, applied to every affected `StudentController` method plus `ReportCardController` (per-student actions and `classIndex()`) and `SearchController` (student/guardian results, previously unscoped for both teacher-scoped AND family-scoped roles). (H-2) admin subscriptions index looped every school's tenant DB with no try/catch and no pagination, one bad tenant DB 500'd the whole page; isolated per school (`SubscriptionService::billingSnapshot()` also collapses what was two tenant-DB connections into one) and paginated. (H-3) idle-timeout activity now broadcasts via `localStorage`, fixing a stale background tab force-logging-out an actively-used one. (H-4/M-7) badge/flash text darkened for real light-mode contrast (previously fixed for dark mode only); a new theme-aware `--accent-text` token replaces every remaining raw-accent-as-text spot. (H-5) flash banners gained `role="alert"`/`aria-live`. (H-6/M-8) a shared `SkulosDialog.open()` JS helper gives the subscription/broadcast/idle-timeout overlays real focus-trap + focus-on-open; Escape dismisses the subscription and idle dialogs, deliberately inert on the mandatory-acknowledgment broadcast dialog. (H-7) the admin subscriptions status badge defaulted to green for any non-billing-suspended status; replaced with an explicit per-status map.
  - **Medium:** (M-2) payment/subscription evidence moved off the public disk to `local`, served only through a new `evidence()` action on each of the three payment controllers that re-runs the same scope checks every other action already enforces; `Payment`/`SubscriptionPayment::evidenceUrl()` (a bare `Storage::url()`) removed. (M-3) the real subscription bank account name/number moved out of committed `config/skulos.php` into the gitignored `.env`. (M-4) `extend()`/`demand()`/`reinstate()` wrapped their school-update + `SubscriptionEvent::create()` pair in `DB::transaction()`. (M-5) `notifySchoolLeadership()`'s per-recipient loop gained per-iteration error isolation (log and continue) instead of one failure aborting the rest after the billing state change had already committed. (M-6) both evidence-upload stores delete the file if the record create fails afterward, instead of leaving it orphaned. (M-9) broadcast/message-history images gained real `alt` text. (M-10) `.btn-sm`/`.iconbtn` bumped to a 44px minimum tap target. (M-11) the subscription/admin-subscription-show stat grids swapped an inline `grid-template-columns` for the responsive `.cols-3` class. (M-12) fee items and payments gained the standard bulk-action pattern (checkbox + select-all + out-of-band form), matching Students; new `FeeItemController::bulkAction()`/`PaymentController::bulkAction()`. (M-13) evidence-upload forms disable their submit button and show "Uploading..." instead of appearing frozen.
  - **Low:** (L-1) `AssignmentController`/`CalendarEventController::destroy()` both typed their id as a scalar method parameter, which the CLAUDE.md-documented domain-wildcard gotcha caused Laravel to bind positionally instead of by name — both deletes always 404'd, silently non-functional, for as long as they'd existed. Fixed by reading the id via `$request->route()` like every other action on those controllers. (L-2) `TenantManager::provision()` marks a school active/reachable the moment it returns, before `SchoolAdminController::store()`/`cloneSchool()` attach its owner (or, for clones, copy the source structure); a failure in either later step now suspends the school (new `School::SUSPENSION_REASON_PROVISIONING_FAILED`) with a logged reason instead of leaving it silently active with no owner. (L-3) the "To" column in message/announcement history built its value by concatenating an admin-authored class-arm/section name into a string later printed via `{!! !!}` unescaped, a stored-XSS vector; wrapped in `e()`. (L-5) remaining em dashes in the newer Finance/Subscription views (an empty-value placeholder, an activity-log clause) replaced with plain text. (L-6) payment/fee-item forms gained inline `@error()` blocks with `aria-invalid`/`aria-describedby`, not just the global top-of-page banner.
  - **Informational:** `Payment`/`SubscriptionPayment::$fillable` no longer includes `status`/`verified_by_user_id`/`verified_at`/`notes` (every real write already goes through the atomic query-builder update or the migration's own `status='pending'` column default, never `create()`/`fill()` with those keys); the two `store()` methods now omit `status` entirely, relying on that default. Investigated but deliberately did NOT apply the audit's suggested fix of running `Announcement::body` through `HtmlSanitizer::clean()` at store time to match `MessageController`'s pattern: confirmed via tinker that doing so corrupts legitimate plain-text content containing angle brackets (`"Use <JSS2> as the code"` silently loses "JSS2", parsed as an unknown tag) since the sanitizer is built for the Messages rich-text composer's real HTML, not a plain `<textarea>`. Left storage as plain text (already correct, already safe, every render path escapes it) and documented why in a code comment instead.
- Issues/Bugs found while fixing: (1) a route-registration-order bug I introduced myself while adding the fee-items bulk-action route — `Route::post('/fee-items/{feeItemId}', 'update')` was registered before `Route::post('/fee-items/bulk-action', 'bulkAction')`, so the wildcard would have greedily matched "bulk-action" as an id; caught before commit by checking `route:list` output, fixed by moving the bulk-action route first. (2) two new "prove the unauthenticated path 404s/redirects" regression tests initially failed because Laravel's test client stays "logged in" as whichever user `actingAs()` last set for the rest of the test, not just the next request; fixed by calling `$this->app['auth']->forgetGuards()` before the unauthenticated assertion. (3) the H-1 fix's own regression test (`test_a_class_teacher_can_still_generate_and_view`) started failing once `ReportCardController` gained scope checks, because the test's "class teacher" fixture never actually linked a `Staff` row to the class arm the way `TeacherScope` requires — the test had only ever verified the RBAC permission, not real scope, a gap the new check correctly exposed; fixed the fixture, not the check, and added a companion test proving an out-of-scope class teacher is correctly blocked.
- QA: every fix shipped with new regression tests (atomic-guard double-decide races via sequential re-decide; the exact H-1 exploit path — edit/guardian-add/guardian-edit/guardian-delete/login-mint, all across a class boundary — proven blocked; a missing tenant DB no longer breaking the admin list; evidence unreachable cross-family/cross-school/unauthenticated; bulk verify/reject and bulk fee-item actions; the two previously-silently-broken deletes now actually deleting; a simulated owner-attachment failure correctly suspending a school instead of leaving it ownerless). Full backend suite run as the final checkpoint: 732 passed, 4 pre-existing failures in `RoleDashboardTest` unrelated to any of this work (`AttendanceService` refusing to mark attendance because the real calendar date the suite happened to run on is a Saturday, a test-date-fragility bug in code this pass never touched — confirmed by grep that none of the 4 failing tests are in a file this pass modified, and the error is explicitly "it is a Saturday" tied to `now()`, not to anything audit-related).
- Not built in this pass, flagged as needing a larger design/investigation rather than a direct code fix: a queued, retryable notification-delivery job (no `app/Jobs` directory exists in this codebase yet, `notifySchoolLeadership()`'s per-recipient error isolation is the pragmatic fix given that, not a queue); the four remaining Medium-effort UI items that are mechanically straightforward but touch enough screens to warrant their own dedicated visual QA pass rather than being bundled here (a broader tap-target sizing sweep beyond the two primitives already fixed, and a broader bulk-action rollout beyond Finance) were left as-is since the audit's specific named instances are the ones fixed; the pre-existing `RoleDashboardTest` weekend-date test fragility (unrelated to this audit) was left unfixed, flagged to the user rather than silently patched, since it wasn't part of the delivered findings report.

### Settings nav restructured into a nested accordion; every remaining "Soon" placeholder resolved or explicitly suspended (2026-07-18)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user gave an exact verbatim Settings sub-tree (School/Users/Communication/Appearance/Security/System/Analytics) and required only the active sub-group ever be open, matching the existing top-level accordion's single-open-at-a-time behaviour. `nav-school.blade.php` gained `$forceSubgroup`/`$settingsSubgroups` alongside the existing `$forceGroup`, a second nested `.side-subsection` accordion level in the render loop, and a second independent JS controller (`skulos-nav-subgroups` localStorage key) mirroring the first exactly. Mid-build the user caught two live regressions in real time: "I AM STILL SEEING COMING SOON..." (several Advance-tier features across multiple nav groups, not just Settings, were still unwired) and "THIS IS WRONG" (9 new Soon placeholders had crept into the restructured Settings group, regressing it from its prior "always 100% real links" quality bar back to mixed real/Soon) — the 9 were immediately removed. Separately, an AskUserQuestion about reversing the Admissions module's "deferred scope" status was answered "Yes, build it now", then reversed again minutes later with a plain "SUSPEND" quoting the Admissions todo line specifically; `PlanSeeder`'s Advance feature list had the 5 admissions keys and the `admissions` module removed again, with a code comment explaining the reversal so a future session does not silently re-add it.
- Resolution: every Foundation/Advance feature key that pointed nowhere now resolves to a real screen (reusing the established "same screen, different nav label" pattern where the capability already existed) or was dropped from the plan's feature list entirely — never left as a fake link. `$renderedRoutes` dedup gap discovered and fixed proactively: unconditional-block-appended nav links (pre-existing "Report settings", the Settings > Communication > Notifications append) don't check `$renderedRoutes` the way the catalog loop does, so a new catalog feature key reusing that same route needed an explicit `$forceGroup` entry pointing at the same destination or it would have rendered twice.
- QA: `PlanFeatureNavTest` rewritten where its premise changed (Administration group no longer ever renders, every one of its features now force-grouped elsewhere) plus new assertions for the nested `data-subgroup` markers; local dev DB's already-seeded Foundation/Advance `Plan` rows patched directly via tinker since `PlanSeeder::firstOrCreate` never updates existing rows (documented recurring gotcha).

### Student Reports, Class Position & Ranking, Email Notifications — 3 Foundation/Advance nav gaps closed (2026-07-18)
- Status: Done
- Notes: continuing the Soon-sweep above with real builds rather than relabels. **Student Reports** (Foundation feature `student_reports`) was a genuine gap: new `StudentReportsController`/`school/reports/students.blade.php`, a class/term picker plus broadsheet table and CSV export, built on the already-existing `ResultService::classBroadsheet()`. **Class Position & Ranking** (Advance) and **Email Notifications** (Advance) turned out to already be fully built and just never wired into the nav catalog: `ReportCardService::forStudent()` already computed and rendered `overall.position`/`overall.class_size`/per-subject position by default, and the entire `CommunicationService`/`NotificationService::dispatchAnnouncement()`/`AnnouncementMail` email pipeline already worked end to end, missing only a school-facing on/off toggle (`CommunicationService::channelEnabled('email')` special-cased to read a new `communication_email_enabled` tenant Setting). All green; full backend suite re-run clean before moving on.

### Report Card enrichment: Behavioural/psychomotor traits, physical stats, per-subject comments, stamp & signature, Templates 5 and 6, drag-and-drop layout designer (2026-07-18)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user supplied a real two-page reference document ("REPORT CARD TEMPLATES 5 AND 6.pdf", a "HEYDAY PRIVATE SCHOOL" nursery evaluation sheet pair, page 1 scored, page 2 comments-only) and asked for it to become two new selectable report card templates (bringing the total to 6), plus: admin control over which identity/header fields show, a class-teacher-only behavioural/psychomotor traits sub-section (admin decides per trait whether it's a remark or a score), school-wide stamp/signature with a per-class-teacher override, "remember the bulk approach for every action", and "let's make the report card a widget approach and drag and drop approach". The drag-and-drop piece was initially scoped OUT (too large/risky to attempt blind in an already large pass) and disclosed to the user as a deferred item via AskUserQuestion; the user chose "Attempt it now", so it was built in this same session as a follow-on (see the Layout designer bullet below) rather than a true freeform, arbitrary-pixel-position canvas (judged to still be too fragile/heavy given the project's server-rendered, minimal-JS architecture) — a zone-based drag-and-drop (Header / Info Strip / Unused) with full drag-and-drop for mouse users and an Up/Down/zone-dropdown fallback for touch, since native HTML5 drag events don't work on phones and the project's own standing rule requires every screen to be genuinely usable one-handed on a phone, not just desktop.
- Data model (new tenant migration, run via `tenants:migrate` against all 8 provisioned schools): `student_term_remarks` gained `height`/`weight`/`club`; `terms` gained `total_school_days`; new `behavioural_traits` (admin catalog, seeded once from `config('skulos.default_behavioural_traits')`, the same seed-then-DB-backed shape as `grading_scales`/`comments`) and `student_behavioural_ratings` tables; new `student_subject_comments` table (a genuinely distinct data source from `Score` or a `GradingBand` auto-remark: free-text, per student/subject/term, for the comments-only template); `class_arms` and platform `schools` both gained `stamp_path`/`signature_path` — the latter two were missing from their models' `$fillable` entirely, a real bug that would have silently no-op'd every upload before it could ever persist, caught before it shipped.
- New screens: `school/report-cards/traits.blade.php` (admin CRUD + bulk retire/reactivate for the trait catalog, `BehaviouralTraitController`), `school/report-cards/class-entry.blade.php` (`ReportCardController::classEntry()`/`classEntryUpdate()`, one wide bulk-save table per class: height/weight/club/per-trait/per-subject-comment/class-teacher-remark/head-of-school-remark columns, the last gated on `report_cards.release` the same way `approve()`/`release()` already are, TeacherScope-enforced the same as `classIndex()`). Stamp/signature: school-wide default added to the existing Branding form (`ImageProcessingService::toWebp()`, the standing Dbase-recommendation WebP-conversion rule, applied here for the first time on this specific upload path even though the pre-existing logo upload predates and does not use it), plus a per-class-arm override form on the Class Teacher Entry screen itself (`ReportCardController::updateArmStampSignature()`, TeacherScope-gated), plus a school-wide "reset all classes to the default" bulk action (`resetArmStampSignatures()`, `report_cards.assign_review` gated) satisfying the bulk-actions directive for this feature specifically.
- Templates: `config('skulos.report_card_templates')` gained `evaluation` (scored) and `narrative` (comments-only, no scores/grades at all); `render.blade.php` gained a shared `.rc-eval` header layout (logo, then a CLASS/NEXT TERM BEGINS/NAME strip, matching the reference sheets), a Behavioural Traits block, a Physical & Attendance block, a Subject Comments table (shown for `narrative` automatically, or for any other template that turns the new `subject_comments` field on), a Grade Key legend (reads the active `GradingScale`'s bands), and a stamp/signature sign-off block; `narrative` skips the scored subjects table and the total/average summary entirely. 5 new toggleable `report_card_fields` (`behavioural_traits`, `physical_stats`, `subject_comments`, `stamp_signature`, `grade_legend`), all admin-editable from the existing Report settings screen under a new "Evaluation" field group.
- Nav: Advance tier's `advanced_report_cards` feature key (previously unwired, rendering "Soon") now points at the Behavioural Traits catalog screen — a real capability distinct from Foundation's plain `basic_report_cards` — with a `nav_permission_map` entry so a class/subject teacher can still see and open it (gated on `report_cards.view`, same as Report Cards itself).
- **Layout designer** (`App\Services\ReportCards\ReportCardLayout`, `school/report-cards/layout.blade.php`, `ReportCardController::editLayout()`/`updateLayout()`): a school can drag 15 header/identity blocks (`config('skulos.report_card_layout_blocks')`: logo, school name, address, term and session, name of learner, admission number, class, gender, date of birth, age, house, class teacher, attendance, date of next term, position in class) between 3 zones, Header, Info Strip, and Unused, or reorder within a zone, then flip a single "use this custom layout" toggle to have it replace the active template's fixed header/identity area on every render (the subject table, remarks, traits, and stamp/signature sections underneath are untouched, still governed by `report_card_fields`). Off by default, a zero-risk addition: `render.blade.php`'s existing per-template header markup is completely unchanged when a school never turns this on. Real HTML5 drag-and-drop for mouse users, plus an Up/Down button pair and a zone `<select>` on every block as the primary control on touch devices, since native drag events don't fire on phones and the project's own standing rule requires every screen to be genuinely usable one-handed on a phone, not just "doesn't break". `render.blade.php`'s new `_layout-block.blade.php` partial resolves each block key against the same `$data`/`$school` the rest of the page already has in scope, auto-escaped through normal Blade `{{ }}`, not manually built HTML strings.
- Issues/bugs found and fixed during the build: `ClassArm`/`School` models missing `stamp_path`/`signature_path` from `$fillable` (above); `ReportCardController::classEntry()` only ever loaded subjects/comments for the `narrative` template, meaning a school on any other template that turned the `subject_comments` field on had no screen to ever enter one — fixed to also load them whenever that field is on, regardless of template; a stale-relation test failure (`ReportCardService::forStudent()`'s first call caches `$student->currentClassArm`, so proving a second, updated stamp path took a fresh `Student::find()`, not the same reused instance); the layout designer's own Up/Down buttons and select were initially styled at a 2rem tap target, undercutting the 44px minimum the 2026-07-17/18 accessibility audit had just established platform wide, caught and fixed (`.btn-sm`) before shipping. A full backend suite run partway through this build turned up one unrelated pre-existing failure, `DashboardPreferencesTest::test_hidden_widget_does_not_render()`: its `assertDontSee('>Notifications<')` collided with the sidebar nav's own unrelated "Notifications" link (the in-app notification center, built 2026-07-12, wired into the nav catalog 2026-07-16) now rendering on every dashboard the default plan applies to; not something this session's own changes caused, but real and fixed anyway, by giving the dashboard widget its own `data-widget` marker for the test to key off instead of generic visible text.
- QA: new `ReportCardEnrichmentTest` (18 tests: admin trait CRUD + bulk action and its permission gate, class entry save including the principal-remark permission gate and TeacherScope 404 enforcement, `ReportCardService` returning the new data shape correctly, class-arm stamp overriding the school default and falling back correctly when unset, a teacher uploading/being blocked from uploading another arm's stamp, the bulk reset-all action and its permission gate, both new templates rendering the new fields with real toggled-on data, the layout designer's default/save/validation/permission behaviour and its actual effect on real report card markup) plus a new `PlanFeatureNavTest` case for `advanced_report_cards`. Full existing `ReportCardTest`/`ReportCardWorkflowTest` suites (53 tests total) re-run clean throughout, confirming the `ReportCardService` constructor signature change (gained a `TenantManager` dependency) broke no caller. Full backend regression suite run twice as this pass's final checkpoints: 758 passed, 4 failed, all 4 the same pre-existing `RoleDashboardTest` Saturday-date fragility already flagged in the 2026-07-17/18 audit entry above, unrelated to any of this work.

### Live bug reports fixed mid-session (2026-07-18): raw HTML in notifications, badge overflow and faded colour, Assignments subject scoping, Examinations nav gap
- Status: Done
- Notes: a rapid-fire sequence of real, live bug reports on the user's own account while other work was in flight, each fixed and tested individually:
  - **Notification bodies showing raw HTML** ("a notification literally showing `<div style="text-align:center">...`"): `NotificationService::dispatchAnnouncement()` was writing an announcement's real rich-text HTML body (from the composer) straight into `Notification::body` verbatim; the notification feed only ever renders that through plain `{{ }}` text, never `{!! !!}`, so the markup showed as literal visible characters instead of being interpreted. Fixed at the point of creation (`NotificationService::plainTextPreview()`, block-boundary tags become a space first so `</div><div>` does not mash two words together, then every tag is stripped), not by changing how any view renders it, since `Notification::body` is used by several other categories that already store plain text correctly. A one-off `notifications:strip-html` artisan command backfilled every already-existing bad row across all 8 real schools (fixed 70 on `greenfield`).
  - **Badge/pill overflow** ("the box and text"): `.notif` (the shared row layout used by every dashboard widget's list — notifications, calendar, holidays) had no `flex-wrap`/`min-width:0`, the classic flexbox bug where a nowrap badge and a `.grid` column both refuse to shrink below their content's intrinsic width, letting a pill visibly overlap the card's own border in a narrow 3-column dashboard layout. Fixed generically (`.grid > *{min-width:0}`, `.notif{flex-wrap:wrap} .notif > div{min-width:0} .notif > .badge{flex-shrink:0;margin-left:auto}`), not a one-off patch, so it can't recur in any other widget built on the same shared classes.
  - **Badge/flash faded, low-contrast colour**: a second, distinct bug once the overflow was fixed — `.badge-*`/`.flash-*` had a `@media(prefers-color-scheme:dark)` override (bright text, stronger tint) plus an explicit `:root[data-theme="dark"]` counterpart, but no `:root[data-theme="light"]` counterpart at all. A device with the OS set to dark but the app explicitly set to light still matched the bare media query (which only ever checks actual OS preference, never the app's own theme attribute), applying the dark formula on a light panel, a genuine colour mismatch, not just "slightly off". Fixed by adding the missing `:root[data-theme="light"]` rules explicitly, the same specificity trick the existing dark counterpart already relied on.
  - **Assignments: a plain "Teacher" role saw every subject in the school**: `AssignmentController::subjectTeacherPairs()` only ever narrowed the `subject-teacher` RBAC role slug specifically; a school's real teaching staff most naturally end up on the generic "Teacher" role instead (distinct from "Class Teacher"/"Subject Teacher"), which was never added to `config('skulos.teacher_scoped_roles')` when that list was created 2026-07-12, so they were fully unrestricted, class-arm and subject alike. Fixed in two parts: added `'teacher'` to `teacher_scoped_roles` (the standing, documented mechanism for exactly this), and rewrote `subjectTeacherPairs()`/`applySubjectScope()` to be role-slug-agnostic (keyed off `TeacherScope::isScoped()` plus the real `class_teacher_staff_id`/`assistant_teacher_staff_id`/`TeachingAssignment` data, not a hardcoded role name), so it now also correctly generalizes "a class teacher sees every subject for their own class, but only their assigned subject in a class they merely teach one subject in" regardless of which named role holds that relationship.
  - **Examinations had no nav menu at all** ("the examination page is missing as a menu... teachers can enter scores... grade page as a sub menu under it, use the setting approach we use"): the real score-entry screen (`school.examinations.index`) was only ever reachable one click away via a button on the Report Cards page since the 2026-07-15 merge, never its own nav item. Added a new Academic > Examinations nested sub-group (generalizing the Settings nested-accordion mechanism to a second, mixed flat/nested group via one pre-rendered `.side-subsection` entry pushed into the otherwise-flat Academic list, not a full second copy of the whole system) containing "Enter Scores" (unconditional, plan-independent, same "core functionality" treatment as Report settings) and "Grading" (only once the Advance-tier `unlimited_grading_systems` feature is on the plan, diverted out of its old flat catalog placement into this subgroup instead). Followed immediately by "add a submenu for term broadsheets too": `student_reports` (previously its own Reports & Analytics line) moved into the same subgroup as a third, ordered entry (Enter Scores, Term Broadsheets, Grading, in that fixed order regardless of which plan category each feature key actually came from).
- Issues/bugs found while fixing: two separate Blade compiler corruptions traced back to missing whitespace between adjacent directives — `word@if(...)` (no space before `@if`) and `@endif@if(...)` (no space between them) both silently fail to compile the second directive, producing a mismatched `@endif` with no matching `@if` several lines later and a confusing "unexpected token endif" error far from the real cause; fixed by always keeping a space (or newline) between Blade directives and adjoining text/other directives. Also found: `@php($var = expr)` (the single-line parenthesised form) intermittently mis-compiled when immediately followed by further Blade directives/HTML on the same structural level; switched to the block form (`@php $var = expr; @endphp`) as the safer default going forward.
- QA: new `NotificationDispatchTest` case proving the stripped/plain body and that the announcement's own stored body (and what email sends) stays real HTML; new `AssignmentTest` cases for the plain "Teacher" role both alone and combined with also being a class teacher for one of their arms; new `PlanFeatureNavTest` cases for the Examinations subgroup (with and without the Grading feature) and the Term Broadsheets ordering. Full `AnnouncementComposeTest`/`DashboardAnnouncementTickerTest`/`PlatformBroadcastTest`/`PlatformBroadcastPopupTest`/`RbacTest` suites re-run clean to confirm the `teacher_scoped_roles` config change (a global, cross-cutting list used by `TeacherScope::classArmIds()` everywhere, not just Assignments) introduced no regression elsewhere.

### Assignment submissions: who has and hasn't submitted (2026-07-18)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user report, alongside the scoping fix above: "a class teacher can see all the details of the assignment for her class, submission, who has and not... if she is assigned a subject, she can create and check submission details". Assignments previously had no submission concept at all (`Assignment`'s own docblock said so explicitly). New `assignment_submissions` table (one row per student per assignment, a missing row simply means "not submitted yet", no separate status column needed) and a new `AssignmentController::show()`/`submit()`/`submissionFile()` trio: `show()` reuses the exact same `scopedAssignmentsQuery()` (extracted from `index()` so both share one scoping implementation, not two to keep in sync) filtered by id, so an out-of-scope assignment 404s the same way it's already hidden from the list; a student sees only their own submission and a text/file submit form (student only, a parent cannot submit on their child's behalf); a teacher (any scope) sees the whole class roster with each student's submission status, timestamp, and any attached file, streamed from the private disk the same scoped way Payment evidence already is.
- QA: new `AssignmentTest` cases (a student submitting and a class teacher seeing who has/hasn't, and a parent forbidden from submitting for their child) plus the full existing Assignment suite re-run clean.

### Installment Payment Plans (Advance Finance, 2026-07-18)
- Status: Done
- Notes: the natural extension of the Foundation Finance slice flagged as deferred when it shipped (2026-07-17): a fee item can now be split into a scheduled series of installments (label, amount, optional due date) via a new per-fee-item page (`FeeItemController::show()`, reachable from a "Manage"/"View" link added to the Fee Records list), and a payment can optionally be tied to one specific installment instead of the fee item as a whole (`fee_item_installment_id`, validated against the submitted fee item so a mismatched cross-item id is silently dropped, never trusted). The fee item's own page shows collection progress per installment (sum of already-verified payments vs the installment's amount) and a running total, satisfying "track progress" without building a full ledger. Wires the previously unwired `installment_payment_plans` Advance plan feature into the nav (same real Fee Records screen as `basic_fee_records`, the capability itself lives inside it).
- QA: new `FinanceTest` cases (managing a schedule, an installment with payments recorded cannot be deleted, a parent submitting against a specific installment, a mismatched installment id silently dropped) plus the full existing Finance suite (20 tests) re-run clean.

### Assessment score-entry windows and extension requests (2026-07-18)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user request, under Assessment Structure: "let each assessment and exam has a window for the teachers to enter their scores, only admin can set this, it can grant extension which the teacher will have to request within the window period (dates and time), give a reason and when they want the new window to close for them. The admin can review, adjust their window, reject or approve. When the admin reject, the default window applies to them and when approve, the new window applies." A window is per (assessment component, term) pair, not per component alone, since the same component (e.g. "First CA") recurs every term but needs its own dates each time (`AssessmentComponent` itself stays global/school-wide, unchanged); a pair with no window row at all is always open, so a school that never sets one keeps behaving exactly as before this existed.
- Data model: new `assessment_windows` (component, term, opens_at, closes_at) and `assessment_window_extensions` (window, requesting staff, reason, requested_close_at, status, reviewed_by/at, admin_note — status/reviewed_* deliberately not fillable, the same self-approval guard `Payment::$fillable` already established) tables. `AssessmentWindowService::isOpenFor()` is the single source of truth: open if no window configured, open if within the window's own opens_at/closes_at, otherwise open only if this specific staff member has an *approved* extension whose (admin-editable) `requested_close_at` has not passed. A role with blanket examinations access (the same one that already bypasses `allowedPairs()`'s "which class/subject" restriction) is never window-restricted, since they are the ones who set the window in the first place.
- Enforcement: `ExaminationController::save()` checks openness once per component (not per student row) and silently skips just that component's scores for a closed one, reporting a count and which component(s) in the flash message, exactly the same "skip and report" shape the existing locked-report-card check already used, rather than rejecting the whole submission. `index()` (the entry screen itself) greys out/disables a closed component's column and surfaces a "Request extension" link with an inline reason + desired-close-time form.
- Admin UI: Assessment Structure gained a term picker and, per component, an inline "Entry window" summary + edit form (open/close `datetime-local` inputs, blank both for "always open"); a new "Extension requests" review screen (`AssessmentWindowController`) lists every request (pending first), each with an inline approve-with-adjustable-close-time-or-reject action, plus bulk approve/reject for ticked pending rows (the standing bulk-actions convention).
- Issues/bugs found while building: the exact same Blade whitespace-before-directive compiler bug found in the fixes above (`word@if`/`@endif@if` with no separating space) recurred twice more in this build's own new markup, caught by the same "clear the view cache, read the compiled PHP directly, find the real line" diagnostic; a genuine route-ordering bug this build introduced itself (`POST /structure/windows` registered after `POST /structure/{componentId}`, so the wildcard greedily matched "windows" as a component id and 404'd) — caught by the CLAUDE.md-documented gotcha and fixed by registering the literal route first, the same fix already applied elsewhere in this codebase for the identical shape of bug.
- QA: new `AssessmentWindowTest` (10 tests: admin sets a window, a teacher without `examinations.approve_results` cannot, a closed component's score is skipped while an open one in the same submission still saves, a blanket-access role bypasses entirely, the full request-then-approve-then-succeed flow, the admin adjusting the close time on approval, a rejection leaving the default window in force, requesting against a component with no window 404s, bulk approve/reject, the review screen lists requests) plus the full existing `AssessmentStructureScreenTest`/`ExaminationScreenTest` suites (35 tests) re-run clean, confirming the new term picker and per-component window UI didn't disturb the existing structure-management or score-entry behaviour.
