Added explicit A4 sizing/margins, tighter print-only spacing on every block, and `break-inside:avoid` on tables, rows, and section blocks so realistic single-term data fits one page, and whatever occasionally doesn't still breaks cleanly between sections instead of mid-row.
- Separately, user request: "bulk printing and exporting of report card for a class in PDF or EXCEL, admins and class teachers." Excel was already covered by the existing class broadsheet CSV export (`StudentReportsController`), just not linked from where a class teacher would actually look for it. PDF is new: a "Print all" screen renders every student's report card, one per page, in one browser print/save-as-PDF pass. Extracted the single-card body markup out of `render.blade.php` into a shared `_sheet.blade.php` partial so the bulk view reuses the exact same per-student rendering rather than duplicating ~80 lines of template logic that could drift out of sync.
- QA: manual print-preview verification for the A4 fit (no headless browser available in this environment, same documented limitation as earlier screenshot-based QA); new feature test for the bulk print route rendering every student in a class.

### Auto-provision student and parent logins, no manual create-login step (2026-07-22)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user request: "Why do we have to manually create login for students and parents... students can login with their admission number and the default password which must be change upon first login. Parents can login with either email or phone number." Adding a student or guardian now provisions their login immediately, with a school-configurable default password (Student Field Settings) rather than the existing `provision()`'s random-and-emailed one, since a student has no guaranteed email/phone to send anything to and the whole point of a default password is that it's already known, not a secret to deliver. `must_change_password` still forces a real password at first sign in either way. A guardian's login uses whichever real contact they were required to give (email or phone, per the school's own `parent_identifier` setting); a second child sharing the same real parent correctly reuses that one login rather than duplicating it or resetting its password. The existing manual "create login" buttons stay as-is for records that predate this change.
- QA: new feature tests covering auto-provisioning on student create, on guardian create, sibling login reuse, and that `must_change_password` is still enforced. Full `StudentScreenTest` suite green.

The platform-wide input padding rule outranked `.side-search input`'s, fixed by matching it with a more specific `[type="search"]` selector, same pattern as every prior occurrence of this exact bug class this session.
- Also switched the active nav item's `scrollIntoView` from `block:'nearest'` to `block:'start'`, since `'nearest'` was landing the active item flush against the bottom of the sidebar for anything below the fold, rather than visibly near the top.

### Real per-stream rename, instead of renaming the whole class (2026-07-22)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user report: "I renamed the first class stream to Primary 1 silver and the main class name added silver to it and the second stream also added silver to it. Each stream should be a unique class under the main class name." There was no way to rename a stream at all before this, only to add new ones, so the reported "rename" was actually editing the class's own name field hoping it would scope to one stream, which correctly (if confusingly) renames the whole class, what every stream's own label is built from.
- The new rename deliberately never mutates an `Arm` row's `name` in place: `Arm` rows are shared/reused across classes by design (the same "Gold" row can back a stream on two different classes), so an in-place rename would silently rename every other class's same-labelled stream too, the exact "second stream also added silver to it" bug from the other direction. Reassigning the one `ClassArm` to a find-or-created `Arm` matching the new name is the same safe pattern `storeArm()` already uses for adding a stream, and never touches another class's stream.
- Live data fix: the real greenfield school's own corrupted state from before this fix existed (`SchoolClass` "Primary 1 Silver" should have stayed "Primary 1", one `ClassArm` had no `Arm` at all) was corrected directly via tinker using this same safe pattern, not left as stale data.
- QA: new test proving renaming one class's stream never touches the class name or another class's same-named stream.

### Examination Timetable, Master Timetable, and real population for Greenfield Academy (2026-07-22)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user request, given as a detailed spec: a subsection for examination timetable where the admin populates it by entering fields (Paper 1, Paper 2, Break, Paper 3..., the duration per paper and break, when the first paper starts and the time auto populates for other ones) or by uploading a filled template, scoped to a whole section or one specific class; a master timetable "showing for all classes in the school so that the admin, school owner, head teacher, assistant, principal and vice principals can see it at a glance"; then, once both existed, "POPULATE THE TIMETABLES FOR CLASSES AND EXAM FOR ALL CLASSES IN Greenfield Academy, MAKE USE OF THE SUBJECTS THERE AND THE STAFF FOR EACH TEACHERS."
- **Examination Timetable**: deliberately its own tables/service (`ExamPaper`, `ExamTimetableEntry`, `ExamTimetableService`), not a reuse of the existing weekly timetable grid: an exam timetable is dated across a real calendar window and duration-driven (one start time, every paper's time chained off the durations before it), not a recurring day-of-week pattern with independently timed rows the way a regular class period already is. The CSV template mirrors the entry grid exactly (Date, Paper, Time, Subject) so upload and manual entry stay interchangeable for the same scope/date window. `scope_type`/`scope_id` (section vs class) reuses the same EAV-style plain-column-pair pattern the Custom Fields build established, not a real polymorphic relation.
- **Master Timetable**: one card per day, one row per class, one column per teaching period, reusing the exact same `gridForClassArm()` data the per-class editor already computes, just for every class arm instead of one. Gated on `timetable.update`, the same permission the per-class editor already requires, which the named leadership roles already hold via their own existing blanket grants.
- **Population**: `timetable:populate` (new console command, reusable and re-runnable, matching `schools:seed-demo-data`'s own shape), fills every class arm's weekly grid by cycling through that class's real (subject, staff) teaching assignments, and builds an examination timetable per section from the subjects actually offered there, skipping weekends. Run against greenfield: 293 weekly timetable entries across 16 classes, 24 examination timetable entries. The requested per-section (Nursery/Primary/Secondary) start time and period duration is deliberately not modeled: `TimetablePeriod` is one shared catalog for the whole school by design (every class arm's grid reads the same period rows), splitting that into a real per-section catalog would be a bigger schema change than this data-population task and out of scope; the existing shared catalog (08:00 start, 40 minute periods) already matches the Secondary tier's own requested numbers exactly.
- Follow-up bug fixed the same session: the Examination Timetable index page only ever showed anything once `scope_type`/`scope_id`/`start_date` were all explicitly picked, so a first visit with nothing selected looked identical to a genuinely empty one, reported as "no seeded data for greenfield academy" even though the 24 populated entries above already existed. Now falls back to the earliest scope/date that actually has entries when nothing is chosen, spanning enough days to show all of it.
- QA: new `ExaminationTimetableTest`, `TimetableTest` additions for the master grid, and `PopulateTimetablesTest` covering the command's real output counts and its default-index-state fix.

- QA: new `AcademicInsightsAndWorkloadTest` case proving every tied teacher shows, not just one; manual verification of the popup dismissal paths.

### Class Teacher Entry: a CSV template round trip for Narrative Evaluation classes (2026-07-22)

Columns mirror the on-screen table exactly (physical stats, traits, one column per subject comment, remarks), pre-filled with each student's current values rather than blank, matched back on upload by admission number.
- Issues/Bugs: caught and fixed before it shipped: the download filename interpolated the class name directly, and a class name can legitimately contain "/" (e.g. "Creche/Reception"), which `Content-Disposition` rejects outright rather than just rendering oddly.

### Dbase recommendation recompliance recheck (2026-07-22)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user directive: "recheck against the Dbase recommendation," a structured audit of every controller/command touched or built this batch against the standing rules (no file bytes in DB, WebP for images, no permanent generated PDFs, pagination, column selection, retention for unbounded growth). Found and fixed one real gap (Communication History's recipient drill-down, see that entry above). Everything else checked out: bulk report card PDF print is pure `window.print()` with nothing generated or stored server side; every CSV upload streams via `fgetcsv` rather than loading the whole file into memory or persisting the upload; the year of populated attendance data uses column-scoped queries and chunked bulk inserts, not one-row-at-a-time Eloquent creates; the Attendance Dashboard's own figures are aggregate `COUNT`/`GROUP BY` queries, never a raw row dump; no new file/image upload feature landed this batch, so the WebP-conversion rule had nothing new to apply to.
- Deliberate exemptions, reasoned through rather than blindly "fixed": Custom Fields index left unpaginated (a small, admin-curated list of field definitions, realistically 5 to 20 rows, pagination controls would be clutter without real benefit); the growing attendance and examination timetable data correctly left out of the existing retention-purge cycle (`RetentionCleanup`, which already covers notifications/communication logs/sessions/failed logins), since both are legitimate, permanent academic record data, not ephemeral operational noise, the same category as scores and report cards.
- QA: full backend regression suite, 958 passed / 0 failed, run as the final gate for this whole batch.

### Live bug: duplicate exam timetable entry threw UniqueConstraintViolationException (2026-07-22)
- Status: Done
- Notes: reported live on the real greenfield school via a full Laravel error page (`POST /timetable/exam`). Root cause was the same date-cast serialization mismatch bug class already documented in this tracker for the `Attendance` model: `ExamTimetableEntry`'s `'exam_date' => 'date'` cast serializes to `Y-m-d H:i:s` on save, but `updateOrCreate()`'s WHERE-lookup array is never run through that same cast, so a plain `Y-m-d` string never matched an already-saved `Y-m-d 00:00:00` row, and the second save of the same logical key always attempted an INSERT instead of an UPDATE. Fixed the same way as the earlier occurrence: cast removed, stored/compared as a plain string, every call site that relied on a Carbon instance updated to `Carbon::parse()` explicitly.
- Issues/Bugs: the live greenfield database already had 24 exam timetable rows corrupted by the pre-fix behaviour.
- Resolution: normalized directly via tinker (`substr($row->exam_date, 0, 10)`), not left as stale data.
- QA: new `ExaminationTimetableTest` case proving the same date/paper saved twice updates in place instead of duplicating.

### Parent Mobile field now syncs from the primary guardian's phone (2026-07-22)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user report: a parent's contact phone showed "Not set" under student details even after it was populated on the guardian record. New `StudentController::syncParentMobile()` (finds the primary-or-first guardian, updates the legacy `Student.parent_mobile` column, re-fetching guardians fresh to avoid stale relation caching) called from `storeGuardian()`, `updateGuardian()`, and `destroyGuardian()`.
- Resolution: real data backfilled across 6 already-provisioned schools via a tinker loop, not left stale until the next guardian edit happened to trigger it.
- QA: new `StudentScreenTest` case.

### Sort and filter added to the Students list page (2026-07-22)
- Status: Done
- Notes: the flat-roster branch of `StudentController::index()` gained `status`/`sort`/`dir` query params, matching the shape already established for Staff/Parent lists.

### Timetable nav renamed, and a Class/My Timetable toggle for class teachers (2026-07-22)
- Status: Done
- Notes: "My Timetable" nav label changed to plain "Timetable." `TimetableController::mine()` now detects whether the requesting staff member is a class teacher (`ClassArm::where('class_teacher_staff_id', ...)`); if so it offers a `?view=class`/`?view=personal` toggle (default class); a subject teacher, not being a class teacher, sees no toggle at all and goes straight to their own personal schedule, unchanged from before.

- Status: Done
- Notes: `AssignmentController::index()` gained `due_from`/`due_to` query params applied via `whereDate('due_on', ...)`, available to every role (not just admins), the section/class filters staying admin-only as before.

### Report Cards restricted to a class/subject teacher's own class, hidden entirely from subject teachers (2026-07-22, commit `018b315`)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user report: "Class teacher or subject teachers should not be seeing other classes report cards but only the class assigned to them. Subject teachers should not see any class report card at all. Its shows error 404 for subject teacher now but it ought not to be a menu option for subject teachers at all." Root cause: `nav_permission_map['result_management']` was gated on `examinations.view` while the actual screen it points at (`school.report-cards.index`, shared with `basic_report_cards`) checks `report_cards.view` — a subject teacher holds the looser permission (needed for score entry) but not the stricter one, so the nav rendered a link to a screen they'd immediately 404 on. Fixed by matching both feature keys to the same real permission the screen itself checks.
- Issues/Bugs: while debugging, discovered the two tests originally written for this in `ReportCardWorkflowTest.php` were unreliable: that file's `schoolWithMember()` helper never seeds a `Plan`, so `School::hasFeature()` is always false there regardless of the fix, making any nav-visibility assertion in that file meaningless. Traced via temporary `Log::info()` instrumentation directly in the nav partial (removed before commit) after several static-analysis hypotheses about RBAC module-gating turned out to be red herrings.
- Resolution: moved both tests to `PlanFeatureNavTest.php`, which has proper `Plan`-seeding infrastructure, using its existing `schoolOnPlan()` helper. Both pass; full `PlanFeatureNavTest` (40 passed) and `ReportCardWorkflowTest` (35 passed) re-run clean.

### Knowledge Base scoped to what each viewing role can actually do (2026-07-22, commit `9e3ceed`)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user report: "Knowdlge base showing under other staff should be related to the things the user can do only." `config('skulos.knowledge_base_start_routes')` restructured from `route => label` to `route => ['label' => ..., 'permission' => ...]`, each entry's permission hand-matched to the exact one its destination controller checks (17 entries cross-checked). `School\KnowledgeBaseController::index()` now filters an article out of a user's own Help list when they lack the associated permission, and `show()` 404s (not 403, matching the standing "don't leak existence" rule) on a direct link to a restricted article. An article with no `start_route` (general orientation content) always shows.
- Issues/Bugs: the natural `config("skulos.knowledge_base_start_routes.{$article->start_route}.permission")` dot-notation lookup in `show()` silently broke, since `start_route` values like `school.students.create` contain dots themselves, which Laravel's `Arr::get()` misparses as nested keys.
- Resolution: switched to plain array access (`config(...)[$article->start_route]['permission'] ?? null`) instead of dot notation.
- QA: `KnowledgeBaseTest` extended, 14 passed including a new case proving a subject teacher sees general orientation content but not a permission-gated article, by URL or by listing.

### Subject Insights: a scoped Reports & Analytics screen for class/subject teachers (2026-07-22, commit `4f85b8b`)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user request: "report and analytics should have subject insights for class teacher and subject teachers with the necessary date, filter and sort options and calendar date option and term option." New `SubjectInsightsController`/`school.reports.subject-insights.index`, distinct from the existing Academic Insights (whole school, leadership only): scoped to only the (class, subject) pairs a teacher's own `TeachingAssignment` rows say they actually teach, the identical scoping `ExaminationController::allowedPairs()` already uses for score entry, so what a teacher can see here always matches what they can enter scores for. Term, class, and subject filters, sortable columns, gated on the same `examinations.view` every class/subject teacher already holds.
- New Foundation plan feature `subject_insights`: added to the plan feature catalog, `nav_permission_map`, and `PlanSeeder`'s `$foundationFeatures`; every already-provisioned school's `Plan.features` array resynced directly via tinker so the nav link shows immediately rather than only for schools provisioned after this change (the same "plan feature never reaches an already-provisioned school on its own" gotcha shape as tenant migrations/permissions/module states).
- QA: new `SubjectInsightsTest`, 5 passed, including scoping (a teacher never sees a class/subject they don't teach), filters, and the empty state for a teacher with no teaching assignment.

### Guardian contact details in global search now respect guardian_visibility (2026-07-22, commit `f30fb86`)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user report: "when subject teacher use the search for student/staff/parent function, it should blur the parent contact details if the admin has set it." `SearchController` previously returned a matching guardian's raw email/phone to any user holding `students.view`, including a scoped subject teacher, regardless of the school's `guardian_visibility` setting. Now computes the exact same `canSeeGuardianDetails` rule already used by `StudentController::show()`/`ParentDirectoryController` (a class teacher/assistant always sees it; a subject teacher only if the school opted into "both"), server side, and the search view masks the Contact column to "Restricted" when it doesn't apply. The guardian query itself was already scoped by `TeacherScope`/`FamilyScope` from an earlier audit finding, so this only closes the contact-detail leak, not a broader access leak.
- QA: `GlobalSearchTest` extended, 3 new cases: a subject teacher sees a guardian's name but not their contact by default, sees it when the school allows "both," and a class teacher always sees it regardless of the setting.

### Auto-provisioned login default password corrected to match the stated convention (2026-07-22, commit `3022a76`)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user report (items 142/143 of this batch): "students are to use their admission numbers as username and the default pass is password123... for parents, can use email or phone and the default pass of password123." `config('skulos.default_student_password')`/`default_parent_password'` were actually `Student@123`/`Parent@123`, never matching the stated convention or `schools:seed-demo-data`'s own `DEMO_PASSWORD`.
- Issues/Bugs: before changing the default, audited every already-provisioned school's real student/guardian logins (`Hash::check()` against both the old and new default across all 9 provisioned schools) to check whether a live reset was also needed.
- Resolution: every existing account was created via the demo seeder and already used `password123`, so only the config default itself needed correcting, nothing to reset. Also proved parent sign in works with either the guardian's email or phone against the same account, not just whichever was used to create it.
- QA: `StudentScreenTest` updated (two pre-existing tests asserted the old default literally), plus a new phone-login assertion. Full suite re-run clean, 33 passed.

Investigated `Storage::url()`/`config('filesystems.php')` (`'url' => env('APP_URL').'/storage'`), confirmed `APP_URL=http://localhost:8000` in `.env` matches the dev server, checked for hardcoded domain strings and CSV export content for embedded links; found no reproducible lead or concrete failing case. ## Architecture, deployment, and mobile UX pass (2026-07-22, user-directed: "any recommendation(s) for me based on architecture, design, ui ux, etc" → "proceed with all")

### `php artisan skulos:resync`: one command for three previously-manual "reach every school" gotchas (2026-07-22, commit `9f5af51`)
- Status: Done
- Notes: this tracker documents three separate incidents where a catalog change (a tenant migration, an `extra_permissions` entry, a `module_states` row) never reached an already-provisioned school on its own, each requiring a specific manual command or tinker script remembered after the fact. New `php artisan skulos:resync` wraps all three: tenant migrations and a wildcard-role permission resync run by default (both purely additive), module state resync from each school's current plan is opt-in via `--modules` since it can revert state, matching `BillingController::assignPlan()`'s own reset-to-plan-defaults behaviour.
- The wildcard-role resync closes a real gap in the original documented fix pattern: a plain "template has it, school role doesn't" diff cannot tell a genuinely new permission apart from one a school admin intentionally unchecked via Roles & Permissions, both look identical as a missing row. `RoleManagementController::updateRolePermissions()` now `touch()`es the role on every save, and the resync only ever adds a permission whose own `created_at` is strictly after the role's last deliberate edit, so an intentional removal is never silently undone.
- Also, unlike `assignPlan()`'s own logic, the `--modules` resync checks for an active `SchoolAddOn` before disabling a module not in the current plan, so a module granted as an add-on beyond the plan survives the resync rather than being silently revoked, closing a latent bug `assignPlan()` itself still has.
- QA: new `SkulosResyncCommandTest`, 5 passed, including the additive-vs-intentional-removal case and the add-on-preservation case.

### Deployment readiness audit: two real gaps found and documented (2026-07-22)
- Status: Done
- Notes: `DEPLOYMENT.md` was already thorough (cron, tenant migrations, self-hosted licensing) but never mentioned `php artisan storage:link`, despite school branding logos, report card stamps/signatures, and chat attachments all depending on it (`FILESYSTEM_DISK=local`, the `public` disk, no S3 configured). Added to both the main cPanel section and the self-hosted install runbook, including the fallback if a host disables PHP's `symlink()`. Also confirmed no queue worker/daemon is currently needed (nothing in the app implements `ShouldQueue` yet, every mail send runs synchronously inline) and documented that as a known scaling ceiling to revisit later, not a gap to fix now.

### Attendance, Score entry, and Term Broadsheets made genuinely usable on a phone (2026-07-22, commit `b15e7ec`)
- Status: Done
- Notes: added a shared `.table-stack` CSS pattern (below true phone width, a wide table collapses from a horizontally-scrolled grid into one bordered card per row, each cell labelled from its own column header via a `data-label` attribute since `thead` hides) and applied it to the three widest, most frequently used data tables in the product: the attendance register, the score entry grid (one column per assessment component), and Term Broadsheets (one column per subject). Verified with real Playwright screenshots at 375px viewport width against seeded demo data on a throwaway school, not just reasoned about from the CSS, then the throwaway school was fully cleaned up.
- Issues/Bugs: the full, unfiltered regression suite this work triggered (run as its own standing recommendation, see below) surfaced one real leftover: `TeacherScopeTest` still expected a 404 from the Create login routes for an out-of-scope student, but the `students.manage_settings` gate added earlier this same day now blocks a class teacher from those routes entirely regardless of scope, so 403 is the correct response.
- Resolution: updated the two affected assertions; not a regression, a stale expectation from an earlier fix the same session.
- QA: new assertions in `AttendanceScreenTest`, `ExaminationScreenTest`, `StudentReportsTest` confirming the `.table-stack` markup and `data-label` attributes render; full suite re-run clean.

### Stat card grids fixed for phone width; messaging FAB given real bottom clearance (2026-07-22, commit `8430123`)
- Status: Done
- Notes: screenshot review of the mobile work above surfaced two further, real issues. Several 4/3-card stat rows (Attendance index and dashboard, Term Broadsheets, Assignments, Performance Dashboard, the platform admin dashboard) used a hardcoded inline `grid-template-columns` instead of the shared `.cols-4`/`.cols-3` utility classes, so they never picked up the app's own existing responsive collapse at all and stayed cramped at 2 narrow columns even at phone width, wrapping a longer value like a student's name across several lines. Converted all of them to the shared classes and extended `.cols-4` to collapse to one column each below true phone width, matching the collapse `.cols-3`/`.cols-2` already had.
- Also gave the fixed bottom-right messaging FAB real clearance from a page's own bottom content (extra `.content` bottom padding at phone width), confirmed via screenshot that a form's final Save/Upload button no longer renders underneath it. A transient overlap with whatever content happens to scroll past during an active scroll remains, the same ordinary tradeoff any persistent floating action button has (Gmail's compose button, WhatsApp's chat bubble behave identically); not engineered around further.
- Checked broadly (~60 files containing a `<table>`) for other genuinely wide, many-column, daily-browsing tables that would benefit from the same `.table-stack` treatment as the three above; found none — the rest are either several separate narrow tables in one file, report card print/PDF templates (a fixed document layout that should not collapse), or already narrow enough not to need it.
- QA: new `class="grid cols-4"` assertions in `AttendanceScreenTest`, `StudentReportsTest`.

### Security review of the day's Knowledge Base / Subject Insights / Search / password changes (2026-07-22)
- Status: Done, no findings
- Notes: ran the project's `/security-review` flow against the diff for Knowledge Base scoping, Subject Insights, search guardian masking, and the login default password fix. One candidate (the password default itself as a weak/shared credential) was investigated in depth and ruled out at confidence 2/10: the diff only changes the literal string, the underlying design (a shared, admin-editable, out-of-band default with a real `must_change_password` enforcement gate verified to block every route until cleared) predates this change and was equally guessable before it. Everything else checked out clean: `KnowledgeBaseController::show()` correctly 404s rather than leaking existence, `SubjectInsightsController`'s filters only narrow an already-scoped collection with no IDOR path, and the search masking is enforced server side on top of an already-scoped query.

### Full, unfiltered regression suite run as the closing gate for this whole pass (2026-07-22)
- Status: Done
- Notes: user-directed standing recommendation ("a full, unfiltered regression run before your next real deployment"), run twice: once as a baseline before the architecture/deployment/mobile work started (surfaced the `TeacherScopeTest` issue above), once again after every fix in this section landed. Final result: **1014 passed, 0 failed, 3977 assertions**, covering the entire product, not a filtered subset.

## SSS streams, mutually exclusive subjects, and a large report card correctness/polish pass (2026-07-23/24, live report against real greenfield data)

### SSS 1-3 split into real Science & Technology / Arts & Humanities / Commercial streams (`sss:setup-streams`)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-23
- Notes: user report against a real "Generated Report Card" download: every SSS student was scored on all 24 catalog subjects with no real stream separation. New `php artisan sss:setup-streams {school}` (idempotent, re-runnable): trims the class level subject list to an explicit `config('skulos.sss_shared_subjects')` allowlist, creates/reuses 3 `ClassArm` streams per SSS class (`config('skulos.sss_stream_subjects')`), splits students round robin by admission number, re-points/cleans `Score`/`SubjectRemark` rows to the student's real stream. `ClassArm::offeredSubjects()` (new) is additive (class level ∪ arm level), not "arm's own list or class fallback" — a first draft got this backwards and was caught by a failing test before ever touching real data.
- Issues/Bugs: (1) matching SSS classes via the `default_subjects.ss.match` substring keywords (`'ss1'`, `'ss 1'`, ...) also matched "JSS 1/2/3" (substring collision), caught on a throwaway school before ever running against greenfield. (2) An initial `classLevelSubjectNames()` computed the class level list as "catalog minus whatever a stream claims" (an implicit leftover); trimming Arts & Humanities from 9 to 6 stream subjects (see cap fix below) silently promoted the 3 dropped subjects (Music/French/Arabic) to shared-by-every-stream instead of just dropping them. (3) `Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Collection::only()` overrides the base `Collection::only()` to mean "these primary keys" not "these array keys" — `ElectiveGroups::resolve()`'s first draft used `$byName->keyBy('name')->only($group)` and silently matched nothing, so mutual exclusivity (see below) did nothing on the first real run.
- Resolution: (1) strict `/^s{2,3}\s?\d/` prefix regex against the class name itself, not the section-suggestion keywords. (2) explicit `config('skulos.sss_shared_subjects')` allowlist replaces the leftover computation. (3) `ElectiveGroups::resolve()` rewritten to `$offeredSubjects->filter(fn($s) => in_array($s->name, $group, true))` instead of `keyBy()->only()`.
- QA: `SetupSssStreamsTest` (5 tests, including the JSS-never-mistaken-for-SSS regression and the additive-offeredSubjects regression), re-run against real greenfield after every fix (backed up first to `greenfield-backup-before-sss-streams.sqlite`).

### No student scored on more than one of a mutually exclusive subject group, school wide
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-24
- Notes: user directive: "no student can take both Christian Religious Studies and Islamic Studies, they have to select one. Same as the languages, student must only have one of Yoruba, Hausa or Igbo... update the seeded data for all students across the school with this too." New `config('skulos.mutually_exclusive_subjects')` (`[[CRS, Islamic Studies], [Yoruba, Hausa, Igbo]]`, school wide since JSS also carries CRS/Islamic Studies as separate subjects) and `App\Support\ElectiveGroups::resolve()`: a class/stream's own subject list is still a real MENU with every option offered, this only constrains what one student is ever actually scored on, honouring an existing score over a deterministic (student id modulo) pick so a re-run never silently flips an existing choice. Consumed by both `sss:setup-streams` (collapses an already-double-scored student down to one, cleans the other's Score/SubjectRemark rows) and `schools:populate-scores` (skips generating a second score for an already-satisfied group, same cleanup for pre-existing violations).
- Issues/Bugs: none beyond the `Collection::only()` bug already covered above (`ElectiveGroups` is the shared helper both commands consume).
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: new tests in both `SetupSssStreamsTest` and `PopulateStudentScoresTest` (including a real 2-teachers-worth-of-double-scoring cleanup case, confirming the lower subject id / existing score wins deterministically). Verified against real greenfield: 320/320 students checked, 0 violations across both groups after a real re-run.

### Report card: font sizes/spacing tightened, genuinely fits one A4 page, section headers centered, physical/attendance data populated, class teacher contact
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-23
- Notes: several rounds of direct user screenshot feedback on a real generated report card:
  1. **Page 1 blank space / page 2 overflow (first pass)**: `break-inside:avoid` applied to `.rc-table` as a whole (not just its rows) told the browser to push the entire table to page 2 if it didn't fit in whatever remained of page 1, exactly backwards from the desired "start right after the header, break cleanly between rows" behaviour every other template already has. Fixed by scoping `break-inside:avoid` to `.rc-table tr` only.
  2. **Still overflowing after that fix, fonts/spacing "too much and big"**: every size on the page was reduced once (~13-14px down to ~11-12px, tighter block/table margins, a `.rc-sheet p{margin:...}` reset for the browser's own default paragraph margins the header's tagline `<p>` had never had reset). Measured the actual rendered height in print media via Playwright afterwards anyway (a real 11 subject Arts & Humanities student, with behavioural traits + physical stats + the new class teacher contact rows all on) and found it still came to **~1184px against a ~1047px A4 content budget**, genuinely too tall, not a false break-avoid artifact. Root cause: every size on this page is set in `rem` (relative to the document root, not to `.rc-sheet`'s own font-size), so an earlier `.rc-sheet{font-size:.92em}` print rule had been almost a no-op the whole time. Fixed with `html{font-size:13.5px}` (print media only, was the browser default 16px) plus a further round of individual paddings, which scales every rem measurement on the page together in proportion — brought the same student down to **949px**, comfortably one page, confirmed via an actual generated PDF (not just a screenshot).
  3. **Section headers centered**: `.rc-block h3` (not `.rc-block > h3`, the Behavioural Traits/Physical & Attendance headers are a grandchild via the `.rc-twocol` wrapper, not a direct child) now text-align centered, matching the site wide "section headers centered" design rule that had not yet been applied to a report card's own block headers.
  4. **Physical & Attendance data populated for all learners**: `schools:populate-scores` extended to fill `height`/`weight`/`club` (per student, once per term, independently of the other already-filled remark fields) and `terms.total_school_days` (once per term, whole school). Height/weight derived from the student's own real age (`85 + age*5.2` / `13 + age*2.3`cm/kg, clamped to a plausible child/teen range), club picked deterministically from a new `config('skulos.demo_clubs')` catalog.
  5. **"Days school was open" is a real calculation, not a guess (user correction the same day)**: "this is calculated from the term start date and end date. excluding the weekends... and other public holidays, mid term break set by the admin - admin can also check the calculated date and make adjustment to the number auto calculated." New `AttendanceService::schoolDaysBetween()` reuses the exact same weekend/holiday/break rule `nonSchoolDayReason()` already enforces for attendance marking, so "a school day" means the same thing in both places. `AcademicsController::updateTermDates()` auto calculates when the days field is submitted blank (a genuine admin override, typed explicitly, is honoured and survives future date edits); the Sessions & Terms settings page pre-fills the field with a live calculated preview so the admin can review/adjust before saving, per the user's own description. Real greenfield terms recalculated from their actual dates once this landed (was a `mt_rand(58,65)` placeholder before the calculator existed).
  6. **Class teacher name and phone on the report card**: user was explicit this is CONTENT inside the existing Physical & Attendance column, not a new draggable header/info layout block, with its own on/off toggle (`class_teacher_contact`, distinct from the existing name only `class_teacher` identity block) on the Report Card Settings page — corrected mid-implementation after the first plan considered a layout-block toggle instead. `ReportCardService::forStudent()` threads the class arm's `class_teacher_staff_id` through to `Staff::phone`.
  7. **`rc-photo`/`rc-photo-placeholder` CSS was missing from `class-print.blade.php`** (present in `render.blade.php` from an earlier student-photo pass but never mirrored to the bulk class print view) — added, so bulk printing a whole class no longer silently drops the student photo layout block.
- Issues/Bugs: `Term.total_school_days` was not in `Term::$fillable` — `$term->update(['total_school_days' => ...])` was silently no-opping (Laravel drops a non-fillable attribute rather than erroring), caught by a new test asserting the value actually persisted.
- Resolution: added `total_school_days` to `Term::$fillable`.
- QA: new tests in `AttendanceTest` (weekend + holiday + ranged mid term break exclusion), `PopulateStudentScoresTest` (physical stats + total_school_days population, independent-of-already-set-height), `SetupSssStreamsTest`/`PopulateStudentScoresTest` (elective group enforcement, see above). Verified end to end against real greenfield via Playwright: logged in as a real staff account, rendered a real SSS1 Arts & Humanities student's report card, generated an actual PDF and confirmed one page, 11 real subjects (5 shared + 4 always + 1 religious choice + 1 language choice), centered section headers, class teacher name/phone rendering, height/weight/club/days-open all populated. Full backend suite re-run clean throughout.

### Incidental: pre-existing `MessagingWidgetTest` false failure found and fixed during this pass's full suite run
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-24
- Notes: not caused by anything in this pass (confirmed via a git-stash comparison against the prior commit); a 2026-07-22 CSS comment in `layouts/app.blade.php` (`/* The messaging FAB (messaging-widget.blade.php's .mw-fab) is a fixed... */`, explaining a bottom padding clearance fix) renders unconditionally on every dashboard regardless of role, so `assertDontSee('mw-fab', false)`'s bare substring check always failed even when the widget itself correctly did not render for a role with no messaging permission — the same class of bug as an earlier "Bulk Generate Report Cards" nav text containing the substring "Report Cards" tripping an unrelated `assertDontSee('Report Cards')` assertion (also found and fixed this session, `PlanFeatureNavTest`, wrong root cause: the new bulk generate nav link was missing the `hasFeature('basic_report_cards')` plan gate the sibling "Pending review" link already had, so a school on a plan without the report cards feature at all could still see it).

### Finance: class-scoped fee items, bulk CSV upload, fee approval chain, term switcher, class-level payment status (2026-07-25/26, commits `f20b14a`)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-25
- Notes: fee items can now be scoped to specific classes via a checkbox picker (`fee_item_school_class` pivot, empty = applies to every class, backward compatible); a fee schedule matrix table renders above the add-item form once any fee items exist. Bulk CSV upload + downloadable template added (`FeeItemController::template()`/`bulkUpload()`, additive not destructive on unknown-class rows). Term picker offers a synthetic `upcoming:{year}` option, only when the current term is the last of its session and the next session doesn't exist yet, resolved into a real session+term on submit via `AcademicDefaults::createSessionWithTerms()` (extracted from `AcademicsController` for reuse). Admin can configure a fee approval chain (`fee_approval_enabled` + `fee_approval_chain` Settings, entries are `role:slug` or `staff:{id}`, staff picked via the app-wide searchable-select enhancement); a pending fee item is hidden from parents until approved (`FeeItem::APPROVAL_PENDING/APPROVED/REJECTED`, atomic query-builder transition guard against a two-approvers race, same pattern `PaymentController::applyDecision()` already used). Payments page adds a class-level fee status table (one row per class ARM/stream, not per class — found live 2026-07-25/26 that a class with multiple streams, e.g. Year 1 Gold/Silver or SSS's Science/Arts/Commercial, was shown as one merged row) with a "Nudge parents" action (one Notification per guardian, not per student), and an admin-configurable payment due-date countdown with a daily parent popup reminder (mirrors the existing subscription-popup pattern, localStorage dismiss-once-per-day key). A `finance_active_term_id` Setting plus a `?term_id=` query switcher lets admin/parents view a different term's status while the Setting controls what parents see by default; the "default term" select has an explicit "school's current term (automatic)" option so an unset Setting doesn't cosmetically look like a term was chosen. `FeeBalanceService` added for the payment popup's per-guardian outstanding calc (deliberately not used to refactor `PaymentController::classStatus()`/`nudgeUnpaid()`, which keep their own bulk-optimized inline versions to avoid N+1 queries in a loop). Populated real fee data for greenfield academy (6 class bands, Tuition/Books/Uniform/Sportswear/Cardigan plus flat PTA Levy/End of Year Party) for both First Term 2026/2027 (upcoming) and Third Term 2025/2026 (current, per direct user request "add seeded data for current term fees too").
- Issues/Bugs: `nav_permission_map` had zero entries for the 5 finance feature keys, hiding Finance from parents regardless of real permission (fixed, mapped to `finance.view`). `BankAccount::$fillable` omitted `is_active`, silently no-opping `toggleActive()`. `FeeItemController::store()`/`update()`'s `term_id` validation rule included `'string'`, rejecting the raw PHP int Laravel's test client sends (a real browser form would string-coerce it) — removed, the resolver closure already handles both. `SchoolClass` picker for class-scoping included a retired class — filtered to `active()`.
- Resolution: see above per bug. 52 FinanceTest cases passing (grew from ~22 across this and the prior session's Finance work).
- QA: full suite 1088 passed / 0 failed at close. Verified live on greenfield via Playwright (class-status table showing all 14 real class arms including all 9 SSS stream rows split correctly, term switcher, payment reminder card, bank accounts, fee schedule matrix).

Same flat day/period-per-row CSV shape and `timetable.view` gate as the page itself; PDF stays covered by the existing Print button, same as the master export.

### Notification deep links, and notifying the applicant when their own profile change is decided (2026-07-25/26, commit `469b737`)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-25
- Notes: `config('skulos.notification_deep_links')` maps a notification category to the screen where it can actually be acted on (`Notification::deepLinkUrl()`); wired into both the header bell panel and the Notification Center's "Take me there" link. Deliberately a category-to-route map, not a specific record, since every notification recipient is already pre-filtered to someone eligible to act by the time the row is written, so no extra permission check is layered on here. A category with nothing to act on (birthday, anniversary) stays unlinked. Separately, `ProfileController::approve()`/`reject()`/`bulkApprove()`/`bulkReject()` now notify the applicant themselves (`profile_change_approved`/`profile_change_rejected` categories, in-app only, unlike the password-reset flow's email — a profile-change applicant is by definition already an authenticated member who can see an in-app notification).
- Issues/Bugs: none.
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: NotificationCenterTest (8 tests) and ProfileTest (16 tests, including a new bulk-approve/bulk-reject notification test) both green.

### Menu icon audit: every genuinely different nav item now has its own icon (2026-07-25/26, commit `393a87f`)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-25
- Notes: user report (FEEDBACK LIST.docx): "recheck all the menu icons, as I don't want similar icons representing items there only if it means same thing." Audited all 57 real nav links against the 34-icon `icon.blade.php` catalog via a Playwright DOM script (grouped links by rendered SVG path string); found 14 groups of genuinely different screens silently sharing one icon (worst: Enter Scores/Grading/Term Broadsheets/Term Calculation/Bulk Generate/Pending Review/Assessment Structure/At Risk Thresholds all sharing one bar-chart icon). Added 28 new distinct glyphs (compass, sliders, field, widgets, workload, approve, schedule, terms, narrative, toggle, fee, renew, history, house, campus, builder, traits, structure, broadsheet, scale, pencil, percent, copies, inbox, warning, parents, insight, stats) and reassigned each affected `$builtRoutes`/`$link()` call site in `nav-school.blade.php`. A shared icon is left alone only where the two labels point at the literal same route (Staff Portal/Teacher Portal, Student Fields/Parent Settings/Staff Settings/Custom Fields, Calendar/Academic Calendar) or are genuinely the same category of screen (Report settings/Mail Settings, both plain config screens) — matching the "only if it means same thing" carve-out in the report.
- Issues/Bugs: none found after the fix (re-ran the same detection script; remaining 3 shared-icon groups are all defensible per above).
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: PlanFeatureNavTest (40 tests) green; verified visually via Playwright screenshot of the expanded Reports & Analytics group, all 10 reassigned icons render distinctly with no glitches.

The page (and the Students screen's own "View" link into it, which shares this same route) now shows class structure only: streams, class/assistant teacher, subjects and their teachers.

### Score entry: live total/grade calculation, 5 second autosave, auto-suggested remark (2026-07-25/26, commit `2d0eb3c`)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-25
- Notes: `ExaminationController`'s score entry screen now computes a live running total and grade client side as CA/exam components are typed (reading the same grading scale/bands the server uses), autosaves in the background every 5 seconds instead of requiring an explicit save click per row, and pre-fills a suggested remark from the school's own comment bank (DB-backed, not `config()` read live, per the standing "everything must be a DB-backed layer" rule) based on the computed grade.
- Issues/Bugs: none.

### Fee schedule matrix: optional flag, automated per-class totals, term switcher, section filter (2026-07-26, commit `91797f3`)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-26
- Notes: four separate pieces of direct user feedback on the fee schedule table folded into one build. (1) `is_optional` boolean added to `FeeItem` (migration, `$fillable`/`casts()`), a checkbox on the fee item form; the schedule now sorts non-optional items first (`usort` on `isOptional`) instead of an arbitrary/creation order that had been interleaving repeated "X (Optional)" rows through the list. (2) Two automated total rows per class, "Total including Optional Items" and "Total excluding Optional Items", computed by summing raw floats per class before formatting, not typed in by anyone. (3) `/finance/fee-items` previously rendered every term's schedule stacked on the page at once; `FeeItemController::index()` now resolves a single `$scheduleTerm` (from `?term_id=` or `Term::current()`, mirroring the existing Payments page term switcher pattern) via a small GET form. (4) A second GET form filters the schedule to one section (Nursery/Primary/Secondary), preserving the term selection via a hidden input. `feeSchedule()` was renamed `feeScheduleForTerm()` and rescoped to one term + one already-filtered class collection rather than looping every term.
- Issues/Bugs: Blade's `{{ }}` HTML-escapes JSON inside an attribute (a test asserting a raw `"key":"value"` substring via `assertSee(..., false)` failed even though the browser correctly unescapes it before JS reads `dataset`) — fixed by asserting on visible text instead. Two tests using `assertDontSee` against page-wide text produced false failures because the page legitimately has two tables at different scopes (the term-filtered schedule and an unfiltered "browse every fee item" list) — fixed by asserting on `$response->viewData(...)` directly instead of scraping the whole page.
- Resolution: see above per bug.
- QA: FinanceTest grew to 56 passing at this point; real greenfield fee items backfilled live (`is_optional=true` on the School/Sports Wear/Cardigan "(Optional)" rows, 36 rows) and `tenants:migrate` run across all 9 provisioned schools for the new column.

### Payments: per-class payment drill-down, hiding the raw fee-item breakdown from parents, splitting one payment across multiple children (2026-07-26, commit `f7296b7`)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-26
- Notes: three pieces of direct user feedback.
  1. **Per-class drill-down**: "let's add the access to view parents who have paid under the Payments section so that if the user clicks on the class, it shows all the students who have paid, amount paid, balance, date paid, and same for those who are yet to pay." New `PaymentController::classDetail()`/`payments/class-detail.blade.php`, reached by clicking a class row or its new "View" button on the existing class-status table. Reuses the same `FeeItem::appliesToClass()` owed calculation already used by `classStatus()`/`nudgeUnpaid()`; "date paid" is `Payment::verified_at` (real datetime set when a bursar verifies), shown as "Not paid yet" when null.
  2. **Hide the raw breakdown from parents**: "parents should not be seeing the individual breakdown of the fees like this, just fee schedule is okay to be seen." The entire fee-items list card on `/finance/fee-items` (Name/Term/Amount/Classes/Installments/Status/View/Manage columns) is now wrapped in `@if($canManage)`, whole-card rather than gating individual controls, since the complaint was about the section existing at all for a parent, not specific buttons within it. Parents still see the schedule matrix above it, unaffected.
  3. **Split one payment across multiple children**: "for parents with more than one child, add the option of check box to select in case the parent wants the payment to cover more than one child." `payments.store` now accepts `student_ids[]` (was a single `student_id`); when a parent has more than one linked child the Submit Payment form shows a checkbox per child instead of a dropdown, with a live "this will be split evenly: NGN X each for N children" hint. The controller creates one `Payment` row per selected child (each still individually verifiable/rejectable, balances/records all stay per-student as before), the total amount split evenly with the rounding remainder landing on the last child so the shares always sum back exactly, and all rows in one submission share a new `payment_group_id` column (migration, nullable, null for the ordinary single-child case) so the records screen can show they came from one evidence upload. A crafted student id outside the parent's own family is dropped from the batch, not rejected outright, so the legitimate children in a mixed submission still get recorded.
- Issues/Bugs: none found this pass.
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: FinanceTest grew to 61 passing (class-detail drill-down, parent-hides-breakdown, and three multi-child-split tests: even split + shared group id, single-child has no group id, foreign student id dropped not trusted). Full unfiltered `php artisan test` re-run clean, 1103 passed / 0 failed. `tenants:migrate` run across all 9 provisioned schools for the new `payment_group_id` column.

### Report Settings: admin toggle for the score entry auto suggested remark (2026-07-26, commit `a175cea`)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-26
- Notes: user request: "on/off toggle for the score entry remark everywhere it appears." New `ReportCardController::SETTING_SUGGESTED_REMARK_ENABLED` tenant Setting (`score_entry_suggested_remark_enabled`, on by default), same pattern as the existing review stage toggle (`report_cards.assign_review` permission to change, a checkbox on Report Settings that self submits). `ExaminationController::index()` reads it once and gates both the server side prefilled suggestion (the `$suggestions` array) and the `commentBands` JSON feed the client side live re-suggestion reads; deliberately does NOT gate `gradingBands`, since the live total/grade calculation is a separate always on feature that must keep working regardless of this toggle.
- Issues/Bugs: none.
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: new `test_the_suggested_remark_can_be_turned_off_and_the_grade_calc_still_works` (ExaminationScreenTest) and two new ReportCardWorkflowTest cases (owner can toggle on/off, a teacher without `report_cards.assign_review` cannot). Full unfiltered `php artisan test` re-run clean, 1106 passed / 0 failed.

### Narrative Evaluation made per class per term, re-toggleable across terms (2026-07-26, commit `214ea43`)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-26
- Notes: user request: "per-class-per-term toggle, re-toggleable across terms." Narrative Evaluation (built 2026-07-18) was a single permanent `school_classes.evaluation_style` flag read live at render time, no term dimension: switching a class back off later would silently rewrite an earlier term's already-generated report cards too. New `ClassNarrativeTerm` model/table (`school_class_id` + `term_id`, unique pair, one row per class per term a school explicitly turned it on for) replaces `evaluation_style` as the live source of truth at every call site: `ReportCardController::show()`/`classPrint()`/`classEntry()`/`classEntryTemplate()`/`classEntryUpload()`, and `ExaminationController::allowedPairs()`/`narrativeClasses()` (both of which needed a `$termId` parameter threaded through, since neither previously carried term context). `SchoolClass::isNarrativeEvaluation()` replaced with `isNarrativeEvaluationForTerm(?int $termId)`. The Narrative Evaluation settings page gained a term switcher (`?term_id=`, defaults to the school's current term); saving only ever creates/deletes `ClassNarrativeTerm` rows for the one term being viewed, never touching any other term. The one-off `skulos:backfill-narrative-evaluation` console command (2026-07-18, "populate all Creche/Reception across the schools") was rewritten the same way, targeting the school's current term via `firstOrCreate` instead of the old permanent column write, so it stays meaningful rather than silently becoming a no-op. The new table's own migration backfills any class already marked narrative under the old column into whichever term was current at migration time, so an in-progress term's report cards do not silently flip back to scored the moment the migration lands; every other term (past or future) starts blank.
- Issues/Bugs: none.
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: `NarrativeEvaluationTest` grew from 10 to 11 (new `test_the_toggle_is_independent_per_term` regression, existing tests rewritten to create `ClassNarrativeTerm` rows instead of setting the old column). Full unfiltered `php artisan test` re-run clean, 1107 passed / 0 failed. `tenants:migrate` run across all 9 provisioned schools for the new `class_narrative_terms` table.

### Submit Payment: "All Fees" option with a full or partial payment plan and a live balance (2026-07-26, commit `6e96b2e`)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-26
- Notes: user request: "add an item of all fees... based on the child or children selected, the parent should be asked if they want to make full payment or partial payment (this is only for non optional items) then it calculate the total amount to be paid, if parent select partial payment, ask them how many percent (25, 50, 75, 90%), user can edit the amount... if parent has made payment before, show the balance left... and then show Complete Payment button." A new synthetic `all_fees` option sits in the "what is this for" picker alongside General payment and individual fee items; picking it reveals a payment plan card computed live from data attributes shipped on each student checkbox (`data-owed`/`data-paid`, from a new `FeeBalanceService::nonOptionalOwedForClass()` restricted to `is_optional = false` fee items, and the existing `paidByStudent()`, same "every verified payment ever, not term-scoped" simplification the rest of Finance already uses). Combined across whichever children are ticked (reusing the multi-child selection built 2026-07-26 earlier the same day): Full Payment fills in the outstanding balance (owed minus already paid, clamped to 0), Partial Payment reveals 25/50/75/90 percent buttons that set the amount to that share of the balance, and the amount field stays editable regardless of which button was clicked. The Full Payment button relabels itself "Complete Payment" once the child has any prior verified payment. Submitting `fee_item_id=all_fees` is treated the same as an empty "General payment" (`fee_item_id` merged to null before validation) since it is a client side amount calculator, not a real catalog row.
- Issues/Bugs: none.
- Resolution: n/a.
- QA: two new FinanceTest cases (the create page ships the right non-optional-owed/paid data attributes for a real class with one non-optional and one optional fee item plus a prior part payment; submitting `all_fees` saves as `fee_item_id` null). Full unfiltered `php artisan test` re-run clean, 1109 passed / 0 failed. Verified live on greenfield: real parent `nkechi.ade@gmail.com`'s two children show real computed non-optional owed totals (₦59,500 / ₦83,500) with `paid="0"`, the All Fees option and payment plan markup both render.

### Announcements: each row now opens a full read page instead of a truncated preview (2026-07-26, commit `0321fe7`)
- Status: Done
- Started: 2026-07-26
- Notes: user report: "one should be able to click on each one so as to read it fully" (the index list truncated every body to 90 characters via `Str::limit`, with no way to open the rest). New `AnnouncementController::show()`/`announcements/show.blade.php`; each index row's title now links to it. `Announcement::body` is validated plain text (no rich HTML), so the show page renders it via Blade's normal auto-escaping `{{ }}` with `white-space:pre-wrap` to preserve line breaks, no sanitizer needed.
- Issues/Bugs: a test comparing the rendered page against the literal POSTed body failed by one trailing space, `TrimStrings` middleware trims trailing whitespace from POSTed text server side before it is ever saved.
- Resolution: test assertion compares against `trim($longBody)` instead.
- QA: AnnouncementComposeTest (5 tests) green.

## 2. Branding Decisions (reference — already decided, no action needed)

- [x] Product name: **SKULOS** (evolved from "School Management System" → "SchoolOS" → final: **SKULOS**)
- [x] Tagline: *"SKULOS — The Complete Operating System for Modern Schools"*
- [x] Positioning: an **Education Operating System / Education ERP**, not a "School Management System"
- [x] Future sub-brand naming convention agreed: `SKULOS <Product>` — e.g. SKULOS Learn (LMS), SKULOS Assess (CBT/proctoring), SKULOS AI, SKULOS Sites (website builder), SKULOS Pay, SKULOS Connect (comms), SKULOS Mobile, SKULOS Market (marketplace), SKULOS Analytics

## 3. Confirmed Technology Stack (decisions made — track as infra setup tasks)

- [ ] Backend: **Laravel 12** (chosen over Django, ASP.NET, Spring Boot, NestJS)
- [ ] Frontend: **Next.js + React + TypeScript** (chosen over plain React, Vue, Angular)
- [ ] UI layer: **Tailwind CSS + ShadCN UI + Framer Motion**
- [ ] Database: **MySQL** for both dev and prod (PostgreSQL was the initial recommendation but changed to MySQL for cPanel-hosting compatibility; Postgres support may be revisited later)
- [ ] Queue & cache: **Redis**
- [ ] File storage: **AWS S3-compatible storage**, with a local storage option
- [ ] Search: **Laravel Scout + Meilisearch**
- [ ] Auth: **Laravel Sanctum**, single identity / SSO across all modules; future OAuth, Google login, Microsoft login, 2FA, passwordless auth
- [ ] Deployment: **Docker, Linux, Nginx, Cloudflare**; cPanel-compatible deployment path for smaller/self-hosted customers
- [ ] Multi-tenancy model: **Hybrid** — one shared Platform Database (schools, billing, licenses, marketplace, domains, global settings, platform users) + **one dedicated database per school** (dedicated storage, backups, configuration, branding)
- [ ] Domain strategy: subdomain per school (e.g. `greenfield.skulos.app`) with optional custom domain (e.g. `portal.school.com`)
- [x] **Deployment targets (confirmed 2026-07-26):** both a live **cPanel** hosting environment (Laravel backend + MySQL — the original always-planned path) and **Vercel** (for the Next.js frontend) are to be considered/kept viable throughout development. Don't make architecture choices that lock out either target — e.g. avoid Vercel-only edge/serverless-only frontend patterns if they'd break a plain Node/static export path, and keep the Laravel API deployable on standard shared/VPS cPanel hosting.

## 4. Platform / Core Infrastructure Build Tracker

These are cross-cutting capabilities every module depends on — build these foundations first.

### 4.1 Multi-tenancy & provisioning
- [ ] Platform (central) database schema: schools, subscriptions, billing, licenses, domains, marketplace, super admin, support tickets, system-wide audit logs
- [ ] Per-school database provisioning pipeline (create DB, run migrations, seed defaults)
- [ ] Subdomain auto-assignment per school
- [ ] Custom domain support per school
- [ ] School cloning (duplicate a school's full configuration — sections, classes, grading, branding, workflows — excluding students/staff/transactions — into a new school instance)
- [ ] School Setup / Builder Wizard (guided onboarding — target: fully operational school in under ~15 minutes):
  - [ ] Step: School type selection (Nursery/Primary/Secondary/College/University — multi-select, one school can run several together)
  - [ ] Step: Curriculum selection (Nigerian, British/IGCSE, American, Montessori, Cambridge, Custom — open-ended)
  - [ ] Step: Logo & branding upload
  - [ ] Step: School information (name, motto, full address, email, mobile)

### 4.2 Identity, roles & access
- [ ] Single Sign-On: one identity per user across every module/portal the user has access to
- [ ] Role-Based Access Control engine with **configurable, not hardcoded, permissions**
- [ ] Full role catalogue to implement (each needs its own permission set):
  - [ ] Platform Super Admin
  - [ ] School Owner
  - [ ] School Admin
  - [ ] Vice Principal
  - [ ] Principal
  - [ ] Registrar
  - [ ] Examination Officer
  - [ ] Bursar
  - [ ] ICT Officer
  - [ ] Secretary
  - [ ] Teacher
  - [ ] Class Teacher
  - [ ] Subject Teacher
  - [ ] Librarian
  - [ ] Nurse
  - [ ] Store Keeper
  - [ ] Accountant
  - [ ] Parent (single account, multiple children — including children in different classes or different schools under the same ownership group)
  - [ ] Student
  - [ ] Security
  - [ ] Hostel Master
  - [ ] Transport Officer
  - [ ] Custom/user-defined roles
- [ ] Multi-school account switching (one login, switch between schools without re-authenticating) — owners of e.g. "Royal Nursery / Royal Primary / Royal College" managed from one account
- [ ] Feature/module toggle system: Enable / Disable / Hide / Read-only, settable at **Per School**, **Per Role**, and **Per User** granularity

### 4.3 Cross-cutting operational capabilities ("Article 3" bulk-everywhere principle)
- [ ] Bulk operations framework applied to every significant module:
  - [ ] Bulk Create / Single Create parity
  - [ ] Bulk Import (with downloadable templates per entity type)
  - [ ] Bulk Export
  - [ ] Bulk Update
  - [ ] Bulk Delete
  - [ ] Bulk Assign
  - [ ] Bulk Promote
  - [ ] Bulk Archive
  - [ ] Bulk Print
  - [ ] Bulk Email
  - [ ] Bulk SMS
  - [ ] Bulk Generate IDs
  - [ ] Bulk Generate Passwords
  - [ ] Bulk Admit
  - [ ] Bulk Graduate
  - [ ] Bulk Suspend
  - [ ] Bulk Restore
- [ ] Import data validation with row-level error reporting (e.g. "Row 15 — Admission Number already exists") and a downloadable corrected-file flow, not a generic "Import Failed"
- [ ] Downloadable bulk import templates for: Students, Parents, Staff, Subjects, Classes, Results, Attendance, Library Books, Inventory, Payments
- [ ] Audit trail engine, system-wide: records who, when, IP address, device, browser, old value, new value for every action affecting institutional data — nothing deleted without a trace
- [ ] Custom fields engine — schools can add arbitrary fields to core entities (e.g. Blood Group, Passport Number, Tribe, Bus Route) without code changes
- [ ] Workflow engine — configurable multi-step approval chains (e.g. Admission → Registrar approves → Principal approves → Finance approves → Student admitted), editable by schools
- [ ] Form builder — drag-and-drop creation of arbitrary forms (Visitor Form, Leave Form, Permission Slip, Transfer Form, Complaint Form, Disciplinary Form, Parent Feedback Form, Student Exit Form, etc.)
- [ ] Automation/rules engine — trigger-based automations (e.g. "Fee Paid → Send Receipt + Notify Parent + Notify Accountant"; "Attendance below 70% → Notify Parent + Notify Class Teacher")
- [ ] Document generator — template-based generation of: Admission Letter, Offer Letter, ID Card, Transcript, Certificate, Testimonial, Receipt, Invoice, Employment Letter, Promotion Letter, Character Certificate, Bonafide Letter
- [ ] Theme builder — per-school logo, primary/secondary colour, sidebar style, fonts, portal background, login page, without touching code (foundation for future White Label)
- [ ] Report builder — drag-and-drop custom report creation across all modules
- [ ] Universal export support — every report/module exportable to **PDF, Excel, CSV, and Word**
- [ ] Notification Center — single pane showing SMS / Email / WhatsApp / Push / In-App messages with delivery status, read status, failures, and retries
- [ ] Plugin Marketplace (WordPress-style) — install/uninstall optional modules (Library, Payroll, Transport, CBT, Hostel, Biometric, AI Assistant, etc.) without a full platform upgrade
- [ ] API Center — every module exposes secure REST APIs for future mobile apps, marketplace plugins, government systems, payment gateways, BI tools, and third-party integrations

Not rebuilding this list item by item here since the Build Log entry is the live, accurate account; short version:

## 5. Core Modules (mandatory — always available)

  - **Email address is optional**, not a required field
  - Extended profile: Admission record, Academic History, Medical info, Attendance, Behaviour, Library activity, Payments, Exam/CA history, Certificates, Guardian info, Transport, Hostel, Documents (Passport photo, Birth Certificate, State of Origin, Religion, Nationality, Blood Group, Genotype, Previous School, Emergency Contact)

  - Core fields: Staff ID Number, First/Last/Other Names, Gender, Class and/or Subjects taught, unique positions (Store Keeper, Accountant, ICT, Director, etc. — extensible list)

## 5b. Deferred to Later Build (moved out of Core Modules, 2026-07-08)

Moved out at the user's explicit direction so the first build phase can focus on the academic/people/communication backbone. These are **not cancelled** — they remain required, just sequenced later. Move back into section 5 (with a status update) when their build phase starts.

- [ ] **Admissions / Enrollment** — online application forms, entrance examinations conducted in-platform, CBT with webcam monitoring via LearnGuard integration
- [ ] **Finance** — school fees, discounts, scholarships, installment plans, automatic invoicing, receipts, expenses, income, payroll, budgets, ledger, bank accounts, payment gateway integration, bank transfer + proof-of-payment upload, refunds, financial reports & revenue analytics
- [ ] **Visitor Management**
- [ ] **School Assets Management**

Reasoning: these four are either commerce-adjacent (Finance, Admissions involve payment/external-integration surface area) or facilities-adjacent (Visitor, Assets) rather than part of the core academic/records/communication backbone every school needs on day one. Building Student/Staff/Parent records, academic structure, results, and communication first gives a coherent, testable vertical slice before layering in money-handling and facilities modules.

## 5c. Deferred Sub-Items Carved Out of Otherwise-Completed Builds

Unlike section 5b (whole modules never started), these are pieces explicitly left out of a build that otherwise shipped, each because of a hard technical constraint or genuine scope size, not an oversight. Track here so they surface again as later items get built rather than staying buried in a single Build Log entry's prose. Build back in when the blocking constraint is resolved or the user explicitly asks.

- [ ] **True AI-generated report card comments/summaries** — carved out of the Comment Bank / Intelligent Assessment & Report Card Workflow build (2026-07-12, see that Build Log entry). Hard constraint: no AI provider is configured anywhere in the app. The Comment Bank ships instead as a deterministic, DB-backed, score-range lookup (100 seed comments across 5 bands, admin-editable), which the user explicitly confirmed is the right scope for now ("this is not really an AI engine, just use the bank against the score range"). Same constraint blocks every item under section 6's "AI Assistant / AI Features" list (attendance prediction, risk analysis, question generation, etc.) — build this once a provider is chosen and wired up, not piecemeal per feature.
- [ ] **Full Teacher Workspace vision** ("Class teacher and Subject Teacher.docx", uploaded 2026-07-12) — the 3-column Teacher Workspace shell, Today's Schedule (needs Timetable, which doesn't exist), Lesson Notes, Assignments, Quizzes, Behaviour records, Fee-status widgets, a VP Academics report-card review stage, and a 360-degree AI-summarized student profile. Carved out of the teacher-scoping/nav/dashboard build (2026-07-12, see that entry's "Not built in this pass" note). Reference kept at memory `teacher_workspace_vision.md`. Needs Timetable and several other modules to exist first, plus the AI provider above for the summary piece — a genuinely large standalone build, not a quick add-on.
- [ ] **Full BPM / Workflow Engine** ("User role cycles.docx", uploaded 2026-07-12) — a generic, admin-configurable drag-and-drop workflow builder (arbitrary approval chains/state machines across any module, not just report cards). The Report Card review chain built 2026-07-12 deliberately solves the report-card-specific case only (an ordered list of role slugs in `config('skulos.report_card_review_roles')`/tenant Setting `report_card_review_chain`), not the general engine the doc envisions. Reference kept at memory `bpm_workflow_engine_vision.md`. Revisit if a second module needs the same configurable-chain shape, at which point extracting a shared engine starts paying for itself.

## 7. Business / Commercial Platform Features

- [ ] Subscription plans: Starter, Basic, Standard, Premium, Enterprise (tiered by student count / module access — actual pricing figures still TBD, doc marks them "illustrative only")
- [ ] Termly billing (aligned to 3-term academic calendar)
- [ ] Annual billing (= 3× termly rate, may carry promotional discount, configurable by Super Admin)
- [ ] Free trial: 1 academic term by default; Super Admin can configure trial duration, modules, student limits, storage limits, branding, and extensions
- [ ] School registration — both modes required:
  - [ ] Self-registration (with optional Super-Admin approval gate)
  - [ ] Manual registration by Platform Super Admin (enterprise onboarding path)
  - [ ] Global toggle to enable/disable self-registration
- [ ] White-label capability — architecture must support it, but **disabled at initial commercial launch**: custom domain, custom logo, colour themes, org-specific email templates, brand removal (future premium tier)
- [ ] Multi-school management under one owner account with a school-switcher (SSO, no re-login)
- [ ] Flexible/combinable pricing engine:
  - [ ] Student-count-based pricing
  - [ ] Module-based pricing
  - [ ] Storage-based pricing
  - [ ] Flat-rate plans
  - [ ] Enterprise custom agreements
- [ ] Storage add-on purchases, independent of subscription tier upgrades
- [ ] Platform-level billing & invoicing (distinct from a school's own Finance module)
- [ ] Revenue streams to support in billing engine:
  - [ ] Primary: termly/annual subscriptions
  - [ ] Secondary: additional modules, SMS credits, WhatsApp messaging, AI usage credits, storage upgrades, premium support, data migration services, staff training, implementation services, marketplace commissions, White Label licensing, school website hosting, mobile app access
  - [ ] Future: government contracts, CBT services for external institutions, online certification, digital content marketplace, API licensing, analytics subscriptions
- [ ] Commercial success metrics/reporting for Super Admin: registered schools, active subscriptions, ARR, retention rate, trial-to-paid conversion, ARPU, uptime, satisfaction scores, support response time, feature adoption, marketplace activity, API usage, mobile adoption (future)

## 13. Marketing Site & Admin CMS (new — started 2026-07-26)

Distinct from the product itself: the public-facing marketing site (landing page, About, Pricing, Contact) needs to be **content-managed**, not static HTML, because pricing figures, plan features, and company/contact info must be editable by the Platform Super Admin and reflect immediately on the live site. This means the marketing site cannot live on GitHub Pages alone (static hosting, no database) — it needs the real backend.

- [ ] Marketing site backend: Laravel-rendered (or served via API from Laravel + a lightweight frontend) so it can share auth/admin tooling with the rest of the platform, backed by MySQL
- [ ] Admin-editable **Site Settings**: business/legal name, contact email, phone/WhatsApp, physical address, social links — surfaced on About/Contact pages
- [ ] Admin-editable **Pricing Plans**: name, price per student per term, and a per-plan checklist of which features to display (checkbox-driven, not hardcoded per plan)
- [ ] Admin-editable **About** content
- [ ] Public pages (Home/landing, About, Pricing, Contact) read from the database and update automatically when the admin changes content — no redeploy needed for content changes
- [ ] Decide hosting split: Laravel admin + API on cPanel (per confirmed deployment target above); marketing frontend either server-rendered by Laravel directly, or a separate Next.js frontend deployable to Vercel that consumes the API — **pending a plan/approval before implementation starts**
- [x] Current static GitHub Pages site (`index.html`, `about.html`, `pricing.html`, `contact.html`, added 2026-07-08) is a stopgap only — treat as temporary until the CMS-backed version replaces it. Content on About/Pricing/Contact was authored directly (not placeholder/dummy text) per instruction, but is hardcoded HTML, not database-backed — it does not yet satisfy the "admin can edit, site auto-updates" requirement above. The Contact page form currently submits via a `mailto:` action (opens the visitor's email client) as an honest interim mechanism — no real form handler exists until the backend does.

## 14. SKULOS Pricing Plan & Feature Catalog (confirmed 2026-07-10, supersedes section 6/section 7 tier framing)

> Source: `SKULOS PRICING PLAN AND FEATURES.docx` (uploaded to repo root 2026-07-10). This is now **the** authoritative feature catalog per tier, replacing the looser lists in section 5/section 5b/section 6. It does not replace section 5/section 5b/section 6 as the module inventory, but it decides **which modules must be genuinely working, per tier, before that tier can be sold.**
>
> Tier rename (see Confirmed Business Decisions): **Foundation** (was Starter, ₦500/student/term), **Advance** (was Growth, ₦800/student/term), **Professional** (₦1,200/student/term), **Enterprise** (₦1,500/student/term).
>
> **Launch scope:** only Foundation and Advance need to be sellable at launch. Professional and Enterprise are documented in full below for completeness and marketing display (shown "Coming soon" on the public pricing page, not purchasable), but nothing under them blocks launch.
>
> Status tags used below: **BUILT** (working end to end in the product UI), **PARTIAL** (model/service exists but no usable screen, or a screen exists but a supporting piece is missing), **NOT BUILT** (nothing exists). ### What's actually left to build (updated 2026-07-19 status, in rough priority order)

Every Foundation and Advance item that can be built without reversing a standing scope decision is now done — see the Foundation/Advance checklists above, all checked, and the corresponding Build Log entries. Timetable Management, deferred as recently as earlier in this same window, was un-deferred by direct user request 2026-07-19 and is now built too. 1. **Admissions module** (Advance) — genuinely net new, the whole deferred module (5 features), the single largest remaining build in Foundation/Advance. Explicitly SUSPENDED 2026-07-18 by direct user command ("SUSPEND"), after initially being confirmed in scope. **Reversed again 2026-07-21**: user said "let's work on these" (picking Admissions from a list of pending items) then, once research had started (RBAC/TrialRequest/PDF/upload conventions read, no code written yet — see the aborted attempt below), "defere admisiion" (defer admission) moments later — the exact same "build it now" then reversed-within-minutes pattern already on record from 2026-07-18. Confirmed clean: only a single cosmetic code comment had been touched (config/skulos.php's `modules` catalog) and was reverted in the same turn; no migration, model, controller, or PlanSeeder change was ever made. Do not start again without the user reversing this a third time, and given the pattern, consider confirming intent explicitly before beginning actual implementation next time, not just before researching.
